<script>
/* ===================== SPACE Studio v0.1: engine =====================
   One model. Every screen reads M. Every edit → recompute() → fresh M.
   Units: mm internally, metres on screen (Singapore).                  */
const SQFT=0.092903; // m² per sq ft
const mm=v=>(window.SPACEU&&SPACEU.imp())?SPACEU.ftin(v):(v/1000).toFixed(2)+' m';
const m2=v=>(window.SPACEU&&SPACEU.imp())?SPACEU.sqft(v/1e6):(v/1e6).toFixed(1)+' m²';
const sgd=v=>'S$'+Math.round(v).toLocaleString();
const H_FULL=2600, H_HALF=1100, H_GLASS=2600, T_EXT=150, T_INT=100;

/* ---------- Singapore sample plans: rooms as rectangles (mm) + openings ---------- */

   {p:'Foyer',r:[0,0,1800,2200]},{p:'Dining',r:[0,2200,1800,4400]},{p:'Living',r:[1800,0,6400,4400]},

 opens:[{at:[900,0],w:900,k:'door'},{at:[1800,1100],w:900,k:'door'},{at:[1800,3300],w:1500,k:'opening'},{at:[6400,1500],w:900,k:'door'},

   {at:[4100,0],w:1800,k:'window'},{at:[1900,8400],w:1500,k:'window'},{at:[6800,8400],w:1500,k:'window'},{at:[9600,8400],w:1500,k:'window'},
   {at:[11000,6400],w:1200,k:'window'},{at:[10300,0],w:1200,k:'window'},{at:[0,6400],w:1500,k:'window'}]};

const scalePlan=(sx,sy)=>({rects:BASE.rects.map(r=>({p:r.p,r:[r.r[0]*sx,r.r[1]*sy,r.r[2]*sx,r.r[3]*sy].map(v=>Math.round(v/100)*100)})),

 condo3:{label:'Condominium 3-bed · 100 m²',kind:'Condo',...scalePlan(11500/11000,8800/8400)}
};

/* ---------- walls from rectangles: junction-to-junction, deduped ---------- */
function wallsFromRects(rects){
  const bx0=Math.min(...rects.map(r=>r.r[0])),bx1=Math.max(...rects.map(r=>r.r[2]));
  const by0=Math.min(...rects.map(r=>r.r[1])),by1=Math.max(...rects.map(r=>r.r[3]));
  const corners=rects.flatMap(r=>[[r.r[0],r.r[1]],[r.r[2],r.r[1]],[r.r[0],r.r[3]],[r.r[2],r.r[3]]]);
  const segs={};
  const add=(axis,fixed,a,b)=>{
    // split at any corner lying strictly inside this edge
    const cuts=corners.filter(c=>(axis==='x'?c[1]===fixed&&c[0]>a&&c[0]<b:c[0]===fixed&&c[1]>a&&c[1]<b)).map(c=>axis==='x'?c[0]:c[1]);
    const pts=[a,...new Set(cuts)].sort((p,q)=>p-q);pts.push(b);
    for(let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++){const k=axis+'|'+fixed+'|'+pts[i]+'|'+pts[i+1];
      const ext=axis==='x'?(fixed===by0||fixed===by1):(fixed===bx0||fixed===bx1);
      segs[k]={axis,fixed,a:pts[i],b:pts[i+1],t:ext?T_EXT:T_INT,ext};}
  };
  for(const r of rects){const [x0,y0,x1,y1]=r.r;add('x',y0,x0,x1);add('x',y1,x0,x1);add('y',x0,y0,y1);add('y',x1,y0,y1);}
  let n=0;
  return Object.values(segs).sort((p,q)=>p.axis===q.axis?(p.fixed-q.fixed||p.a-q.a):(p.axis<q.axis?-1:1))
    .map(s=>({id:'W'+(++n),...s,type:'full',state:'existing',opens:[]}));
}
function attachOpenings(walls,opens){
  let n=0;
  for(const o of opens){
    const w=walls.find(w=>w.axis==='x'?Math.abs(o.at[1]-w.fixed)<1&&o.at[0]>=w.a&&o.at[0]<=w.b
                                    :Math.abs(o.at[0]-w.fixed)<1&&o.at[1]>=w.a&&o.at[1]<=w.b);
    if(!w)continue;
    const c=w.axis==='x'?o.at[0]:o.at[1];
    const a=Math.max(w.a+100,c-o.w/2),b=Math.min(w.b-100,c+o.w/2);
    if(b-a<300)continue;
    w.opens.push({id:'O'+(++n),kind:o.k,a,b,sill:o.k==='window'?900:0,head:2100,state:'existing'});
  }
}
const wpts=w=>w.axis==='x'?[[w.a,w.fixed],[w.b,w.fixed]]:[[w.fixed,w.a],[w.fixed,w.b]];
const wlen=w=>w.b-w.a;
const wH=w=>w.type==='half'?H_HALF:(w.type==='glass'?H_GLASS:H_FULL);
const active=w=>w.state!=='demolished';

/* ---------- room detection: cell decomposition + flood fill (refuses open regions) ---------- */
function detectRooms(walls){
  const live=walls.filter(active);
  if(!live.length)return {rooms:[],open:[],bounds:null};
  const xs=[...new Set(live.flatMap(w=>wpts(w).map(p=>p[0])))].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  const ys=[...new Set(live.flatMap(w=>wpts(w).map(p=>p[1])))].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  const bx0=xs[0],bx1=xs[xs.length-1],by0=ys[0],by1=ys[ys.length-1];
  const blockedV=(x,y0,y1)=>live.some(w=>w.axis==='y'&&w.fixed===x&&w.a<=y0+1&&w.b>=y1-1);
  const blockedH=(y,x0,x1)=>live.some(w=>w.axis==='x'&&w.fixed===y&&w.a<=x0+1&&w.b>=x1-1);
  const NX=xs.length-1,NY=ys.length-1;
  const lab=Array.from({length:NX},()=>Array(NY).fill(-1));
  const regions=[];
  for(let i=0;i<NX;i++)for(let j=0;j<NY;j++){
    if(lab[i][j]>=0)continue;
    const id=regions.length;const cells=[];const st=[[i,j]];lab[i][j]=id;let open=false;
    while(st.length){const [a,b]=st.pop();cells.push([a,b]);
      const x0=xs[a],x1=xs[a+1],y0=ys[b],y1=ys[b+1];
      // west
      if(!blockedV(x0,y0,y1)){if(a===0)open=true;else if(lab[a-1][b]<0){lab[a-1][b]=id;st.push([a-1,b]);}}
      if(!blockedV(x1,y0,y1)){if(a===NX-1)open=true;else if(lab[a+1][b]<0){lab[a+1][b]=id;st.push([a+1,b]);}}
      if(!blockedH(y0,x0,x1)){if(b===0)open=true;else if(lab[a][b-1]<0){lab[a][b-1]=id;st.push([a,b-1]);}}
      if(!blockedH(y1,x0,x1)){if(b===NY-1)open=true;else if(lab[a][b+1]<0){lab[a][b+1]=id;st.push([a,b+1]);}}
    }
    const rc=cells.map(([a,b])=>({x0:xs[a],y0:ys[b],x1:xs[a+1],y1:ys[b+1]}));
    const area=rc.reduce((s,c)=>s+(c.x1-c.x0)*(c.y1-c.y0),0);
    let cx=0,cy=0;for(const c of rc){const A=(c.x1-c.x0)*(c.y1-c.y0);cx+=A*(c.x0+c.x1)/2;cy+=A*(c.y0+c.y1)/2;}
    regions.push({cells:rc,area,cx:cx/area,cy:cy/area,open,
      bbox:{x0:Math.min(...rc.map(c=>c.x0)),x1:Math.max(...rc.map(c=>c.x1)),y0:Math.min(...rc.map(c=>c.y0)),y1:Math.max(...rc.map(c=>c.y1))}});
  }
  const rooms=regions.filter(r=>!r.open&&r.area>0).sort((p,q)=>q.area-p.area).map((r,i)=>({id:'R'+(i+1),...r}));
  const open=regions.filter(r=>r.open);
  return {rooms,open,bounds:{x0:bx0,x1:bx1,y0:by0,y1:by1}};
}
const inRoom=(r,x,y)=>r.cells.some(c=>x>=c.x0&&x<=c.x1&&y>=c.y0&&y<=c.y1);
const WET=new Set(['Kitchen','Master Bath','Common Bath','Service Yard']);
const PROGRAMS=['Foyer','Living','Dining','Hallway','Kitchen','Master Bedroom','Bedroom 2','Bedroom 3','Study','Master Bath','Common Bath','Service Yard','Store / Shelter','Unassigned'];
const PTINT={Foyer:'#EFEAE4',Hallway:'#F1EEEA',Living:'#F3EDE5',Dining:'#F3EDE5',Kitchen:'#E9ECEA','Master Bedroom':'#F0E9E6','Bedroom 2':'#F0E9E6','Bedroom 3':'#F0E9E6',Study:'#ECEBE7','Master Bath':'#E6ECEE','Common Bath':'#E6ECEE','Service Yard':'#E9ECEA','Store / Shelter':'#ECEBE7',Unassigned:'#F6F3F0'};
function suggestProgram(r,rooms){
  const A=r.area/1e6; if(r===rooms[0])return 'Living';
  if(A<3.5)return 'Store / Shelter'; if(A<6)return 'Common Bath'; if(A<9)return 'Study'; if(A<13)return 'Bedroom 2'; if(A<17)return 'Master Bedroom'; return 'Kitchen';
}

function setPaint(name){const hex=palHex(name);if(!hex)return;const tgt=STATE.paintTarget||'wall';snap();const P=STATE.paint=STATE.paint||{};const before=P[tgt+'Name']||'theme';
  P[tgt]=hex;P[tgt+'Name']=name;P.styleId=null;P.style=null;const row=SLIB.PAL.find(x=>x['SPACE colour']===name);
  logIt('Paint',{wall:'walls',accent:'accent',furn:'joinery'}[tgt],before,name+' '+hex,'SPACE palette · LRV '+(row?row.LRV:''));recompute('Paint: '+name);}
function resetPaint(){snap();STATE.paint=null;logIt('Paint','all','library paint','theme','');recompute('Paint back to the theme');}
function applyStyle(id){const s=((SLIB&&SLIB.SCH&&SLIB.SCH.styles)||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);if(!s||!STATE.M)return;snap();

  logIt('House style',s.name,'-','walls '+s.wall+' · accent '+s.accent+' · joinery '+s.joinery,n+' room floors from the SPACE library');recompute('House style: '+s.name);}
function libRow(r){const F=SLIB&&SLIB.FUR,C=SLIB&&SLIB.CRP;if(!F&&!C)return '';const grp={};

  return `<div class="row" style="margin-top:8px;margin-bottom:0;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap"><span style="font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--copper);min-width:150px">SPACE library</span>
   <select id="lib-f" style="max-width:360px">${Object.entries(grp).map(([g,a])=>`<optgroup label="${g}">${a.map(([v,t])=>`<option value="${v}">${t}</option>`).join('')}</optgroup>`).join('')}</select>

function addLib(rid){const r=STATE.M.rooms.find(r=>r.id===rid);const v=(document.getElementById('lib-f')||{}).value||'';if(!r||!v)return;

function complianceFlags(){const C=(SLIB&&SLIB.COMP)||[];if(!C.length||!STATE.M)return null;const M=STATE.M,hdb=STATE.kind==='HDB';const out=[];
  const add=(t,why)=>{const r=C.find(x=>x.topic===t);if(r&&!out.some(o=>o.r===r))out.push({r,why});};
  const dem=STATE.walls.filter(w=>w.state==='demolished'),neu=STATE.walls.filter(w=>w.state==='new');
  const opensChanged=STATE.walls.some(w=>w.opens.some(o=>o.state==='new'||o.state==='removed'));
  const wet=M.rooms.filter(r=>WET.has(r.program)&&r.floor!=='keep');
  const shelter=M.rooms.find(r=>r.program==='Store / Shelter');
  if(hdb){add('Permit principle','HDB flat: works that need approval are listed below');
    if(dem.length){add('All wall demolition needs approval',dem.length+' wall'+(dem.length>1?'s':'')+' marked for demolition: '+dem.map(w=>w.id).join(', '));
      add('Professional Engineer supervision','applies if any demolished element turns out to be reinforced concrete');
      add('Identifying a reinforced concrete wall','HDB publishes no method: confirm each wall with HDB for this flat');
      add('What can never be hacked','structural elements stay, whatever the design');add('Hacking duration','hacking is in scope');}
    if(neu.length)add('Works not needing a permit',neu.length+' new wall'+(neu.length>1?'s':'')+': a 63 mm hollow block, 80 mm glass block or gypsum partition needs no permit');
    const why=[dem.length?'wall demolition':'',opensChanged?'door openings moved or sealed':'',wet.length?'wet area floor finishes replaced':''].filter(Boolean).join(', ');
    if(why)add('Works needing a permit',why);
    if(wet.length){add('Floor finish thickness',wet.map(r=>r.program).join(', '));add('Loading from finishes','floor build-ups: the 120 kg per square metre limit is the one that binds');
      if(wet.some(r=>/Bath/.test(r.program))){add('Bathroom waterproofing','bathroom finishes replaced');add('The three year rule, correctly stated','bathroom finishes, counted from block completion');}}
    add('Directory of Renovation Contractors','every HDB renovation, permit or not');add('General renovation, weekdays and Saturday','the programme assumes permitted hours');
    if(dem.length)add('Noisy work','hacking and drilling windows');
    if(M.rooms.some(r=>r.program==='Service Yard'))add('Service yard','service yard in the plan');}
  if(shelter&&(shelter.items.length||shelter.floor!=='keep')){add('Drilling','items or finishes planned in the household shelter');add('Floor finishes','shelter floor');add('The door','household shelter door');add('Door opening','the 90 degree swing stays clear');add('Concealing the door behind carpentry','not addressed by SCDF: confirm first');}
  else if(shelter)add('Why the rules exist','household shelter in the plan');
  add('Home Fire Alarm Device','required in every home');
  if(STATE.carp.length||M.builtins)add('What needs a licensed worker','built-in lighting and power points');
  if(M.rooms.some(r=>r.program==='Kitchen'))add('Who may work on gas','kitchen in scope');
  return out;}

/* ---------- furniture as real objects: plan symbols (2D) and parts (3D) ----------
   local frame: origin at item centre, x along width, y along depth; y<0 = the wall / head side */
const CAT={bed:['bedk','bedq','bedss','beds'],sofa:['sofa','sofa2'],table:['coffee','side','dining6','dining4','desk'],cab:['bedside','dresser','shoe','vanity','fridge','washer','ward','ward24','shelf','custom']};
const catOf=k=>Object.keys(CAT).find(c=>CAT[c].includes(k))||k;
function mix(hex,to,f){const a=parseInt(hex.slice(1),16),b=parseInt(to.slice(1),16);const ch=sh=>Math.round(((a>>sh)&255)*(1-f)+((b>>sh)&255)*f);return 'rgb('+ch(16)+','+ch(8)+','+ch(0)+')';}
function palette(it,T){const bi=isBI(it);const base=it.fill||(T?(bi?T.accent:T.furn):(bi?'#C9A98C':'#E3DCD3'));
  return {frame:base,top:T?mix(T.accent,'#ffffff',.15):'#C9AC8C',cushion:mix(base,'#ffffff',.35),mattress:T?mix(T.wall,'#ffffff',.3):'#F2EEE9',pillow:'#FBFAF7',dark:T?T.dark:'#3B3C3A',metal:'#B9BDBF',glass:'#DDE8EC'};}
function symbol(it){ // returns svg fragments in local mm coords
  const {w,d,h}=dims(it),k=it.kind,c=catOf(k),o=[];const R=(x,y,ww,hh,fill,rx,extra)=>o.push(`<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${ww}" height="${hh}" rx="${rx||0}" fill="${fill}" ${extra||''}/>`);
  const L=(x1,y1,x2,y2,extra)=>o.push(`<line x1="${x1}" y1="${y1}" x2="${x2}" y2="${y2}" ${extra||''}/>`);
  const x0=-w/2,y0=-d/2;
  if(c==='bed'){R(x0,y0,w,d,'F',30);R(x0+40,y0+60,w-80,d-100,'M',40);const n=w>1200?2:1,pw=(w-120-(n-1)*60)/n;
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++)R(x0+60+i*(pw+60),y0+110,pw,340,'P',60);L(x0+40,y0+560,x0+w-40,y0+560,'stroke="S" stroke-opacity=".5"');
    R(x0+40,y0+560,w-80,d-600,'C',30,'opacity=".55"');}
  else if(c==='sofa'){R(x0,y0,w,d,'F',70);R(x0+40,y0+40,w-80,180,'C',40);R(x0+40,y0+40,150,d-80,'C',40);R(x0+w-190,y0+40,150,d-80,'C',40);
    const n=w>1900?3:2,cw=(w-380-(n-1)*30)/n;for(let i=0;i<n;i++)R(x0+190+i*(cw+30),y0+240,cw,d-300,'K',40);}
  else if(k==='sofaL'){R(x0,y0,w,900,'F',70);R(x0+w-900,y0,900,d,'F',70);R(x0+40,y0+40,w-80,180,'C',40);R(x0+w-220,y0+40,180,d-80,'C',40);
    const n=Math.round((w-900)/700);const cw=(w-980-(n-1)*30)/n;for(let i=0;i<n;i++)R(x0+40+i*(cw+30),y0+240,cw,600,'K',40);R(x0+w-860,y0+900,600,d-940,'K',40);}
  else if(c==='table'){const rx=k.startsWith('dining')?40:20;R(x0,y0,w,d,'T',rx);
    if(k.startsWith('dining')){const nl=k==='dining6'?2:2;for(let i=0;i<nl;i++){const cx=x0+(i+0.5)*w/nl-225;R(cx,y0-520,450,450,'F',60);L(cx+30,y0-500,cx+420,y0-500,'stroke="D" stroke-width="40"');R(cx,y0+d+70,450,450,'F',60);L(cx+30,y0+d+500,cx+420,y0+d+500,'stroke="D" stroke-width="40"');}
      if(k==='dining6'){R(x0-520,-225,450,450,'F',60);L(x0-500,-195,x0-500,195,'stroke="D" stroke-width="40"');R(x0+w+70,-225,450,450,'F',60);L(x0+w+500,-195,x0+w+500,195,'stroke="D" stroke-width="40"');}}
    if(k==='desk'){R(-225,y0+d+80,450,450,'F',60);L(-195,y0+d+500,195,y0+d+500,'stroke="D" stroke-width="40"');L(x0+40,y0+d-30,x0+w-40,y0+d-30,'stroke="S" stroke-opacity=".4"');}}
  else if(k==='kcab'){R(x0,y0,w,d,'F',10);L(x0,y0+d-20,x0+w,y0+d-20,'stroke="D" stroke-width="20"');
    R(x0+w*0.12,y0+100,520,d-200,'S',60,'fill-opacity=".3" stroke="D" stroke-width="12"');o.push(`<circle cx="${x0+w*0.12+260}" cy="${y0+80}" r="30" fill="D"/>`);
    const hx=x0+w*0.62;for(const [dx,dy,r] of [[110,150,90],[330,150,90],[110,d-150,110],[330,d-150,110]])o.push(`<circle cx="${hx+dx}" cy="${y0+dy}" r="${r}" fill="none" stroke="D" stroke-width="16"/>`);
    for(let x=x0+600;x<x0+w-100;x+=600)L(x,y0,x,y0+d,'stroke="S" stroke-opacity=".5"');}
  else if(k==='island'){R(x0,y0,w,d,'T',20);for(const [dx,dy] of [[-260,-120],[-60,-120],[-260,120],[-60,120]])o.push(`<circle cx="${dx}" cy="${dy}" r="95" fill="none" stroke="D" stroke-width="16"/>`);R(x0+w-700,y0+150,520,d-300,'S',60,'fill-opacity=".3" stroke="D" stroke-width="12"');}
  else if(k==='tv'){R(x0,y0,w,60,'D',0);R(x0+w*0.2,y0+60,w*0.6,d-60,'F',10);L(x0+w*0.3,y0+90,x0+w*0.7,y0+90,'stroke="D" stroke-width="30"');}
  else if(c==='cab'){R(x0,y0,w,d,'F',10);
    if(k==='vanity'){o.push(`<ellipse cx="0" cy="0" rx="${Math.min(260,w*0.3)}" ry="${Math.min(180,d*0.3)}" fill="S" fill-opacity=".35" stroke="D" stroke-width="12"/>`);}
    else if(k==='washer'){o.push(`<circle cx="0" cy="0" r="${Math.min(w,d)*0.32}" fill="none" stroke="D" stroke-width="16"/><circle cx="0" cy="0" r="${Math.min(w,d)*0.12}" fill="D"/>`);}
    else if(k==='fridge'){L(x0+w/2,y0,x0+w/2,y0+d,'stroke="D" stroke-width="14"');L(x0+w/2-40,y0+60,x0+w/2-40,y0+d-60,'stroke="D" stroke-width="30"');}
    else if(k==='shelf'){for(let x=x0+300;x<x0+w-50;x+=300)L(x,y0,x,y0+d,'stroke="S"');}
    else if(k==='ward'||k==='ward24'||k==='shoe'||k==='dresser'){const n=Math.max(1,Math.round(w/500));for(let i=1;i<n;i++)L(x0+i*w/n,y0,x0+i*w/n,y0+d,'stroke="S"');for(let i=0;i<n;i++)L(x0+(i+.5)*w/n-60,y0+d-90,x0+(i+.5)*w/n+60,y0+d-90,'stroke="D" stroke-width="30"');}
    else if(k==='custom'&&it.carp){const n=Math.max(1,Math.round(w/600));for(let i=1;i<n;i++)L(x0+i*w/n,y0,x0+i*w/n,y0+d,'stroke="S"');L(x0,y0+d-20,x0+w,y0+d-20,'stroke="D" stroke-width="20"');}
    else if(k==='custom'){L(x0,y0,x0+w,y0+d,'stroke="S" stroke-opacity=".5"');L(x0+w,y0,x0,y0+d,'stroke="S" stroke-opacity=".5"');}}
  else R(x0,y0,w,d,'F',10);
  return o.join('');
}
function symbolSVG(it,X,Y,sc,T,extra){const P=palette(it,T);
  const frag=symbol(it).replace(/"F"/g,`"${P.frame}"`).replace(/"M"/g,`"${P.mattress}"`).replace(/"P"/g,`"${P.pillow}"`).replace(/"C"/g,`"${P.cushion}"`)
    .replace(/"K"/g,`"${mix(P.cushion,'#000000',.06)}"`).replace(/"T"/g,`"${P.top}"`).replace(/"D"/g,`"${P.dark}"`).replace(/"S"/g,`"${P.metal}"`);
  return `<g transform="translate(${X(it.x)} ${Y(it.y)}) scale(${sc}) rotate(${it.rot?90:0})" stroke="${P.dark}" stroke-width="${18}" stroke-opacity=".55" ${extra||''}>${frag}</g>`;}
function parts(it){ // local boxes {x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1,role}
  const {w,d,h}=dims(it),k=it.kind,c=catOf(k),o=[];const B=(x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1,role)=>o.push({x0,y0,z0,x1,y1,z1,role});
  const X0=-w/2,Y0=-d/2;
  if(c==='bed'){B(X0,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+d,280,'frame');B(X0,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+60,900,'frame');B(X0+40,Y0+60,280,X0+w-40,Y0+d-40,500,'mattress');
    const n=w>1200?2:1,pw=(w-120-(n-1)*60)/n;for(let i=0;i<n;i++)B(X0+60+i*(pw+60),Y0+110,500,X0+60+i*(pw+60)+pw,Y0+450,600,'pillow');B(X0+30,Y0+560,500,X0+w-30,Y0+d-40,560,'cushion');}
  else if(c==='sofa'){B(X0,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+d,430,'frame');B(X0,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+220,h,'frame');B(X0,Y0,0,X0+180,Y0+d,650,'frame');B(X0+w-180,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+d,650,'frame');
    const n=w>1900?3:2,cw=(w-380-(n-1)*30)/n;for(let i=0;i<n;i++)B(X0+190+i*(cw+30),Y0+230,430,X0+190+i*(cw+30)+cw,Y0+d-30,540,'cushion');}
  else if(k==='sofaL'){B(X0,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+900,430,'frame');B(X0+w-900,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+d,430,'frame');B(X0,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+220,h,'frame');B(X0+w-220,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+d,h,'frame');
    B(X0+40,Y0+230,430,X0+w-240,Y0+860,540,'cushion');B(X0+w-860,Y0+900,430,X0+w-240,Y0+d-40,540,'cushion');}
  else if(c==='table'){B(X0,Y0,h-40,X0+w,Y0+d,h,'top');
    if(k==='desk'){B(X0,Y0,0,X0+30,Y0+d,h-40,'frame');B(X0+w-30,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+d,h-40,'frame');B(-225,Y0+d+80,0,225,Y0+d+530,450,'cushion');B(-225,Y0+d+480,450,225,Y0+d+530,900,'frame');}
    else for(const [dx,dy] of [[60,60],[w-120,60],[60,d-120],[w-120,d-120]])B(X0+dx,Y0+dy,0,X0+dx+60,Y0+dy+60,h-40,'dark');
    if(k.startsWith('dining')){const chair=(cx,cy,bx0,by0,bx1,by1)=>{B(cx-225,cy-225,0,cx+225,cy+225,450,'cushion');B(bx0,by0,450,bx1,by1,900,'frame');for(const [dx,dy] of [[-200,-200],[160,-200],[-200,160],[160,160]])B(cx+dx,cy+dy,0,cx+dx+40,cy+dy+40,400,'dark');};
      for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const cx=X0+(i+0.5)*w/2;chair(cx,Y0-300,cx-225,Y0-525,cx+225,Y0-480);chair(cx,Y0+d+300,cx-225,Y0+d+480,cx+225,Y0+d+525);}
      if(k==='dining6'){chair(X0-300,0,X0-525,-225,X0-480,225);chair(X0+w+300,0,X0+w+480,-225,X0+w+525,225);}}}
  else if(k==='kcab'){B(X0,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+d,900,'frame');B(X0-10,Y0-10,900,X0+w+10,Y0+d+20,940,'top');B(X0,Y0,1500,X0+w,Y0+350,2200,'frame');B(X0,Y0,940,X0+w,Y0+20,1500,'mattress');}
  else if(k==='island'){B(X0,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+d,900,'frame');B(X0-20,Y0-20,900,X0+w+20,Y0+d+20,940,'top');}
  else if(k==='tv'){B(X0,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+50,h,'frame');B(X0+w*0.2,Y0+50,0,X0+w*0.8,Y0+d,480,'frame');B(X0+w*0.3,Y0+50,850,X0+w*0.7,Y0+80,1550,'dark');}
  else if(c==='cab'){B(X0,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+d,h,'frame');if(k==='vanity'||k==='dresser'||k==='bedside'||k==='shoe')B(X0-10,Y0-10,h,X0+w+10,Y0+d+10,h+30,'top');if(k==='fridge')B(X0+w/2-10,Y0+d,0,X0+w/2+10,Y0+d+20,h,'dark');}
  else B(X0,Y0,0,X0+w,Y0+d,h,'frame');
  return o;
}
function worldParts(it){return parts(it).map(b=>{const r=it.rot;const x0=r?-b.y1:b.x0,x1=r?-b.y0:b.x1,y0=r?b.x0:b.y0,y1=r?b.x1:b.y1;return {lo:[it.x+x0,it.y+y0,b.z0],hi:[it.x+x1,it.y+y1,b.z1],role:b.role};});}

/* ---------- placement + space-available analysis ---------- */
function roomSides(r){
  const B=r.bbox,sides={N:{door:false},S:{door:false},W:{door:false},E:{door:false}};
  for(const wid of r.walls){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===wid);if(!w)continue;
    const side=w.axis==='x'?(w.fixed===B.y0?'N':(w.fixed===B.y1?'S':null)):(w.fixed===B.x0?'W':(w.fixed===B.x1?'E':null));
    if(!side)continue;sides[side].wall=true;
    if(w.opens.some(o=>o.kind==='door'&&o.state!=='removed'))sides[side].door=true;}
  return sides;
}
function clearLen(w){const ends=[w.a,w.b].map(e=>{const p=w.axis==='x'?[e,w.fixed]:[w.fixed,e];const t=STATE.walls.filter(active).find(x=>x.id!==w.id&&x.axis!==w.axis&&Math.abs(x.fixed-(w.axis==='x'?p[0]:p[1]))<1&&x.a<=(w.axis==='x'?p[1]:p[0])+1&&x.b>=(w.axis==='x'?p[1]:p[0])-1);return t?t.t/2:50;});return Math.round((wlen(w)-ends[0]-ends[1])/10)*10;}
function placeAgainst(r,side,f,al,g){
  const B=r.bbox;g=g||90; // wall half-thickness + skirting; carpentry passes a flush value
  const horiz=side==='N'||side==='S';
  const rot=horiz?0:1;const w=rot?f.d:f.w,d=rot?f.w:f.d;
  let x,y;
  if(side==='N'){y=B.y0+g+d/2;x=B.x0+al*(B.x1-B.x0);}
  else if(side==='S'){y=B.y1-g-d/2;x=B.x0+al*(B.x1-B.x0);}
  else if(side==='W'){x=B.x0+g+w/2;y=B.y0+al*(B.y1-B.y0);}
  else{x=B.x1-g-w/2;y=B.y0+al*(B.y1-B.y0);}
  x=Math.min(B.x1-g-w/2,Math.max(B.x0+g+w/2,x));y=Math.min(B.y1-g-d/2,Math.max(B.y0+g+d/2,y));
  return {x:Math.round(x/50)*50,y:Math.round(y/50)*50,rot};
}
function rectsOverlap(a,b,tol){tol=tol||0;return a.x0<b.x1-tol&&a.x1>b.x0+tol&&a.y0<b.y1-tol&&a.y1>b.y0+tol;}
function overlapArea(a,b){const w=Math.min(a.x1,b.x1)-Math.max(a.x0,b.x0),h=Math.min(a.y1,b.y1)-Math.max(a.y0,b.y0);return w>0&&h>0?w*h:0;}
function clearanceBox(it,r){
  const f=FURN[it.kind],b=itemBox(it),c=f.clr||0,g=140;
  // wall-backed pieces need no clearance on the side that touches a wall
  const B=r?r.bbox:null;
  const nN=B&&b.y0-B.y0<g,nS=B&&B.y1-b.y1<g,nW=B&&b.x0-B.x0<g,nE=B&&B.x1-b.x1<g;
  return {x0:b.x0-(nW?0:c),y0:b.y0-(nN?0:c),x1:b.x1+(nE?0:c),y1:b.y1+(nS?0:c)};
}
function doorSwings(r){const out=[];
  for(const wid of r.walls){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===wid);if(!w)continue;
    for(const o of w.opens){if(o.kind!=='door'||o.state==='removed')continue;const sr=swingRect(w,o);
      if(inRoom(r,(sr.x0+sr.x1)/2,(sr.y0+sr.y1)/2))out.push(sr);}}
  return out;}
function analyseRoom(r){
  const items=r.items,foot=items.map(itemBox);
  const inside=foot.reduce((s,b)=>s+r.cells.reduce((t,c)=>t+overlapArea(b,c),0),0);
  const free=Math.max(0,r.area-inside),freePct=r.area?free/r.area:1;
  const issues=[];const swings=doorSwings(r);
  items.forEach((it,i)=>{const f={...FURN[it.kind],n:nameOf(it)},b=foot[i];
    const insideArea=r.cells.reduce((t,c)=>t+overlapArea(b,c),0),fa=(b.x1-b.x0)*(b.y1-b.y0);
    if(insideArea<fa*0.97)issues.push({id:it.id,sev:'bad',msg:f.n+' crosses a wall / leaves the room'});
    items.forEach((jt,j)=>{if(j<=i)return;if(rectsOverlap(b,foot[j],20))issues.push({id:it.id,sev:'bad',msg:f.n+' overlaps '+nameOf(jt)});});
    const cb=clearanceBox(it,r);
    items.forEach((jt,j)=>{if(j===i)return;if((MATES[it.kind]||[]).includes(jt.kind))return;if(!rectsOverlap(b,foot[j],20)&&rectsOverlap(cb,foot[j]))issues.push({id:it.id,sev:'warn',msg:nameOf(jt)+' sits inside the '+f.clr+' mm clearance of '+f.n});});
    for(const sw of swings)if(rectsOverlap(b,sw))issues.push({id:it.id,sev:'warn',msg:f.n+' blocks the door swing ('+sw.id+')'});
  });
  const bad=issues.filter(i=>i.sev==='bad').length,warn=issues.filter(i=>i.sev==='warn').length;
  const verdict=bad?'does not fit':(warn?'tight: check clearances':(freePct>=0.55?'comfortable':(freePct>=0.4?'tight':'cramped')));
  return {free,freePct,issues,verdict,ok:!bad&&!warn&&freePct>=0.4,swings};
}


/* ---------- door logic: swing direction, hinge side, walkthrough ---------- */
const CIRC=new Set(['Living','Dining','Hallway','Foyer']);
function roomAt(rooms,x,y){return rooms.find(r=>inRoom(r,x,y))||null;}
function sidesOfOpening(rooms,w,o){const c=(o.a+o.b)/2,off=w.t/2+80;
  const P=w.axis==='x'?[c,w.fixed+off]:[w.fixed+off,c],Mn=w.axis==='x'?[c,w.fixed-off]:[w.fixed-off,c];
  return {plus:roomAt(rooms,P[0],P[1]),minus:roomAt(rooms,Mn[0],Mn[1])};}
function orientDoor(rooms,w,o){
  const {plus,minus}=sidesOfOpening(rooms,w,o);
  let into;
  if(!plus&&!minus)into=1;else if(!plus)into=-1;else if(!minus)into=1;
  else{const cp=CIRC.has(plus.program),cm=CIRC.has(minus.program);
    if(cp!==cm)into=cp?-1:1;                       // from circulation into the room it serves
    else if(cp&&cm)into=plus.area>=minus.area?1:-1;   // between two circulation spaces: swing into the larger
    else into=plus.area<=minus.area?1:-1;}            // room to room (bedroom → ensuite): into the smaller
  const hinge=(o.a-w.a)<=(w.b-o.b)?'a':'b';
  return {into,hinge};}
function orientAllDoors(rooms,force){for(const w of STATE.walls)for(const o of w.opens){if(o.kind!=='door')continue;if(o.lock&&!force)continue;const g=orientDoor(rooms,w,o);o.into=g.into;o.hinge=g.hinge;}}
function swingRect(w,o){const L=o.b-o.a,into=o.into||1;
  return w.axis==='x'?{x0:o.a,x1:o.b,y0:into>0?w.fixed:w.fixed-L,y1:into>0?w.fixed+L:w.fixed,id:o.id}
                     :{y0:o.a,y1:o.b,x0:into>0?w.fixed:w.fixed-L,x1:into>0?w.fixed+L:w.fixed,id:o.id};}
function walkthrough(rooms){
  const issues=[],edges=[];const doors=[];
  for(const w of STATE.walls.filter(active))for(const o of w.opens){if(o.state==='removed'||o.kind==='window')continue;
    const {plus,minus}=sidesOfOpening(rooms,w,o);const A=plus?plus.id:'OUT',B=minus?minus.id:'OUT';edges.push([A,B]);
    const wd=o.b-o.a;doors.push({w,o,plus,minus,wd});
    if(o.kind==='door'){
      if(wd<700)issues.push({sev:'warn',msg:o.id+': '+Math.round(wd)+' mm leaf is narrower than a standard 700 mm',id:o.id});
      if(wd>1000)issues.push({sev:'warn',msg:o.id+': '+Math.round(wd)+' mm is wider than a single leaf; make it a cased opening or double leaf',id:o.id});
      if(!plus&&!minus)issues.push({sev:'bad',msg:o.id+' opens between two open regions',id:o.id});}}
  // swing collisions: two door swings in the same room overlapping
  for(let i=0;i<doors.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<doors.length;j++){const a=doors[i],b=doors[j];if(a.o.kind!=='door'||b.o.kind!=='door')continue;
    if(rectsOverlap(swingRect(a.w,a.o),swingRect(b.w,b.o)))issues.push({sev:'warn',msg:a.o.id+' and '+b.o.id+' swing into each other',id:a.o.id});}
  // reachability from outside (the main door)
  const adj={};for(const [A,B] of edges){(adj[A]=adj[A]||new Set()).add(B);(adj[B]=adj[B]||new Set()).add(A);}
  const seen=new Set(['OUT']),st=['OUT'];while(st.length){const n=st.pop();for(const m of (adj[n]||[]))if(!seen.has(m)){seen.add(m);st.push(m);}}
  const hasEntry=edges.some(e=>e.includes('OUT'));
  if(!hasEntry)issues.push({sev:'bad',msg:'No entrance door on an exterior wall',id:null});
  for(const r of rooms){if(!seen.has(r.id)){const anyDoor=edges.some(e=>e.includes(r.id));
    issues.push({sev:'bad',msg:r.program+' ('+r.id+') cannot be reached'+(anyDoor?' from the entrance':': it has no door'),id:r.id});}}
  return {reach:seen,issues,edges,doors:doors.length};}
function autoFixDoors(){snap();let n=0;
  for(const w of STATE.walls)for(const o of w.opens){if(o.kind==='door'&&(o.b-o.a)>1100){o.kind='opening';n++;logIt('Door → opening',w.id+' · '+o.id,Math.round(o.b-o.a)+' mm door','cased opening','wider than a single leaf');}o.lock=false;}
  orientAllDoors(STATE.M.rooms,true);
  logIt('Door logic',(STATE.M.walk?STATE.M.walk.doors:0)+' doors','as drawn','swings re-oriented','doors swing into the room they serve, hinge on the corner side');
  recompute('Door logic applied: '+n+' wide door(s) became openings');}
function setOpenWidth(wid,oid,v){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===wid);const o=w&&w.opens.find(x=>x.id===oid);if(!o)return;snap();const c=(o.a+o.b)/2,half=+v/2;
  const b0=Math.round(o.b-o.a);o.a=Math.max(w.a+100,c-half);o.b=Math.min(w.b-100,c+half);logIt('Resize '+o.kind,wid+' · '+oid,b0+' mm',Math.round(o.b-o.a)+' mm','');recompute();}
function setOpenKind(wid,oid,k){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===wid);const o=w&&w.opens.find(x=>x.id===oid);if(!o||o.kind===k)return;snap();const b=o.kind;o.kind=k;o.sill=k==='window'?900:0;o.lock=false;logIt('Change opening',wid+' · '+oid,b,k,'');recompute();}
function flipDoor(wid,oid,what){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===wid);const o=w&&w.opens.find(x=>x.id===oid);if(!o)return;snap();
  if(what==='swing')o.into=-(o.into||1);else o.hinge=o.hinge==='a'?'b':'a';o.lock=true;logIt('Door '+what,wid+' · '+oid,'auto','flipped ('+(what==='swing'?(o.into>0?'+':'−'):o.hinge)+')','manual override');recompute();}

/* ===================== STATE ===================== */
let STATE={file:null,kind:'HDB',walls:[],progAt:[],floorAt:[],items:[],log:[],carp:[],theme:'japandi',user:null,project:null,
  M:null,rev:0,cur:0,maxSeen:0,locked:false,sub:'walls',view:'changes',tool:'select',selWall:null,selItem:null,addPt:null,selRoom:null,ab:{}};
function nextWallId(){let n=0;for(const w of STATE.walls){const k=+w.id.slice(1);if(k>n)n=k;}return 'W'+(n+1);}
function nextOpenId(){let n=0;for(const w of STATE.walls)for(const o of w.opens){const k=+o.id.slice(1);if(k>n)n=k;}return 'O'+(n+1);}
function logIt(action,target,before,after,detail){
  STATE.log.push({n:STATE.log.length+1,ts:new Date(),action,target,before,after,detail:detail||''});
}
function recompute(msg){
  if(!STATE.walls.length){STATE.M=null;document.getElementById('rev').textContent='no plan loaded';render();return;}
  const R=detectRooms(STATE.walls);
  const rooms=R.rooms.map(r=>{
    const pa=STATE.progAt.filter(p=>inRoom(r,p.x,p.y)).sort((a,b)=>b.area-a.area)[0];
    const fa=STATE.floorAt.filter(p=>inRoom(r,p.x,p.y))[0];
    const program=pa?pa.program:suggestProgram(r,R.rooms);
    const items=STATE.items.filter(it=>inRoom(r,it.x,it.y));
    const walls=STATE.walls.filter(active).filter(w=>r.cells.some(c=>w.axis==='x'?(w.fixed===c.y0||w.fixed===c.y1)&&w.a<c.x1&&w.b>c.x0:(w.fixed===c.x0||w.fixed===c.x1)&&w.a<c.y1&&w.b>c.y0)).map(w=>w.id);
    const wins=STATE.walls.filter(w=>walls.includes(w.id)).flatMap(w=>w.opens.filter(o=>o.kind==='window'&&o.state!=='removed'));
    const floorKey=fa?fa.floor:(WET.has(program)?'homo':'spc');
    const fl=FLOORS[floorKey];const sqft=r.area/1e6/SQFT;
    const room={...r,program,suggested:!pa||!pa.conf,items,walls,windows:wins.length,floor:floorKey,floorCost:sqft*fl.psf,sqft,
      floorWarn:WET.has(program)&&!fl.wet};
    return room;
  });
  orientAllDoors(rooms,false);                 // needs programs: doors swing into the room they serve
  for(const room of rooms)room.space=analyseRoom(room);
  const live=STATE.walls.filter(active);
  const dem=STATE.walls.filter(w=>w.state==='demolished'),neu=STATE.walls.filter(w=>w.state==='new');
  const builtins=STATE.items.filter(it=>isBI(it)).length;
  const floorTile=rooms.filter(r=>FLOORS[r.floor].wet&&r.floor!=='keep').reduce((s,r)=>s+r.area,0)/1e6;
  const floorVinyl=rooms.filter(r=>!FLOORS[r.floor].wet).reduce((s,r)=>s+r.area,0)/1e6;
  const bounds=R.bounds||{x0:0,x1:1,y0:0,y1:1};
  const area=rooms.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.area,0);
  STATE.M={rooms,open:R.open,bounds,area,live,dem,neu,builtins,floorTile,floorVinyl,
    glass:live.filter(w=>w.type==='glass').length,half:live.filter(w=>w.type==='half').length,
    wet:rooms.filter(r=>WET.has(r.program)&&r.program!=='Service Yard').length,
    floorCost:rooms.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.floorCost,0),carpCost:STATE.carp.reduce((s,c)=>s+(c.cost||0),0),
    timeline:null};
  STATE.M.walk=walkthrough(rooms);
  STATE.M.timeline=planTimeline(STATE.M);
  STATE.rev++;
  document.getElementById('rev').textContent='rev '+STATE.rev+' · '+STATE.log.length+' change'+(STATE.log.length===1?'':'s');
  if(msg)toast(msg);
  render();
  autosave();
}
function toast(m){const t=document.getElementById('toast');t.textContent=m;t.classList.add('show');setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'),2200);}
function loadSample(key){
  const s=SAMPLES[key];if(!s)return;
  STATE.file=s.label;STATE.kind=s.kind;
  STATE.walls=wallsFromRects(s.rects);attachOpenings(STATE.walls,s.opens);
  STATE.progAt=s.rects.map(r=>({x:(r.r[0]+r.r[2])/2,y:(r.r[1]+r.r[3])/2,program:r.p,conf:false,area:(r.r[2]-r.r[0])*(r.r[3]-r.r[1])}));
  STATE.floorAt=[];STATE.items=[];STATE.carp=[];STATE.log=[];STATE.rev=0;STATE.maxSeen=0;STATE.locked=false;STATE.sub='walls';STATE.selWall=null;STATE.selItem=null;STATE.selRoom=null;STATE.addPt=null;STATE.tool='select';
  recompute('Plan loaded: '+s.label);go(1);
}
function handleFile(f){
  STATE.pending=f.name;render();
  toast('Received '+f.name+': plan tracing is a studio step in v0.1; load a sample to continue');
}

/* ---------- Singapore renovation timeline (working days), derived from scope ---------- */
function planTimeline(M){
  const rooms=M.rooms.length;
  const structural=M.dem.length+M.neu.length>0;
  const ph=[];let t=0;
  const add=(name,dur,start,note,par)=>{const s=start??t;const e=s+dur;ph.push({name,s,e,dur,note,par:!!par});if(!par)t=e;return e;};
  if(STATE.kind==='HDB'&&structural)add('HDB renovation permit',15,0,'hacking / new-wall works need HDB approval before start');
  else add('Permit & condo / MCST approvals',5,0,STATE.kind==='HDB'?'no hacking: notification only':'MCST renovation deposit + approval');
  add('Site protection & hacking',2+M.dem.length*1,null,M.dem.length?M.dem.length+' wall(s) to demolish + debris haulage':'protection only: nothing to hack');
  if(M.neu.length)add('Masonry: new walls & plastering',M.neu.length*1+3,null,M.neu.length+' new wall(s), 3 days cure + plaster');
  add('Electrical & plumbing rough-in',Math.ceil(3+rooms*0.5),null,rooms+' rooms: points, conduits, water routing');
  if(M.wet)add('Wet works: waterproofing & wall tiles',M.wet*4,null,M.wet+' wet room(s) × 4 days');
  const fl=Math.ceil(M.floorTile/25)+Math.ceil(M.floorVinyl/60)+1;
  add('Flooring',fl,null,M.floorTile.toFixed(0)+' m² tile · '+M.floorVinyl.toFixed(0)+' m² vinyl / laminate');
  const roughEnd=ph.find(p=>p.name.startsWith('Electrical')).e;
  if(M.builtins)add('Carpentry fabrication (off-site)',15,roughEnd,M.builtins+' built-in piece(s): measured after rough-in',true);
  if(M.glass)add('Glass partitions: measure & install',3,null,M.glass+' glass wall(s), tempered');
  add('Ceiling, cornice & painting',Math.ceil(5+rooms*0.3),null,'two coats, low-VOC');
  if(M.builtins){const fab=ph.find(p=>p.par);const s=Math.max(t,fab.e);add('Carpentry installation',Math.ceil(M.builtins/2),s,'built-ins installed after painting');}
  add('Cleaning, defects & handover',2,null,'chemical wash, defects walk with client');
  const total=Math.max(...ph.map(p=>p.e));
  return {ph,total,weeks:total/5};
}
/* ===================== DRAWING ===================== */
function allBounds(){const ws=STATE.walls;const xs=ws.flatMap(w=>wpts(w).map(p=>p[0])),ys=ws.flatMap(w=>wpts(w).map(p=>p[1]));
  return {x0:Math.min(...xs),x1:Math.max(...xs),y0:Math.min(...ys),y1:Math.max(...ys)};}
const dims=it=>{const f=FURN[it.kind];return {w:it.w||f.w,d:it.d||f.d,h:it.h||f.h};};
const nameOf=it=>it.name||FURN[it.kind]['n'];
const isBI=it=>it.kind==='custom'?!!it.bi:!!FURN[it.kind]['bi'];
function itemBox(it){const f=dims(it);const w=it.rot?f.d:f.w,d=it.rot?f.w:f.d;return {x0:it.x-w/2,y0:it.y-d/2,x1:it.x+w/2,y1:it.y+d/2,h:f.h};}
function planSVG(o={}){
  const M=STATE.M;if(!M)return'';
  const B=allBounds(),pad=44,maxW=o.w||640,maxH=o.h||460;
  const s=Math.min((maxW-pad*2)/(B.x1-B.x0),(maxH-pad*2)/(B.y1-B.y0));
  const W=(B.x1-B.x0)*s+pad*2,Hh=(B.y1-B.y0)*s+pad*2;
  const X=v=>pad+(v-B.x0)*s,Y=v=>pad+(v-B.y0)*s;
  const mode=o.mode||'changes';
  const out=[`<rect width="${W}" height="${Hh}" fill="#fff"/>`];
  // grid
  for(let gx=Math.ceil(B.x0/1000)*1000;gx<=B.x1;gx+=1000)out.push(`<line x1="${X(gx)}" y1="${Y(B.y0)}" x2="${X(gx)}" y2="${Y(B.y1)}" stroke="#F0ECE8" stroke-width="1"/>`);
  for(let gy=Math.ceil(B.y0/1000)*1000;gy<=B.y1;gy+=1000)out.push(`<line x1="${X(B.x0)}" y1="${Y(gy)}" x2="${X(B.x1)}" y2="${Y(gy)}" stroke="#F0ECE8" stroke-width="1"/>`);
  // rooms
  for(const r of M.rooms){
    const sel=o.rooms&&STATE.selRoom===r.id;
    const fill=o.theme?floorFill(r,o.theme):(FLOORS[r.floor]&&o.floorCol?(FLOORS[r.floor].tex?'url(#ftx-'+FLOORS[r.floor].lib+') '+FLOORS[r.floor].col:FLOORS[r.floor].col):PTINT[r.program]||PTINT.Unassigned);
    if(!o.theme&&o.floorCol&&FLOORS[r.floor]&&FLOORS[r.floor].tex&&!out.some(x=>x.indexOf('id="ftx-'+FLOORS[r.floor].lib+'"')>0))out.push(`<defs><pattern id="ftx-${FLOORS[r.floor].lib}" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse" width="${1200*s}" height="${1200*s}"><image href="${FLOORS[r.floor].tex}" width="${1200*s}" height="${1200*s}" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice" opacity=".9"/></pattern></defs>`);
    for(const c of r.cells)out.push(`<rect x="${X(c.x0)}" y="${Y(c.y0)}" width="${(c.x1-c.x0)*s}" height="${(c.y1-c.y0)*s}" fill="${fill}" ${o.rooms?`class="wall-hit" onclick="pickRoom('${r.id}')"`:''}/>`);
    if(sel)for(const c of r.cells)out.push(`<rect x="${X(c.x0)+1.5}" y="${Y(c.y0)+1.5}" width="${(c.x1-c.x0)*s-3}" height="${(c.y1-c.y0)*s-3}" fill="none" stroke="#B99A81" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-dasharray="5 4" pointer-events="none"/>`);
  }
  if(o.underlay&&window.ulPlanLayer)out.push(window.ulPlanLayer(X,Y,s,o));
  for(const r of M.open){
    for(const c of r.cells)out.push(`<rect x="${X(c.x0)}" y="${Y(c.y0)}" width="${(c.x1-c.x0)*s}" height="${(c.y1-c.y0)*s}" fill="#F6E4DE"/>`);
    out.push(`<text x="${X(r.cx)}" y="${Y(r.cy)}" font-size="9.5" font-weight="500" fill="#A8503B" text-anchor="middle">open: exterior breached · not a room</text>`);
  }
  // door swing zones + clearance halos (space-available layer)
  if(o.furn&&o.space)for(const r of M.rooms){
    for(const sw of r.space.swings)out.push(`<rect x="${X(sw.x0)}" y="${Y(sw.y0)}" width="${(sw.x1-sw.x0)*s}" height="${(sw.y1-sw.y0)*s}" fill="#9C7B5B" opacity=".08"/>`);
    for(const it of r.items){const cb=clearanceBox(it,r);if(!FURN[it.kind].clr)continue;
      out.push(`<rect x="${X(cb.x0)}" y="${Y(cb.y0)}" width="${(cb.x1-cb.x0)*s}" height="${(cb.y1-cb.y0)*s}" fill="#B99A81" opacity=".10" stroke="#B99A81" stroke-width=".8" stroke-dasharray="3 3" pointer-events="none"/>`);}
  }
  // furniture: drawn as real plan symbols
  if(o.furn)for(const it of STATE.items){
    const b=itemBox(it),sel=STATE.selItem===it.id;
    const rm=M.rooms.find(r=>r.items.includes(it));
    const bad=rm&&rm.space.issues.some(i=>i.id===it.id&&i.sev==='bad'),warn=rm&&rm.space.issues.some(i=>i.id===it.id);
    out.push(symbolSVG(it,X,Y,s,o.theme));
    if(sel||bad||warn)out.push(`<rect x="${X(b.x0)-2}" y="${Y(b.y0)-2}" width="${(b.x1-b.x0)*s+4}" height="${(b.y1-b.y0)*s+4}" rx="3" fill="none" stroke="${bad?'#A8503B':(warn?'#9A6A2E':'#373837')}" stroke-width="${bad||sel?2:1.2}" ${warn&&!bad?'stroke-dasharray="4 3"':''} pointer-events="none"/>`);
    if(o.rooms)out.push(`<rect x="${X(b.x0)}" y="${Y(b.y0)}" width="${(b.x1-b.x0)*s}" height="${(b.y1-b.y0)*s}" fill="transparent" class="wall-hit" style="cursor:grab" onpointerdown="dragStart('${it.id}',event)"><title>${nameOf(it)} · ${dims(it).w}×${dims(it).d}×${dims(it).h} mm</title></rect>`);
  }
  // room labels (after furniture so they stay readable)
  if(!o.nolabel)for(const r of M.rooms){
    out.push(`<text x="${X(r.cx)}" y="${Y(r.cy)-3}" font-size="10.5" font-weight="500" fill="#373837" text-anchor="middle" pointer-events="none" paint-order="stroke" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="3" stroke-opacity=".75">${r.program}${r.suggested?' ?':''}</text>`);
    out.push(`<text x="${X(r.cx)}" y="${Y(r.cy)+9}" font-size="8.5" fill="#6B6966" text-anchor="middle" font-family="IBM Plex Mono,monospace" pointer-events="none" paint-order="stroke" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="3" stroke-opacity=".75">${m2(r.area)}</text>`);}
  // walls
  const show=w=>mode==='existing'?w.state!=='new':(mode==='proposed'?w.state!=='demolished':true);
  for(const w of STATE.walls.filter(show)){
    const [[x1,y1],[x2,y2]]=wpts(w);
    const lw=Math.max(3,w.t*s);
    let col='#373837',dash='';
    const dem=mode==='changes'&&w.state==='demolished';
    if(mode!=='existing'&&w.state==='new')col=o.theme?o.theme.dark:'#B99A81';
    if(dem){col='#A8503B';dash='stroke-dasharray="7 5"';}
    if(o.theme&&w.state!=='new')col=o.theme.dark;
    const sel=o.clickable&&STATE.selWall===w.id;
    if(sel)out.push(`<line x1="${X(x1)}" y1="${Y(y1)}" x2="${X(x2)}" y2="${Y(y2)}" stroke="#B99A81" stroke-width="${lw+8}" stroke-linecap="round" opacity=".45"/>`);
    if(w.type==='glass'){
      out.push(`<line x1="${X(x1)}" y1="${Y(y1)}" x2="${X(x2)}" y2="${Y(y2)}" stroke="#DCE6E9" stroke-width="${lw}" stroke-linecap="butt" ${dash}/>`);
      out.push(`<line x1="${X(x1)}" y1="${Y(y1)}" x2="${X(x2)}" y2="${Y(y2)}" stroke="${dem?col:'#7A98A3'}" stroke-width="1.6" ${dash}/>`);
    }else if(w.type==='half'){
      out.push(`<line x1="${X(x1)}" y1="${Y(y1)}" x2="${X(x2)}" y2="${Y(y2)}" stroke="#D2CDCA" stroke-width="${lw}" stroke-linecap="butt" ${dash}/>`);
      out.push(`<line x1="${X(x1)}" y1="${Y(y1)}" x2="${X(x2)}" y2="${Y(y2)}" stroke="${col}" stroke-width="1.4" stroke-dasharray="3 3"/>`);
    }else out.push(`<line x1="${X(x1)}" y1="${Y(y1)}" x2="${X(x2)}" y2="${Y(y2)}" stroke="${col}" stroke-width="${lw}" stroke-linecap="butt" ${dash}/>`);
    // openings
    for(const op of w.opens){
      const oshow=mode==='existing'?op.state!=='new':(mode==='proposed'?op.state!=='removed':true);
      if(!oshow||dem)continue;
      const h=w.axis==='x';
      const ax=h?X(op.a):X(w.fixed),bx=h?X(op.b):X(w.fixed),ay=h?Y(w.fixed):Y(op.a),by=h?Y(w.fixed):Y(op.b);
      const rem=mode==='changes'&&op.state==='removed';
      if(!rem)out.push(`<line x1="${ax}" y1="${ay}" x2="${bx}" y2="${by}" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="${lw+1}"/>`);
      const oc=rem?'#A8503B':(op.state==='new'&&mode!=='existing'?'#B99A81':(op.kind==='window'?'#7A98A3':'#9C7B5B'));
      if(op.kind==='door'){
        const L=(op.b-op.a)*s,into=op.into||1,hA=op.hinge!=='b';
        const hx=hA?ax:bx,hy=hA?ay:by,jx=hA?bx:ax,jy=hA?by:ay;           // hinge + far jamb
        const tx=h?hx:hx+into*L,ty=h?hy+into*L:hy;                        // leaf tip
        const cross=(tx-hx)*(jy-hy)-(ty-hy)*(jx-hx);const sweep=cross>0?1:0;
        out.push(`<line x1="${ax}" y1="${ay}" x2="${bx}" y2="${by}" stroke="${oc}" stroke-width="1" ${rem?'stroke-dasharray="4 3"':'opacity=".35"'}/>`);
        if(!rem){out.push(`<line x1="${hx}" y1="${hy}" x2="${tx}" y2="${ty}" stroke="${oc}" stroke-width="1.8"/>`);
          out.push(`<path d="M${tx},${ty} A${L},${L} 0 0 ${sweep} ${jx},${jy}" fill="none" stroke="${oc}" stroke-width=".8" opacity=".75"/>`);}
      }else if(op.kind==='opening'){
        out.push(`<line x1="${ax}" y1="${ay}" x2="${bx}" y2="${by}" stroke="${oc}" stroke-width="1" stroke-dasharray="2 3" opacity=".7"/>`);
        const off=lw/2;
        out.push(h?`<line x1="${ax}" y1="${ay-off}" x2="${ax}" y2="${ay+off}" stroke="${oc}" stroke-width="1.2"/><line x1="${bx}" y1="${by-off}" x2="${bx}" y2="${by+off}" stroke="${oc}" stroke-width="1.2"/>`
                  :`<line x1="${ax-off}" y1="${ay}" x2="${ax+off}" y2="${ay}" stroke="${oc}" stroke-width="1.2"/><line x1="${bx-off}" y1="${by}" x2="${bx+off}" y2="${by}" stroke="${oc}" stroke-width="1.2"/>`);
      }else{
        const off=Math.max(1.5,lw*0.28);
        out.push(`<line x1="${ax}" y1="${ay}" x2="${bx}" y2="${by}" stroke="${oc}" stroke-width="1.2" ${rem?'stroke-dasharray="4 3"':''}/>`);
        out.push(h?`<line x1="${ax}" y1="${ay-off}" x2="${bx}" y2="${by-off}" stroke="${oc}" stroke-width=".8"/><line x1="${ax}" y1="${ay+off}" x2="${bx}" y2="${by+off}" stroke="${oc}" stroke-width=".8"/>`
                  :`<line x1="${ax-off}" y1="${ay}" x2="${bx-off}" y2="${by}" stroke="${oc}" stroke-width=".8"/><line x1="${ax+off}" y1="${ay}" x2="${bx+off}" y2="${by}" stroke="${oc}" stroke-width=".8"/>`);
      }
    }
    if(o.clickable)out.push(`<line class="wall-hit" x1="${X(x1)}" y1="${Y(y1)}" x2="${X(x2)}" y2="${Y(y2)}" stroke="transparent" stroke-width="${Math.max(14,lw+6)}" onclick="pickWall('${w.id}',event)"><title>${w.id} · ${mm(wlen(w))} · ${w.type} · ${w.state}${w.ext?' · exterior':''}</title></line>`);
  }
  // dimension layer for exports: room sizes, door widths, exterior wall segments
  if(o.dims){
    for(const r of M.rooms){const bw=r.bbox.x1-r.bbox.x0,bd=r.bbox.y1-r.bbox.y0;
      out.push(`<text x="${X(r.cx)}" y="${Y(r.cy)+20}" font-size="8" fill="#5A5C5A" text-anchor="middle" font-family="IBM Plex Mono,monospace">${(bw/1000).toFixed(2)} × ${(bd/1000).toFixed(2)} m</text>`);}
    for(const w of STATE.walls.filter(show)){for(const op of w.opens){if(op.state==='removed')continue;const c=(op.a+op.b)/2,h=w.axis==='x';
      out.push(`<text x="${h?X(c):X(w.fixed)+9}" y="${h?Y(w.fixed)-6:Y(c)+3}" font-size="6.5" fill="#9C7B5B" text-anchor="${h?'middle':'start'}" font-family="IBM Plex Mono,monospace">${Math.round(op.b-op.a)}</text>`);}
      if(w.ext){const [[x1,y1],[x2,y2]]=wpts(w);const h=w.axis==='x';const off=h?(w.fixed===B.y0?-16:16):(w.fixed===B.x0?-16:16);
        const mx=(X(x1)+X(x2))/2,my=(Y(y1)+Y(y2))/2;
        out.push(h?`<line x1="${X(x1)}" y1="${my+off}" x2="${X(x2)}" y2="${my+off}" stroke="#8C8A86" stroke-width=".6"/><line x1="${X(x1)}" y1="${my+off-3}" x2="${X(x1)}" y2="${my+off+3}" stroke="#8C8A86" stroke-width=".6"/><line x1="${X(x2)}" y1="${my+off-3}" x2="${X(x2)}" y2="${my+off+3}" stroke="#8C8A86" stroke-width=".6"/><text x="${mx}" y="${my+off+(off<0?-3:9)}" font-size="7" fill="#5A5C5A" text-anchor="middle" font-family="IBM Plex Mono,monospace">${Math.round(wlen(w))}</text>`
                  :`<line x1="${mx+off}" y1="${Y(y1)}" x2="${mx+off}" y2="${Y(y2)}" stroke="#8C8A86" stroke-width=".6"/><line x1="${mx+off-3}" y1="${Y(y1)}" x2="${mx+off+3}" y2="${Y(y1)}" stroke="#8C8A86" stroke-width=".6"/><line x1="${mx+off-3}" y1="${Y(y2)}" x2="${mx+off+3}" y2="${Y(y2)}" stroke="#8C8A86" stroke-width=".6"/><text x="${mx+off+(off<0?-4:4)}" y="${my}" font-size="7" fill="#5A5C5A" text-anchor="middle" font-family="IBM Plex Mono,monospace" transform="rotate(-90 ${mx+off+(off<0?-4:4)} ${my})">${Math.round(wlen(w))}</text>`);}}
  }
  // wall ID tags + highlight: so "W23" always points at something on the plan
  const hl=STATE.hlWall;
  if(hl){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===hl);if(w&&show(w)){const [[x1,y1],[x2,y2]]=wpts(w);
    out.push(`<line x1="${X(x1)}" y1="${Y(y1)}" x2="${X(x2)}" y2="${Y(y2)}" stroke="#B99A81" stroke-width="${Math.max(3,w.t*s)+12}" stroke-linecap="round" opacity=".55" pointer-events="none"><animate attributeName="opacity" values=".55;.2;.55" dur="1.2s" repeatCount="indefinite"/></line>`);}}
  const tagWalls=o.walltags===true?STATE.walls.filter(show):(Array.isArray(o.walltags)?STATE.walls.filter(w=>o.walltags.includes(w.id)&&show(w)):[]);
  for(const w of tagWalls){const [[x1,y1],[x2,y2]]=wpts(w);const mx=(X(x1)+X(x2))/2,my=(Y(y1)+Y(y2))/2;const on=hl===w.id||STATE.selWall===w.id;
    out.push(`<g pointer-events="none"><rect x="${mx-13}" y="${my-6.5}" width="26" height="13" rx="6.5" fill="${on?'#B99A81':'#fff'}" stroke="${on?'#B99A81':'#8C8A86'}" stroke-width=".8"/><text x="${mx}" y="${my+3.3}" font-size="8" font-weight="600" fill="${on?'#373837':'#5A5C5A'}" text-anchor="middle" font-family="IBM Plex Mono,monospace">${w.id}</text></g>`);}
  // pending add-wall point
  if(STATE.addPt)out.push(`<circle cx="${X(STATE.addPt[0])}" cy="${Y(STATE.addPt[1])}" r="5" fill="#B99A81" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="1.5"/>`);
  // dimension ticks
  out.push(`<text x="${X((B.x0+B.x1)/2)}" y="${Hh-12}" font-size="9" fill="#8C8A86" text-anchor="middle" font-family="IBM Plex Mono,monospace">${mm(B.x1-B.x0)}</text>`);
  out.push(`<text x="${12}" y="${Y((B.y0+B.y1)/2)}" font-size="9" fill="#8C8A86" text-anchor="middle" font-family="IBM Plex Mono,monospace" transform="rotate(-90 12 ${Y((B.y0+B.y1)/2)})">${mm(B.y1-B.y0)}</text>`);
  if(o.underlay&&window.ulDevLayer)out.push(window.ulDevLayer(X,Y,s,o));
  if(window.stPlanLayer)out.push(window.stPlanLayer(X,Y,s,o));
  return `<svg id="${o.id||'plan-svg'}" viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${Hh}" ${o.clickable?'onclick="planClick(event)"':''} style="width:100%;touch-action:none">${out.join('')}</svg>`;
}

/* ---------- 3D isometric build: walls with real heights, openings cut, furniture as boxes ---------- */
function wallBoxes(w){
  const t2=w.t/2,H=wH(w),out=[];
  const push=(a,b,z0,z1)=>{if(b-a<10||z1-z0<10)return;
    out.push(w.axis==='x'?{lo:[a,w.fixed-t2,z0],hi:[b,w.fixed+t2,z1]}:{lo:[w.fixed-t2,a,z0],hi:[w.fixed+t2,b,z1]});};
  const ops=w.opens.filter(o=>o.state!=='removed').sort((p,q)=>p.a-q.a);
  let cur=w.a;
  for(const o of ops){
    if(o.a>cur)push(cur,o.a,0,H);
    if(o.sill>0)push(o.a,o.b,0,Math.min(o.sill,H));
    if(o.head<H)push(o.a,o.b,o.head,H);
    cur=o.b;}
  if(cur<w.b)push(cur,w.b,0,H);
  return out;
}
function shade(hex,f){const n=parseInt(hex.slice(1),16);return 'rgb('+Math.round(((n>>16)&255)*f)+','+Math.round(((n>>8)&255)*f)+','+Math.round((n&255)*f)+')';}
STATE.iso={yaw:0};
function iso3D(o={}){
  const M=STATE.M;if(!M)return'';
  const T=o.theme||null;
  const c30=Math.cos(Math.PI/6),s30=0.5,SC=0.045;
  const yaw=(o.yaw!=null?o.yaw:STATE.iso.yaw)*Math.PI/180,cy_=Math.cos(yaw),sy_=Math.sin(yaw);
  const CX=(M.bounds.x0+M.bounds.x1)/2,CY=(M.bounds.y0+M.bounds.y1)/2;
  const rot=(x,y)=>[CX+(x-CX)*cy_-(y-CY)*sy_,CY+(x-CX)*sy_+(y-CY)*cy_];
  const P=(x,y,z)=>{const [rx,ry]=rot(x,y);return [(rx-ry)*c30*SC,((rx+ry)*s30-z)*SC];};
  const polys=[],flats=[];
  const room=o.room?M.rooms.find(r=>r.id===o.room):null;
  const rooms=room?[room]:M.rooms;
  // which faces face the viewer depends on the rotation: cull by rotated normal
  const vis={px:cy_-sy_>0,mx:sy_-cy_>0,py:-sy_-cy_>0,my:sy_+cy_>0};
  const sh={px:0.62+0.28*Math.max(0,cy_-sy_)/1.42,mx:0.62+0.28*Math.max(0,sy_-cy_)/1.42,py:0.62+0.28*Math.max(0,-sy_-cy_)/1.42,my:0.62+0.28*Math.max(0,sy_+cy_)/1.42};
  const addBox=(lo,hi,col,op,extra)=>{
    const C=i=>[i&1?hi[0]:lo[0],i&2?hi[1]:lo[1],i&4?hi[2]:lo[2]];
    const face=(ids,f)=>{const wp=ids.map(C);const d=wp.reduce((s,p)=>{const [rx,ry]=rot(p[0],p[1]);return s+rx+ry+p[2]*0.55;},0)/4;
      polys.push({d,pts:wp.map(p=>P(...p)),col:shade(col,f),op,extra});};
    face([4,5,7,6],1);
    if(vis.my)face([0,1,5,4],sh.my);if(vis.px)face([1,3,7,5],sh.px);if(vis.py)face([3,2,6,7],sh.py);if(vis.mx)face([2,0,4,6],sh.mx);};
  // floors
  for(const r of rooms){const fc=floorFill(r,T,'#E8E4DF');
    for(const c of r.cells)flats.push({pts:[P(c.x0,c.y0,0),P(c.x1,c.y0,0),P(c.x1,c.y1,0),P(c.x0,c.y1,0)],col:fc,stroke:T?T.floor:'#DDD7D0'});}
  // walls
  const wallsIn=room?STATE.walls.filter(w=>room.walls.includes(w.id)||w.state==='demolished'&&room.cells.some(c=>w.axis==='x'?w.fixed>=c.y0&&w.fixed<=c.y1&&w.a<c.x1&&w.b>c.x0:w.fixed>=c.x0&&w.fixed<=c.x1&&w.a<c.y1&&w.b>c.y0)):STATE.walls;
  for(const w of wallsIn){
    if(w.state==='demolished'){const t2=w.t/2,[[x1,y1],[x2,y2]]=wpts(w);
      const pts=w.axis==='x'?[[x1,y1-t2],[x2,y1-t2],[x2,y1+t2],[x1,y1+t2]]:[[x1-t2,y1],[x1-t2,y2],[x1+t2,y2],[x1+t2,y1]];
      flats.push({pts:pts.map(p=>P(p[0],p[1],2)),col:'none',stroke:'#A8503B',dash:'4 3'});continue;}
    let col=T?T.wall:(w.state==='new'?'#B99A81':'#D2CDCA');
    let op=1;
    if(w.type==='glass'){col='#B9CDD3';op=.45;}
    // cutaway for single-room views: front walls become 250mm stubs
    let boxes=wallBoxes(w);
    if(room){const back=w.axis==='x'?w.fixed<room.cy:w.fixed<room.cx;
      if(!back)boxes=boxes.map(b=>({lo:b.lo,hi:[b.hi[0],b.hi[1],Math.min(b.hi[2],250)]})).filter(b=>b.hi[2]>b.lo[2]);}
    for(const b of boxes)addBox(b.lo,b.hi,col,op);
  }
  // furniture: assembled from parts
  const items=room?room.items:STATE.items;
  for(const it of items){const P2=palette(it,T);
    for(const b of worldParts(it))addBox(b.lo,b.hi,P2[b.role]||P2.frame,b.role==='glass'?.5:1);}
  polys.sort((a,b)=>a.d-b.d);
  const all=[...polys.flatMap(p=>p.pts),...flats.flatMap(p=>p.pts)];
  if(!all.length)return'';
  const x0=Math.min(...all.map(q=>q[0])),y0=Math.min(...all.map(q=>q[1]));
  const W=Math.max(...all.map(q=>q[0]))-x0+40,Hh=Math.max(...all.map(q=>q[1]))-y0+40;
  const TX=q=>((q[0]-x0+20).toFixed(1)+','+(q[1]-y0+20).toFixed(1));
  const out=[`<rect width="${W}" height="${Hh}" fill="${T?T.wall:'#fff'}" opacity="${T?.35:1}"/>`];
  for(const f of flats)out.push(`<polygon points="${f.pts.map(TX).join(' ')}" fill="${f.col}" stroke="${f.stroke}" stroke-width=".8" ${f.dash?`stroke-dasharray="${f.dash}"`:''}/>`);
  for(const p of polys)out.push(`<polygon points="${p.pts.map(TX).join(' ')}" fill="${p.col}" fill-opacity="${p.op}" stroke="#fff" stroke-width=".5" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`);
  return `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${Hh}" style="width:100%;${o.maxh?'max-height:'+o.maxh+'px':''}">${out.join('')}</svg>`;
}

/* ---------- timeline chart ---------- */
function ganttSVG(tl){
  const W=680,rowH=26,left=232,top=28,weeks=Math.ceil(tl.total/5)+1;
  const Hh=top+tl.ph.length*rowH+34;
  const x=d=>left+(d/(weeks*5))*(W-left-16);
  const out=[`<rect width="${W}" height="${Hh}" fill="#fff"/>`];
  for(let w=0;w<=weeks;w++){out.push(`<line x1="${x(w*5)}" y1="${top-6}" x2="${x(w*5)}" y2="${Hh-26}" stroke="${w%4===0?'#D2CDCA':'#F0ECE8'}" stroke-width="1"/>`);
    if(w%2===0)out.push(`<text x="${x(w*5)}" y="${top-11}" font-size="9" fill="#8C8A86" text-anchor="middle" font-family="IBM Plex Mono,monospace">wk ${w}</text>`);}
  tl.ph.forEach((p,i)=>{const y=top+i*rowH;
    const col=p.name.includes('permit')||p.name.includes('approvals')?'#D2CDCA':(p.par?'#9C7B5B':(p.name.includes('hacking')?'#A8503B':'#B99A81'));
    out.push(`<text x="${left-10}" y="${y+16}" font-size="10.5" fill="#373837" text-anchor="end">${p.name}</text>`);
    out.push(`<rect x="${x(p.s)}" y="${y+5}" width="${Math.max(3,x(p.e)-x(p.s))}" height="16" rx="3" fill="${col}"/>`);
    out.push(`<text x="${x(p.e)+5}" y="${y+16}" font-size="9" fill="#8C8A86" font-family="IBM Plex Mono,monospace">${p.dur} d</text>`);});
  out.push(`<line x1="${x(tl.total)}" y1="${top-6}" x2="${x(tl.total)}" y2="${Hh-26}" stroke="#373837" stroke-width="1.2" stroke-dasharray="5 4"/>`);
  out.push(`<text x="${x(tl.total)}" y="${Hh-10}" font-size="10" font-weight="500" fill="#373837" text-anchor="middle">handover · day ${tl.total}</text>`);
  return `<svg viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${Hh}" class="gantt" style="width:100%">${out.join('')}</svg>`;
}
/* ===================== HANDLERS (every edit → snapshot → log → recompute) ===================== */
STATE.hist=[];
function snap(){STATE.redo=[];STATE.hist.push(JSON.stringify({walls:STATE.walls,items:STATE.items,progAt:STATE.progAt,floorAt:STATE.floorAt,carp:STATE.carp}));if(STATE.hist.length>60)STATE.hist.shift();}
STATE.redo=[];
const snapshotNow=()=>JSON.stringify({walls:STATE.walls,items:STATE.items,progAt:STATE.progAt,floorAt:STATE.floorAt,carp:STATE.carp});
function undo(){if(!STATE.hist.length){toast('Nothing to undo');return;}
  const last=STATE.log[STATE.log.length-1];STATE.redo.push(JSON.stringify({s:snapshotNow(),log:last}));
  const h=JSON.parse(STATE.hist.pop());Object.assign(STATE,h);STATE.log.pop();STATE.selWall=null;STATE.selItem=null;recompute('Undo: '+(last?last.action+' '+last.target:'')+' (Ctrl+Shift+Z to redo)');}
function redo(){if(!STATE.redo.length){toast('Nothing to redo');return;}
  const r=JSON.parse(STATE.redo.pop());STATE.hist.push(snapshotNow());Object.assign(STATE,JSON.parse(r.s));if(r.log){r.log.ts=new Date(r.log.ts);STATE.log.push(r.log);}recompute('Redo: '+(r.log?r.log.action+' '+r.log.target:''));}
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(!(e.metaKey||e.ctrlKey))return;if(/input|select|textarea/i.test(e.target.tagName))return;
  const k=e.key.toLowerCase();if(k==='z'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();undo();}else if((k==='z'&&e.shiftKey)||k==='y'){e.preventDefault();redo();}});
function setTool(t){STATE.tool=t;STATE.addPt=null;render();}
function setView(v){STATE.view=v;render();}
function pickWall(id,e){if(e)e.stopPropagation();if(STATE.tool!=='select')return;STATE.selWall=STATE.selWall===id?null:id;render();}
function pickRoom(id){STATE.selRoom=id;STATE.selItem=null;render();}
function pickItem(id,e){if(e)e.stopPropagation();STATE.selItem=id;const it=STATE.items.find(i=>i.id===id);const r=STATE.M.rooms.find(r=>inRoom(r,it.x,it.y));if(r)STATE.selRoom=r.id;render();}
function svgPoint(e){const svg=e.currentTarget;const pt=svg.createSVGPoint();pt.x=e.clientX;pt.y=e.clientY;const q=pt.matrixTransform(svg.getScreenCTM().inverse());
  const B=allBounds(),pad=44,vb=svg.viewBox.baseVal;const s=Math.min((vb.width-pad*2)/(B.x1-B.x0),(vb.height-pad*2)/(B.y1-B.y0));
  return [Math.round((B.x0+(q.x-pad)/s)/100)*100,Math.round((B.y0+(q.y-pad)/s)/100)*100];}
function planClick(e){
  if(STATE.tool!=='addwall')return;
  const p=svgPoint(e);
  if(!STATE.addPt){STATE.addPt=p;render();toast('Now click the other end: walls snap to the 100 mm grid, orthogonal only');return;}
  const [x1,y1]=STATE.addPt,[x2,y2]=p;
  const axis=Math.abs(x2-x1)>=Math.abs(y2-y1)?'x':'y';
  const a=axis==='x'?Math.min(x1,x2):Math.min(y1,y2),b=axis==='x'?Math.max(x1,x2):Math.max(y1,y2);
  const fixed=axis==='x'?y1:x1;
  STATE.addPt=null;
  if(b-a<300){toast('Too short: a wall needs at least 300 mm');render();return;}
  snap();
  const w={id:nextWallId(),axis,fixed,a,b,t:T_INT,ext:false,type:'full',state:'new',opens:[]};
  STATE.walls.push(w);STATE.selWall=w.id;STATE.tool='select';
  logIt('Add wall',w.id,'-','full wall · '+mm(b-a)+' · 100 mm partition',axis==='x'?'along x at y '+fixed:'along y at x '+fixed);
  recompute(w.id+' added: rooms re-detected');
}
function demolish(id){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!w)return;
  if(w.ext){logIt('Refused',id,'exterior wall','unchanged','structural / exterior: cannot be hacked; needs PE + HDB/BCA approval');
    STATE.rev++;toast('Refused: '+id+' is an exterior / structural wall: recorded, not changed');render();return;}
  snap();
  if(w.state==='new'){STATE.walls=STATE.walls.filter(x=>x.id!==id);STATE.selWall=null;logIt('Remove new wall',id,'new wall','-','');recompute(id+' removed');return;}
  w.state='demolished';logIt('Demolish',id,'existing · '+w.type,'demolished','hack + make good, debris haulage');recompute(id+' marked for demolition: rooms merged'+(STATE.kind==='HDB'?' · HDB permit and PE check added to the compliance list':''));}
function restore(id){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!w)return;snap();w.state='existing';logIt('Restore',id,'demolished','existing','');recompute(id+' restored');}
function setType(id,type){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!w||w.type===type)return;
  if(w.ext&&type!=='full'){logIt('Refused',id,'exterior wall','unchanged','exterior wall cannot become '+type);STATE.rev++;toast('Refused: exterior wall stays full height');render();return;}
  snap();const b=w.type;w.type=type;
  logIt('Change wall type',id,b,type,type==='half'?'1,100 mm half wall':(type==='glass'?'tempered glass partition, full height':'full-height wall'));
  recompute(id+' → '+type);}
function addOpen(id,kind){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!w)return;
  const L=wlen(w),want=kind==='door'?900:(kind==='opening'?1500:1200);if(L<want+300){toast('Wall too short for a '+kind);return;}
  const c=(w.a+w.b)/2;let a=c-want/2,b=c+want/2;
  const clash=w.opens.some(o=>o.state!=='removed'&&o.b>a&&o.a<b);if(clash){toast('An opening already sits there: remove it first');return;}
  snap();const o={id:nextOpenId(),kind,a,b,sill:kind==='window'?900:0,head:2100,state:'new'};w.opens.push(o);
  logIt('Add '+kind,id+' · '+o.id,'-',kind+' '+want+' mm','lintel required over new opening');recompute(kind+' added to '+id);}
function removeOpen(wid,oid){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===wid);const o=w&&w.opens.find(x=>x.id===oid);if(!o)return;snap();
  if(o.state==='new'){w.opens=w.opens.filter(x=>x.id!==oid);logIt('Remove new '+o.kind,wid+' · '+oid,o.kind,'-','');}
  else{o.state='removed';logIt('Seal '+o.kind,wid+' · '+oid,o.kind+' '+(o.b-o.a)+' mm','sealed','brick up + plaster');}
  recompute('Opening updated on '+wid);}
function restoreOpen(wid,oid){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===wid);const o=w&&w.opens.find(x=>x.id===oid);if(!o)return;snap();o.state='existing';logIt('Restore '+o.kind,wid+' · '+oid,'sealed',o.kind,'');recompute('Opening restored');}
function setProgram(rid,p){const r=STATE.M.rooms.find(r=>r.id===rid);if(!r)return;snap();

let itemN=0;
function addItem(rid,kind){const r=STATE.M.rooms.find(r=>r.id===rid);if(!r)return;snap();
  const f=FURN[kind],n=r.items.length;
  // walk-in placement: first item centred, next ones stepped along the room's long axis
  const long=(r.bbox.x1-r.bbox.x0)>=(r.bbox.y1-r.bbox.y0);
  const step=(n%2?1:-1)*Math.ceil(n/2)*Math.max(700,f.bi?900:600);
  let x=r.cx+(long?step:0),y=r.cy+(long?0:step);
  x=Math.min(r.bbox.x1-f.w/2-80,Math.max(r.bbox.x0+f.w/2+80,x));y=Math.min(r.bbox.y1-f.d/2-80,Math.max(r.bbox.y0+f.d/2+80,y));
  const it={id:'F'+(++itemN),kind,x:Math.round(x/50)*50,y:Math.round(y/50)*50,rot:0};STATE.items.push(it);STATE.selItem=it.id;
  logIt('Place '+(f.bi?'built-in':'furniture'),rid+' · '+r.program,'-',f.n,f.w+'×'+f.d+'×'+f.h+' mm');recompute(f.n+' placed in '+r.program);}

  logIt('Place custom '+(bi?'built-in':'piece'),rid+' · '+r.program,'-',nm,w+'×'+d+'×'+h+' mm');recompute(nm+' placed in '+r.program);}
