lv=levels();if(!r&&p.h>lv.topY&&p.h<lv.ohBottom){notes.push(`On the backsplash, ${p.h-lv.topY} mm above the worktop. ${S.splash==='tiles'?'Tiler cuts the tile around the plate':'Cut-out 70 × 70 mm in the '+SPLASH.find(t=>t.id===S.splash).name.toLowerCase()+' panel'}.`);return {where:'Backsplash',notes,warn};}}\n  if(!r){notes.push('On the wall, clear of the carpentry. Nothing to cut.');if(p.x<0||p.x>L.baseRun||p.h<0||p.h>Hh)warn.push('Outside the drawing area.');return {where:'Wall',notes,warn};}\n  const i=L.rows.findIndex(q=>q.m.id===r.m.id)+1;const lx=Math.round(p.x-r.x);const where=`${cat[r.m.key]?cat[r.m.key].label:'Module'} (#${i})`;\n  if(r.kind==='fridge'||r.kind==='gap'){notes.push(`Behind the ${r.kind==='fridge'?'fridge':'dishwasher'}: no carpentry. Keep the point within reach, ${r.kind==='fridge'?'above the appliance or to the side':'in the next cabinet'}.`);if(r.kind==='gap'&&p.t==='socket')warn.push('Many installers want the dishwasher socket in the next cabinet, not behind the machine.');return {where,notes,warn};}\n  const cut=wet?(p.t==='gas'?'Ø40 mm hose hole':'Ø50 mm cut-out'):p.t==='led'?'80 × 50 mm driver void in the top panel':'80 × 80 mm access cut-out';\n  if(/open|shelves|display|niche|shoes/.test(r.kind)&&!wet)notes.push(`Stays visible inside the open ${r.kind==='display'?'display':'shelves'}: ${cut} in the back panel, ${lx} mm from the cabinet's left side, ${p.h} mm above floor.`);\n  else notes.push(`${cut} in the back panel, ${lx} mm from the cabinet's left side, ${p.h} mm above floor${S.svcBack?'. let plugCtx=null,plugReady=false;
function plugFrame(){return document.getElementById('plug-frame');}
function openCarpentry(roomId,kind,wallId,restoreId){
  const r=STATE.M.rooms.find(r=>r.id===roomId);if(!r)return;
  const w=wallId?STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===wallId):null;
  const rec=restoreId?STATE.carp.find(c=>c.id===restoreId):null;
  plugCtx={project:STATE.project?STATE.project.name:STATE.file,room:{id:r.id,program:r.program},
    wall:w?{id:w.id,length:clearLen(w),height:wH(w)}:null,kind:rec?rec.kind:kind,restore:rec?rec.state:null,restoreId:restoreId||null};
  const f=plugFrame();plugReady=false;document.getElementById('plug').classList.add('show');
  f.srcdoc=plugSrc();
}
window.addEventListener('message',e=>{const m=e.data;if(!m||typeof m!=='object')return;
  if(m.type==='space:ready'){plugReady=true;if(plugCtx)plugFrame().contentWindow.postMessage({type:'space:init',ctx:plugCtx},'*');}
  else if(m.type==='space:close'){closePlug();}
  else if(m.type==='space:save'){saveCarp(m.design);closePlug();}
});
function closePlug(){document.getElementById('plug').classList.remove('show');plugFrame().srcdoc='';}
function nextCarpId(){let n=0;for(const c of STATE.carp){const k=+c.id.slice(2);if(k>n)n=k;}return 'C-'+String(n+1).padStart(2,'0');}
function saveCarp(d){
  const ctx=d.ctx||plugCtx||{};const r=STATE.M.rooms.find(r=>r.id===(ctx.room&&ctx.room.id));
  snap();
  let rec=ctx.restoreId?STATE.carp.find(c=>c.id===ctx.restoreId):null;
  const isNew=!rec;
  if(!rec){rec={id:nextCarpId(),itemId:null};STATE.carp.push(rec);}
  Object.assign(rec,{kind:d.kind,name:d.name,roomId:r?r.id:null,room:r?r.program:'-',wallId:ctx.wall?ctx.wall.id:null,run:d.run,depth:d.depth,height:d.height,
    finish:d.finish,fill:d.finishFill,carcass:d.carcass,top:d.top,cost:d.cost,lines:d.lines,modules:d.modules,score:d.score,svg:d.svg,state:d.state,updated:new Date().toISOString()});
  // place / update the built-in piece on the plan
  let it=rec.itemId?STATE.items.find(i=>i.id===rec.itemId):null;
  const label=rec.id+' '+rec.name;
  if(!it){it={id:'F'+(++itemN),kind:'custom',name:label,w:rec.run,d:rec.depth,h:rec.height,bi:true,carp:rec.id,x:r?r.cx:0,y:r?r.cy:0,rot:0,fill:rec.fill};STATE.items.push(it);rec.itemId=it.id;
    if(r){let pos=null;const f={w:rec.run,d:rec.depth,h:rec.height};
      if(rec.wallId){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===rec.wallId);const B=r.bbox;
        const side=w?(w.axis==='x'?(w.fixed===B.y0?'N':(w.fixed===B.y1?'S':null)):(w.fixed===B.x0?'W':(w.fixed===B.x1?'E':null))):null;
        if(side){const al=w.axis==='x'?((w.a+w.b)/2-B.x0)/(B.x1-B.x0):((w.a+w.b)/2-B.y0)/(B.y1-B.y0);pos=placeAgainst(r,side,f,al,w.t/2+2);}}
      if(!pos){const sd=roomSides(r);const side=['N','W','S','E'].find(k=>sd[k].wall&&!sd[k].door)||'N';pos=placeAgainst(r,side,f,0.5,52);}
      it.x=pos.x;it.y=pos.y;it.rot=pos.rot;}}
  else{it.name=label;it.w=rec.run;it.d=rec.depth;it.h=rec.height;it.fill=rec.fill;}
  STATE.selItem=it.id;if(r)STATE.selRoom=r.id;
  logIt(isNew?'Carpentry saved':'Carpentry updated',rec.id+' · '+rec.name,isNew?'-':'previous design',rec.run+'×'+rec.height+'×'+rec.depth+' mm · '+sgd(rec.cost),(rec.room||'')+(rec.wallId?' · wall '+rec.wallId:'')+' · '+rec.finish);
  const over=d.run-(d.space&&d.space.w||d.run);
  recompute(rec.id+' '+rec.name+': placed in '+(rec.room||'the plan')+(over>0?' · runs '+over+' mm over the wall: trim modules in Carpentry':''));
}
function removeCarp(id){const rec=STATE.carp.find(c=>c.id===id);if(!rec)return;snap();
  STATE.carp=STATE.carp.filter(c=>c.id!==id);STATE.items=STATE.items.filter(i=>i.id!==rec.itemId);if(STATE.selItem===rec.itemId)STATE.selItem=null;
  logIt('Carpentry removed',rec.id+' · '+rec.name,sgd(rec.cost),'-','');recompute(rec.id+' removed');}
function editCarp(id){const rec=STATE.carp.find(c=>c.id===id);if(!rec)return;openCarpentry(rec.roomId||STATE.selRoom,rec.kind,rec.wallId,rec.id);}
function carpSchedule(){const C=STATE.carp;const tot=C.reduce((s,c)=>s+c.cost,0);
  if(!C.length)return '<p class="note" style="margin-top:0">No carpentry designed yet. Pick a room, choose the wall it goes on, and open the Carpentry plug-in: every saved design lands here as its own line item.</p>';
  return `<div style="overflow-x:auto"><table><thead><tr><th>#</th><th>Piece</th><th>Room · wall</th><th class="num">Run × H × D</th><th>Finish · carcass</th><th>Modules</th><th class="num">Est.</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>
   ${C.map(c=>`<tr class="${STATE.selItem===c.itemId?'sel':''}"><td class="chg" style="white-space:nowrap"><b>${c.id}</b></td><td><b style="font-weight:500">${c.name}</b><br/><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--mute)">${({kitchen:'Kitchen',wardrobe:'Wardrobe',tv:'TV feature wall',custom:'Custom'})[c.kind]||c.kind}${c.score?' · layout '+c.score+'/100':''}</span></td>
     <td style="font-size:11.5px">${c.room}${c.wallId?'<br/><span style="color:var(--copper);cursor:pointer" onmouseenter="hlWall(\''+c.wallId+'\')" onmouseleave="hlWall(null)">wall '+c.wallId+' ◎</span>':'<br/><span style="color:var(--mute)">freestanding</span>'}</td><td class="num">${c.run}×${c.height}×${c.depth}</td>
     <td style="font-size:11px"><span style="display:inline-block;width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:2px;background:${c.fill};border:1px solid rgba(0,0,0,.15);vertical-align:-1px;margin-right:4px"></span>${c.finish}<br/><span style="color:var(--mute)">${c.carcass}${c.top?' · '+c.top+' top':''}</span></td>
     <td style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--mute);max-width:220px">${c.modules.map(m=>m.label).join(', ')}</td><td class="num">${sgd(c.cost)}</td>
     <td style="white-space:nowrap"><button class="sm" onclick="pickItem('${c.itemId}')">locate</button> <button class="sm" onclick="editCarp('${c.id}')">edit</button> <button class="sm danger" onclick="removeCarp('${c.id}')">remove</button></td></tr>`).join('')}
   <tr><td></td><td><b>Total carpentry</b> · ${C.length} piece${C.length===1?'':'s'}</td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td class="num"><b>${sgd(tot)}</b></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div>`;}
function carpLauncher(r){const sd=roomSides(r);
  const walls=r.walls.map(id=>STATE.walls.find(w=>w.id===id)).filter(Boolean).map(w=>{const B=r.bbox;const side=w.axis==='x'?(w.fixed===B.y0?'N':(w.fixed===B.y1?'S':'')):(w.fixed===B.x0?'W':(w.fixed===B.x1?'E':''));
    return {w,side,clear:clearLen(w),door:w.opens.some(o=>o.kind!=='window'&&o.state!=='removed'),win:w.opens.some(o=>o.kind==='window'&&o.state!=='removed')};}).sort((a,b)=>b.clear-a.clear);
  return `<div style="border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px;margin-top:12px;background:var(--white)">
   <div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:6px"><b style="font-weight:500;font-size:12.5px">Carpentry for this room</b><span style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--mute)">${STATE.carp.filter(c=>c.roomId===r.id).length} designed</span></div>
   <div class="row" style="gap:6px;margin-bottom:6px"><span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--mute)">on wall</span><select id="carp-wall" style="width:auto" onchange="hlWall(this.value)" onfocus="hlWall(this.value)" onblur="hlWall(null)">${walls.map(x=>`<option value="${x.w.id}">${x.w.id} · ${x.side} · ${mm(x.clear)} clear${x.door?' · has door':''}${x.win?' · window':''}</option>`).join('')}<option value="">freestanding</option></select></div>
   <div class="tool">${[['wardrobe','Wardrobe'],['kitchen','Kitchen run'],['tv','TV feature wall'],['custom','Custom piece']].map(([k,l])=>`<button class="sm pri" onclick="openCarpentry('${r.id}','${k}',document.getElementById('carp-wall').value)">+ ${l}</button>`).join('')}</div>
   <p class="note" style="margin-top:6px">Opens SPACE Carpentry with this wall's length pre-filled. Save there and the piece is placed here as a built-in, itemised in the carpentry schedule.</p></div>`;}

/* ===================== ACCOUNTS + PROJECTS (prototype: stored in this browser) ===================== */
const STORE_KEY='space.studio.v1';
function store(){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORE_KEY)||'{}');}catch(e){return {};}}
function storeSet(o){try{localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY,JSON.stringify(o));}catch(e){}}
function projectData(){return {locked:STATE.locked,file:STATE.file,kind:STATE.kind,walls:STATE.walls,items:STATE.items,progAt:STATE.progAt,floorAt:STATE.floorAt,log:STATE.log,carp:STATE.carp,theme:STATE.theme,paint:STATE.paint||null,rev:STATE.rev,itemN};}
let saveT=null;
function autosave(){if(!STATE.project||!STATE.user)return;clearTimeout(saveT);saveT=setTimeout(saveProject,600);}
function saveProject(){if(!STATE.project||!STATE.user)return;const o=store();o.projects=o.projects||[];
  const i=o.projects.findIndex(p=>p.id===STATE.project.id);const rec={...STATE.project,owner:STATE.user.email,updated:new Date().toISOString(),data:projectData()};
  if(i>=0)o.projects[i]=rec;else o.projects.push(rec);storeSet(o);STATE.project.updated=rec.updated;const c=document.getElementById('projchip');if(c)renderChip();}
function renderChip(){const c=document.getElementById('projchip');if(!c)return;
  c.innerHTML=STATE.user?`<i>${(STATE.user.name||'?').slice(0,2).toUpperCase()}</i><span class="who"><span>${STATE.user.name}</span><small>${STATE.project?STATE.project.name+(STATE.project.updated?' · saved '+new Date(STATE.project.updated).toLocaleTimeString('en-SG',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):''):'no project open'}</small></span>
   <button class="sm" onclick="showProjects()">Projects</button><button class="sm" onclick="saveProject();toast('Project saved')" ${STATE.project?'':'disabled'}>Save</button><button class="sm" onclick="signOut()" title="Sign out">⎋</button>`
   :`<button class="pri sm" onclick="showGate('login')">Sign in</button>`;
  const rf=document.getElementById('railuser');if(rf)rf.textContent=STATE.user?'Signed in · '+STATE.user.name+' ('+STATE.user.role+')':'Not signed in';}
function signIn(){const name=(document.getElementById('li-name')||{}).value||'',email=((document.getElementById('li-email')||{}).value||'').trim().toLowerCase(),role=(document.getElementById('li-role')||{}).value||'designer';
  if(!name||!email){toast('Name and e-mail, please');return;}
  STATE.user={name,email,role};const o=store();o.users=o.users||{};o.users[email]={name,role,last:new Date().toISOString()};o.session=email;storeSet(o);showProjects();}
function signOut(){const o=store();delete o.session;storeSet(o);STATE.user=null;STATE.project=null;showGate('login');}
function myProjects(){const o=store();return (o.projects||[]).filter(p=>p.owner===STATE.user.email).sort((a,b)=>(b.updated||'').localeCompare(a.updated||''));}
function showGate(view){const g=document.getElementById('gate');g.classList.add('show');
  const hero=`<div class="hero"><img src="${document.getElementById('mark').src}" alt=""/><div><b>SPACE</b><span>Space planning &amp; design · Singapore</span></div></div>`;
  if(view==='login'){g.innerHTML=`<div class="login">
    <div class="side"><div><img src="${document.getElementById('mark').src}" alt=""/><b>SPACE</b><span class="tag">Space planning &amp; design · Singapore</span>
      <h2>One home, one project: walls, rooms, furnishings and every carpentry piece, itemised.</h2>
      <ul><li>Upload the floor plan, edit walls with a change register the client can see.</li><li>Plan the space with real-size furniture and a live 3D that follows.</li><li>Design built-in carpentry in the SPACE Carpentry plug-in and pull it straight into the plan.</li><li>Export a dimensioned plan pack, or DXF / OBJ for AutoCAD and SketchUp.</li></ul></div>
      <div class="foot">Exclusive access for interior designers and their clients. Prototype build: accounts are kept in this browser.</div></div>
    <div class="form"><div class="box"><h1>Sign in to SPACE</h1><p class="sub">Your projects are private to your account.</p>
      <label for="li-email">Work e-mail</label><input type="email" id="li-email" value="studio@birunidesign.sg" autocomplete="email" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter')signIn()"/>
      <label for="li-pass">Password</label><input type="password" id="li-pass" value="••••••••" autocomplete="current-password" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter')signIn()"/>
      <label for="li-name">Display name</label><input type="text" id="li-name" value="Biruni Design" autocomplete="name"/>
      <label for="li-role">I am a</label><select id="li-role"><option value="designer">Interior designer</option><option value="client">Client: view &amp; comment</option><option value="carpenter">Carpenter: schedules only</option></select>
      <button class="pri big" onclick="signIn()">Sign in →</button>
      <p class="alt">No account? <a href="#" onclick="document.getElementById('li-name').focus();return false">Enter your details above</a>: a first sign-in creates it. Or <a href="#" onclick="document.getElementById('li-email').value='demo@space.sg';document.getElementById('li-name').value='Demo designer';signIn();return false">try the demo account</a>.</p>
    </div></div></div>`;return;}
  const P=myProjects();
  g.innerHTML=`<div class="wrap">${hero}
   <div class="grid g2" style="grid-template-columns:1fr 1.2fr">
    <div class="card"><h3>New project</h3><p class="sub">one home = one project · all its walls, rooms, furnishings and carpentry pieces in one place</p>
     <label for="np-name">Project name</label><input type="text" id="np-name" placeholder="e.g. Tan family · Tampines 4-room"/>
     <label for="np-client">Client</label><input type="text" id="np-client" placeholder="client name"/>
     <label for="np-start">Start from</label><select id="np-start">${Object.entries(SAMPLES).map(([k,v])=>`<option value="${k}">${v.label}</option>`).join('')}<option value="">Blank: I will upload the floor plan</option></select>
     <div class="row" style="margin:18px 0 0"><button class="pri" onclick="newProject()">Create project →</button></div></div>
    <div class="card"><h3>Your projects</h3><p class="sub">Signed in as <b style="color:var(--ink);font-weight:500">${STATE.user.name}</b> · ${STATE.user.email} · <a href="#" onclick="signOut();return false" style="color:var(--copper)">sign out</a></p>
     ${P.length?`<div style="display:grid;gap:10px">${P.map(p=>`<div class="proj" onclick="openProject('${p.id}')"><div><b>${p.name}</b><span>${p.client?p.client+' · ':''}${p.data&&p.data.file?p.data.file:'no plan yet'} · ${p.data?p.data.carp.length+' carpentry · '+p.data.items.length+' pieces · rev '+p.data.rev:''}</span></div><span style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--mute);white-space:nowrap">${p.updated?new Date(p.updated).toLocaleDateString('en-SG',{day:'numeric',month:'short'}):''} <button class="sm danger" onclick="event.stopPropagation();deleteProject('${p.id}')">delete</button></span></div>`).join('')}</div>`
      :'<p class="note" style="margin-top:0">No projects yet: create the first one on the left.</p>'}
     ${STATE.project?`<div class="row" style="margin-top:14px;margin-bottom:0"><button onclick="hideGate()">← Back to ${STATE.project.name}</button></div>`:''}</div></div></div>`;}
function hideGate(){document.getElementById('gate').classList.remove('show');renderChip();}
function showProjects(){showGate('projects');}
function newProject(){const name=(document.getElementById('np-name').value||'').trim()||'Untitled project',client=document.getElementById('np-client').value||'',start=document.getElementById('np-start').value;
  STATE.project={id:'P'+Date.now().toString(36),name,client,created:new Date().toISOString()};
  if(start)loadSample(start);else{STATE.file=null;STATE.walls=[];STATE.items=[];STATE.carp=[];STATE.progAt=[];STATE.floorAt=[];STATE.log=[];STATE.rev=0;STATE.M=null;STATE.cur=0;STATE.maxSeen=0;render();}
  saveProject();hideGate();toast('Project created: '+name);}
function openProject(id){const p=myProjects().find(p=>p.id===id);if(!p)return;STATE.project={id:p.id,name:p.name,client:p.client,created:p.created,updated:p.updated};
  const d=p.data||{};STATE.file=d.file||null;STATE.kind=d.kind||'HDB';STATE.walls=d.walls||[];STATE.items=d.items||[];STATE.progAt=d.progAt||[];STATE.floorAt=d.floorAt||[];STATE.carp=d.carp||[];STATE.theme=d.theme||'japandi';STATE.paint=d.paint||null;
  STATE.log=(d.log||[]).map(l=>({...l,ts:new Date(l.ts)}));STATE.rev=d.rev||0;itemN=d.itemN||STATE.items.length;STATE.hist=[];STATE.selItem=null;STATE.selRoom=null;STATE.selWall=null;
  STATE.locked=!!(d.locked||(STATE.walls.length&&(d.rev>0||(d.log||[]).length)));STATE.sub='walls';STATE.maxSeen=STATE.walls.length?(STATE.locked?3:1):0;STATE.cur=STATE.walls.length?(STATE.locked?3:1):0;
  if(STATE.walls.length){const R=detectRooms(STATE.walls);recompute();}else{STATE.M=null;render();}
  hideGate();toast('Opened '+p.name);}
function deleteProject(id){const o=store();o.projects=(o.projects||[]).filter(p=>p.id!==id);storeSet(o);if(STATE.project&&STATE.project.id===id)STATE.project=null;showProjects();}
function bootAccounts(){const o=store();if(o.session&&o.users&&o.users[o.session]){STATE.user={email:o.session,...o.users[o.session]};showProjects();}else showGate('login');}

/* ===================== SCREENS ===================== */
const STAGES=[
 {k:'upload',n:'Upload',d:'floor plan in',t:'Upload the floor plan',s:'PDF, image or screenshot from the client, plus a theme'},
 {k:'read',n:'Read',d:'walls · openings · rooms · confidence',t:'SPACE reads the plan',s:'walls, openings, rooms and scale, each with a confidence score: a proposal, not yet the model'},
 {k:'confirm',n:'Confirm',d:'check · fix · lock revision 0',t:'Confirm and lock',s:'confirm the scale and each room, then lock the proposal as revision 0'},
 {k:'design',n:'Design',d:'walls · space · carpentry · finishes',t:'Design on the locked model',s:'walls and doors, then furniture, built-ins and flooring: every change registered'},
 {k:'render',n:'Render',d:'house style · standard views',t:'Renders by house style',s:'one house style from the SPACE library, the same standard views of the home'},
 {k:'report',n:'Report',d:'plan pack · deck · programme',t:'Report and pack',s:'the dimensioned pack, the programme and the exports, from this exact revision'}];
const SCREEN={};
const changeTable=(n)=>{const L=[...STATE.log].reverse().slice(0,n||8);
  if(!L.length)return '<p class="note" style="margin-top:4px">No changes yet: the plan is exactly as uploaded. Every edit lands here with before / after.</p>';
  return `<table><thead><tr><th>#</th><th>Action</th><th>Target</th><th>Before → After</th></tr></thead><tbody>${L.map(l=>`<tr><td class="num">${l.n}</td>
   <td><span class="tag ${l.action.startsWith('Demolish')||l.action.startsWith('Seal')||l.action.startsWith('Remove')?'t-dem':(l.action.startsWith('Refused')?'t-warn':'t-new')}">${l.action}</span></td>
   <td class="chg">${l.target}</td><td style="font-size:11px">${l.before} <span style="color:var(--copper)">→</span> ${l.after}${l.detail?`<br/><span style="color:var(--mute)">${l.detail}</span>`:''}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>`;};

 <div class="grid g2">
  <div class="card"><h3>Client floor plan</h3><p class="sub">what the client has: HDB floor plan, developer brochure, or a site sketch</p>

   ${STATE.pending?`<p class="note" style="color:var(--moss)"><b>Received:</b> ${STATE.pending}. In v0.1 the plan is traced into walls by the studio (1 working day); the client sees it in Edit once it is ready. Load a sample below to walk the flow now.</p>`:''}
   <p class="note" style="margin-top:14px"><b>Or start from a Singapore sample:</b></p>
   <div class="row">${Object.entries(SAMPLES).map(([k,s])=>`<div class="selcard ${STATE.file===s.label?'on':''}" onclick="loadSample('${k}')"><b>${s.label}</b><span>${s.kind==='HDB'?'HDB flat: hacking needs an HDB permit; exterior walls are structural':'private condominium: MCST approval, renovation deposit'}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>
   ${STATE.file?`<p class="note" style="color:var(--moss)"><b>Loaded:</b> ${STATE.file}</p>`:''}

   <div class="explain" style="margin-top:16px;margin-bottom:0"><b>One model, every screen.</b> The traced walls become a single model. Edits, rooms, furnishings, renders and the timeline all read from it: change a wall in Edit and the room areas, flooring cost, render views and programme all update. Nothing is typed twice.</div>
  </div>
 </div>`;


function readStats(){const M=STATE.M;if(!M)return null;const walls=STATE.walls.length,opens=STATE.walls.reduce((a,w)=>a+(w.opens||[]).length,0),rooms=M.rooms.length;const low=STATE.walls.filter((w,i)=>i%7===3).map(w=>w.id);return {walls,opens,rooms,conf:STATE.readConf||92,low};}
SCREEN.read=()=>{const st=readStats();if(!st)return '<p class="note">Load a plan in stage 1 first.</p>';
 return `<div class="grid g2">
  <div class="card" id="readcard"><h3>What SPACE read</h3><p class="sub">drawn over the plan: a proposal, nothing is the model until you lock it in stage 3</p>
   <div class="plan-wrap">${planSVG({mode:'changes',clickable:false,walltags:true})}</div></div>
  <div class="card"><h3>The reading</h3><p class="sub">every element carries a score; below 80 percent overall SPACE reads again, up to three passes</p>
   <div class="kv"><span>Scale</span><b>${STATE.kind==='HDB'?'5.0 mm / px · from an 800 mm door':'from the dimension text'}</b></div>
   <div class="kv"><span>Walls detected</span><b>${st.walls}</b></div>
   <div class="kv"><span>Openings detected</span><b>${st.opens}</b></div>
   <div class="kv"><span>Rooms identified</span><b>${st.rooms}</b></div>
   <div class="kv"><span>Overall confidence</span><b style="color:#6E7F63">${st.conf}%</b></div>
   <div class="kv"><span>To check in stage 3</span><b>${st.low.length?st.low.join(', '):'nothing flagged'}</b></div>
   <div class="explain" style="margin-top:14px"><b>Refuse, never guess.</b> A region that cannot be closed into a room comes back open with the gap marked, and SPACE hands it to you rather than inventing a wall.</div>
   <p class="note">In this prototype the Singapore samples arrive pre-read; a file you upload is read by the studio within one working day.</p>
   <div class="row" style="margin-top:12px"><button class="pri" onclick="go(2)">Check and confirm →</button></div>
  </div></div>`;};
SCREEN.confirm=()=>{const M=STATE.M;if(!M)return '<p class="note">Load a plan in stage 1 first.</p>';const st=readStats();
 return `<div class="grid g2">
  <div class="card"><h3>Confirm the reading</h3><p class="sub">accept or fix each item, confirm the rooms, then lock</p>
   <div class="kv"><span>Scale</span><b>${STATE.kind==='HDB'?'800 mm door confirmed':'dimension text confirmed'}</b></div>
   <div class="kv"><span>Low-confidence walls</span><b>${st.low.length?st.low.join(', ')+' · accepted':'none'}</b></div>
   ${M.rooms.map(r=>`<div class="kv"><span>${r.id||''} ${r.program}</span><b>${(r.area/1e6).toFixed(1)} m² · ${r.suggested?'suggested':'confirmed'}</b></div>`).join('')}
   <div class="explain" style="margin-top:14px"><b>Only Lock creates revision 0.</b> Edits go back through the reader (rooms must close, doors must sit on walls), and every round is recorded. Room uses can still be changed in Design.</div>
   <div class="row" style="margin-top:14px"><button id="lockBtn" class="pri" onclick="lockPlan()">${STATE.locked?'Locked as revision 0 · continue →':'Lock as revision 0'}</button>${STATE.locked?'':'<button onclick="go(1)">Back to the reading</button>'}</div>
  </div>
  <div class="card"><h3>Plan with the reading</h3><p class="sub">${STATE.locked?'revision 0, locked':'proposal, not yet locked'}</p>
   <div class="plan-wrap">${planSVG({mode:'changes',clickable:false,walltags:true})}</div></div>
 </div>`;};
function lockPlan(silent){if(!STATE.M)return;if(!STATE.locked){STATE.locked=true;logIt('Lock','plan','proposal','revision 0','scale and rooms confirmed');try{saveProject();}catch(e){}if(!silent)toast('Locked as revision 0');}go(3);}
function setSub(k){STATE.sub=k;render();}
SCREEN.design=()=>`<div class="subtabs2" role="tablist">${[['walls','1','Walls and doors','Move, knock down or add walls, doors and windows. Every change is logged.',ICON_WALLS],['space','2','Space, furniture and carpentry','Place furniture, built-ins and flooring, and see the space you have left.',ICON_SPACE]].map(([k,n,t,d,ic])=>{const on=(k==='space')===(STATE.sub==='space');return `<button class="st2 ${on?'on':''}" role="tab" aria-selected="${on}" onclick="setSub('${k}')"><span class="ic">${ic}</span><span class="tx"><small>Step ${n} of 2</small><b>${t}</b><span>${d}</span></span><span class="go">${on?'You are here':'Open'} ${on?'':'&rarr;'}</span></button>`;}).join('')}</div>`+(STATE.sub==='space'?SCREEN.review():SCREEN.edit());

SCREEN.edit=()=>{
  const M=STATE.M,w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===STATE.selWall);
  const wallPanel=w?`
   <div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:6px"><b style="font-size:15px;font-weight:500">${w.id} <span style="font-weight:400;font-size:11px;color:var(--mute)">${w.axis==='x'?'horizontal':'vertical'} · ${(()=>{const rs=STATE.M.rooms.filter(r=>r.walls.includes(w.id)).map(r=>r.program);return rs.length?'between '+rs.join(' and '):'perimeter';})()}</span> <span class="tag ${w.state==='new'?'t-new':(w.state==='demolished'?'t-dem':'t-ex')}">${w.state}</span> ${w.ext?'<span class="tag t-warn">exterior · structural</span>':''}</b><button class="sm" onclick="STATE.selWall=null;render()">close</button></div>
   ${[['Length',mm(wlen(w))],['Thickness',w.t+' mm'],['Height',wH(w)+' mm'],['Openings',w.opens.filter(o=>o.state!=='removed').length||'none']].map(([k,v])=>`<div class="kv"><span>${k}</span><b>${v}</b></div>`).join('')}
   <p class="note" style="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:6px"><b>Wall type</b></p>
   <div class="tool">${[['full','Full wall'],['half','Half wall 1.1 m'],['glass','Glass partition']].map(([k,l])=>`<button class="${w.type===k?'on':''}" onclick="setType('${w.id}','${k}')" ${w.state==='demolished'?'disabled':''}>${l}</button>`).join('')}</div>
   <p class="note" style="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:6px"><b>Openings</b></p>
   <div class="tool"><button onclick="addOpen('${w.id}','door')" ${w.state==='demolished'?'disabled':''}>+ Door</button><button onclick="addOpen('${w.id}','opening')" ${w.state==='demolished'?'disabled':''}>+ Opening</button><button onclick="addOpen('${w.id}','window')" ${w.state==='demolished'?'disabled':''}>+ Window</button></div>
   ${w.opens.length?`<table style="margin-top:8px"><tbody>${w.opens.map(o=>`<tr><td style="white-space:nowrap"><b>${o.id}</b> <span class="tag ${o.state==='new'?'t-new':(o.state==='removed'?'t-dem':'t-ex')}">${o.state==='removed'?'sealed':o.state}</span></td>
     <td><select onchange="setOpenKind('${w.id}','${o.id}',this.value)" style="width:auto">${['door','opening','window'].map(k=>`<option ${o.kind===k?'selected':''}>${k}</option>`).join('')}</select>
      <select onchange="setOpenWidth('${w.id}','${o.id}',this.value)" style="width:auto">${[700,750,800,900,1000,1200,1500,1800,2400].map(v=>`<option value="${v}" ${Math.abs((o.b-o.a)-v)<60?'selected':''}>${v}</option>`).join('')}</select></td>
     <td style="text-align:right;white-space:nowrap">${o.kind==='door'&&o.state!=='removed'?`<button class="sm" title="flip which way it opens" onclick="flipDoor('${w.id}','${o.id}','swing')">↔ swing</button> <button class="sm" title="hinge on the other jamb" onclick="flipDoor('${w.id}','${o.id}','hinge')">hinge</button> `:''}${o.state==='removed'?`<button class="sm" onclick="restoreOpen('${w.id}','${o.id}')">restore</button>`:`<button class="sm danger" onclick="removeOpen('${w.id}','${o.id}')">${o.state==='new'?'remove':'seal'}</button>`}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>`:''}
   <div class="row" style="margin-top:14px;margin-bottom:0">${w.state==='demolished'?`<button class="pri" onclick="restore('${w.id}')">Restore wall</button>`:`<button class="danger" onclick="demolish('${w.id}')">${w.state==='new'?'Remove this new wall':'Demolish wall'}</button>`}</div>
   ${w.ext?'<p class="note">Exterior and structural walls are shown but cannot be hacked: any attempt is recorded as <b>refused</b> in the change register, so the evidence trail is complete.</p>':''}`
  :`<p class="note" style="margin-top:0">Click any wall on the plan to see it here. Use <b>Add wall</b> to draw a new partition: two clicks, snapped to a 100 mm grid.</p>
    <div class="explain" style="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0"><b>Change register.</b> Every add, demolish, type change, door or window: and every refusal: is written down with a before and after. That is the evidence of what the current plan is versus what changes.</div>`;
  return `<div class="stats">
    <div class="stat"><b>${M.live.length}</b><span>walls</span></div><div class="stat"><b>${M.rooms.length}</b><span>rooms</span></div>
    <div class="stat"><b style="color:var(--brick)">${M.dem.length}</b><span>to demolish</span></div><div class="stat"><b style="color:var(--oak2)">${M.neu.length}</b><span>new walls</span></div>
    <div class="stat"><b>${M.half+M.glass}</b><span>half / glass</span></div><div class="stat"><b>${STATE.log.length}</b><span>changes logged</span></div></div>
   <div class="grid g2">
    <div class="card"><h3>Plan: ${STATE.view==='existing'?'as uploaded':(STATE.view==='proposed'?'proposed':'changes overlay')}</h3><p class="sub">${STATE.file}</p>
     <div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between">
      <div class="tool">${[['existing','Existing'],['proposed','Proposed'],['changes','Changes']].map(([k,l])=>`<button class="${STATE.view===k?'on':''}" onclick="setView('${k}')">${l}</button>`).join('')}</div>
      <div class="tool"><button class="${STATE.tool==='select'?'on':''}" onclick="setTool('select')">Select</button><button class="${STATE.tool==='addwall'?'on':''}" onclick="setTool('addwall')">+ Add wall</button><button onclick="undo()" ${STATE.hist.length?'':'disabled'} title="Ctrl/⌘ + Z">↶ Undo</button><button onclick="redo()" ${STATE.redo.length?'':'disabled'} title="Ctrl/⌘ + Shift + Z">↷ Redo</button><button class="${STATE.showTags!==false?'on':''}" onclick="STATE.showTags=STATE.showTags===false;render()">Wall IDs</button></div></div>
     <div class="plan-wrap ${STATE.tool==='addwall'?'addmode':''}" id="plan-edit">${planSVG({mode:STATE.view,clickable:true,walltags:STATE.showTags!==false})}</div>
     <div class="lg"><span><i style="background:#373837"></i>existing</span><span><i style="background:#B99A81"></i>new</span><span><i style="background:#A8503B"></i>demolish</span><span><i style="background:#D2CDCA"></i>half wall</span><span><i style="background:#B9CDD3"></i>glass</span><span><i style="background:#9C7B5B"></i>door</span><span><i style="background:#7A98A3"></i>window</span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="card"><h3>${w?'Selected wall':'Tools'}</h3><p class="sub">${w?'changes apply instantly and are registered':'select a wall, or draw one'}</p>${wallPanel}</div>
   </div>
   <div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:18px">
    <div class="card"><h3>Walkthrough check</h3><p class="sub">every room reachable from the entrance · doors swing into the room they serve · standard leaf sizes</p>
     ${(()=>{const wk=M.walk;const bad=wk.issues.filter(i=>i.sev==='bad'),warn=wk.issues.filter(i=>i.sev==='warn');
       return `<div class="stats" style="margin-bottom:12px"><div class="stat"><b>${M.rooms.filter(r=>wk.reach.has(r.id)).length}/${M.rooms.length}</b><span>rooms reachable</span></div><div class="stat"><b>${wk.doors}</b><span>doors + openings</span></div><div class="stat"><b style="color:${bad.length?'var(--brick)':(warn.length?'#9A6A2E':'var(--moss)')}">${bad.length?bad.length+' blocking':(warn.length?warn.length+' to check':'clear')}</b><span>verdict</span></div></div>
        ${wk.issues.length?`<ul style="list-style:none">${wk.issues.map(i=>`<li style="font-size:11.5px;padding:3px 0;color:${i.sev==='bad'?'var(--brick)':'#9A6A2E'};cursor:pointer" onmouseenter="${i.id&&i.id.startsWith('O')?`hlDoor('${i.id}')`:''}" onmouseleave="hlWall(null)">${i.sev==='bad'?'✕':'!'} ${i.msg}</li>`).join('')}</ul>`:'<p class="note" style="margin-top:0;color:var(--moss)">Walkthrough is logical: every room has a door, all reachable from the entrance, no swings collide.</p>'}
        <div class="row" style="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0"><button class="pri sm" onclick="autoFixDoors()">Apply door logic</button><span class="note" style="margin:0">re-orients every door into the room it serves, hinge on the corner side; doors wider than 1.1 m become cased openings</span></div>`;})()}
    </div>
    <div class="card"><h3>Change register</h3><p class="sub">newest first · rev ${STATE.rev} · <a href="#" onclick="exportLog();return false" style="color:var(--copper)">export CSV</a></p>${changeTable(8)}</div>
   </div>
   <div class="card" style="margin-top:18px"><h3>3D wall setup</h3><p class="sub">real heights: half walls at 1.1 m, glass translucent, demolished walls as a dashed footprint · rotate or export for SketchUp / Blender</p>
    ${isoControls('iso-edit',{maxh:420})}<div id="iso-edit">${iso3D({maxh:420})}</div></div>`;
};

SCREEN.review=()=>{
  const M=STATE.M;
  if(!STATE.selRoom||!M.rooms.find(r=>r.id===STATE.selRoom))STATE.selRoom=M.rooms[0]&&M.rooms[0].id;
  const r=M.rooms.find(x=>x.id===STATE.selRoom);
  const it=STATE.items.find(i=>i.id===STATE.selItem);
  const catalogue=r?Object.entries(FURN).filter(([k,f])=>f.rooms.includes(r.program)):[];
  const others=r?Object.entries(FURN).filter(([k,f])=>!f.rooms.includes(r.program)):[];
  const panel=r?`
   <div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:8px"><b style="font-size:15px;font-weight:500">${r.id} · ${r.program} ${r.suggested?'<span class="tag t-sug">suggested: confirm</span>':'<span class="tag t-ok">confirmed</span>'}</b><span class="cur" style="font-size:12px;color:var(--mute)">${m2(r.area)} · ${Math.round(r.sqft)} sq ft</span></div>
   <div class="kv"><span>Room is</span><b><select id="prog-${r.id}" onchange="setProgram('${r.id}',this.value)">${PROGRAMS.map(p=>`<option ${p===r.program?'selected':''}>${p}</option>`).join('')}</select></b></div>
   <div class="kv"><span>Flooring</span><b><select id="floor-${r.id}" onchange="setFloor('${r.id}',this.value)">${Object.entries(FLOORS).map(([k,f])=>`<option value="${k}" ${k===r.floor?'selected':''}>${f.n}${f.psf?' · S$'+f.psf+'/sq ft':''}</option>`).join('')}</select></b></div>
   <div class="kv"><span>Flooring estimate</span><b>${r.floor==='keep'?'-':sgd(r.floorCost)}</b></div>
   ${r.floorWarn?'<p class="note" style="color:var(--brick)">Vinyl / laminate is not suitable in a wet area: choose tiles for this room.</p>':''}
   <div style="background:var(--tint);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px;margin-top:12px" id="space-live">${spaceCard(r)}</div>
   <div class="row" style="margin:12px 0 8px"><button class="pri sm" onclick="applySet('${r.id}')" ${SETS[r.program]?'':'disabled'}>Place standard ${r.program.toLowerCase()} set</button>${r.items.length?`<button class="sm" onclick="clearRoom('${r.id}')">Clear room</button>`:''}</div>
   ${carpLauncher(r)}
   ${r.items.length?`<p class="note" style="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:6px"><b>In the room</b>: click one on the plan to move it</p>
    <table><tbody>${r.items.map(i=>`<tr class="${STATE.selItem===i.id?'sel':''}" style="cursor:pointer" onclick="pickItem('${i.id}')"><td>${nameOf(i)}</td><td>${i.carp?'<span class="tag t-new">carpentry</span>':(isBI(i)?'<span class="tag t-new">built-in</span>':'<span class="tag t-ex">loose</span>')}</td><td class="num">${dims(i).w}×${dims(i).d}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table>`:''}
   ${it?`<div style="border:1px solid var(--oak);border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px;margin-top:10px;background:#FBF7F3">
     <div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between;margin-bottom:6px"><b style="font-weight:500">${nameOf(it)}</b><span style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--mute)">${isBI(it)?'built-in':'loose'} · ${it.rot?'rotated':'as drawn'}</span></div>
     <div class="row" style="margin-bottom:8px;gap:6px"><span style="font-size:11px;color:var(--mute)">size mm</span>
      ${[['w','W'],['d','D'],['h','H']].map(([k,l])=>`<label style="font-size:10.5px;color:var(--mute)">${l} <input type="number" id="sz-${k}-${it.id}" value="${dims(it)[k]}" step="50" min="100" max="6000" style="width:68px" onchange="setItemSize('${k}',this.value)"/></label>`).join('')}</div>
     <div class="row" style="margin-bottom:0;gap:6px"><button class="sm" onclick="nudge(-100,0)">←</button><button class="sm" onclick="nudge(100,0)">→</button><button class="sm" onclick="nudge(0,-100)">↑</button><button class="sm" onclick="nudge(0,100)">↓</button><button class="sm" onclick="rotItem()">rotate</button><button class="sm danger" onclick="removeItem()">remove</button></div></div>`:''}`
  :'<p class="note">No enclosed rooms found: check the Edit stage.</p>';
  return `<div class="stats">
    <div class="stat"><b>${M.rooms.length}</b><span>rooms</span></div><div class="stat"><b>${(M.area/1e6).toFixed(0)}</b><span>m² floor area</span></div>
    <div class="stat"><b>${M.rooms.filter(r=>r.suggested).length}</b><span>to confirm</span></div><div class="stat"><b>${STATE.items.length}</b><span>pieces placed</span></div>
    <div class="stat"><b style="color:var(--oak2)">${STATE.carp.length}</b><span>carpentry designs</span></div><div class="stat"><b>${sgd(M.carpCost)}</b><span>carpentry est.</span></div><div class="stat"><b>${sgd(M.floorCost)}</b><span>flooring est.</span></div></div>
   <div class="grid g2">
    <div class="card"><h3>Space plan: click a room</h3><p class="sub">rooms detected from the proposed walls · furnishings drawn to scale · flooring shown by material</p>
     <div class="plan-wrap" id="plan-live">${planSVG({mode:'proposed',furn:true,rooms:true,floorCol:true,space:true,walltags:r?r.walls:[]})}</div>
     <div class="lg"><span><i style="background:#EFEBE6;border:1px solid #8C8A86"></i>loose</span><span><i style="background:#D9C4AE"></i>built-in</span><span><i style="background:#F1E6DB;border:1px dashed #B99A81"></i>clearance needed</span><span><i style="background:#F3EDE5"></i>door swing</span><span><i style="background:#fff;border:2px solid #A8503B"></i>conflict</span></div>
     <div class="row" style="margin-top:10px;margin-bottom:0;justify-content:space-between"><span class="note" style="margin:0">Drag any piece to move it: the 3D follows. Arrow keys nudge, R rotates, Delete removes. "?" = program suggested, not yet confirmed.</span>${M.rooms.some(r=>r.suggested)?'<button class="sm pri" onclick="confirmAll()">Confirm all suggestions</button>':''}</div>
    </div>
    <div class="card"><h3>Room</h3><p class="sub">program · flooring · space read · pieces in the room</p>${panel}</div>
   </div>
   ${r?`<div class="card" style="margin-top:18px"><h3>Furniture &amp; fixtures: add to ${r.program}</h3><p class="sub">standard Singapore sizes · click to place in the selected room, then drag · sizes editable per piece</p>
    ${[['Seating',['sofa','sofa2','sofaL','side','coffee']],['Beds',['bedk','bedq','bedss','beds','bedside']],['Tables & desks',['dining6','dining4','desk']],['Storage & built-in carpentry',['ward','ward24','dresser','shelf','shoe','tv']],['Kitchen',['kcab','island','fridge']],['Bath & yard',['vanity','washer']]].map(([g,ks])=>`<div class="row" style="margin-bottom:8px;align-items:flex-start"><span style="font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--copper);min-width:150px;padding-top:6px">${g}</span><div class="tool">${ks.map(k=>`<button class="sm ${FURN[k].rooms.includes(r.program)?'':''}" style="${FURN[k].rooms.includes(r.program)?'border-color:var(--oak)':''}" onclick="addItem('${r.id}','${k}')" title="${FURN[k].w}×${FURN[k].d}×${FURN[k].h} mm">+ ${FURN[k].n}${FURN[k].bi?' <span style="color:var(--copper)">·bi</span>':''}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`).join('')}
    <div class="row" style="margin-top:4px;margin-bottom:0;align-items:center;gap:8px"><span style="font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--copper);min-width:150px">Custom piece</span>
     <input type="text" id="cu-n" placeholder="name (e.g. piano)" style="width:150px"/><label style="font-size:11px;color:var(--mute)">W <input type="number" id="cu-w" value="1000" step="50" style="width:70px"/></label><label style="font-size:11px;color:var(--mute)">D <input type="number" id="cu-d" value="500" step="50" style="width:70px"/></label><label style="font-size:11px;color:var(--mute)">H <input type="number" id="cu-h" value="800" step="50" style="width:70px"/></label><label style="font-size:11px;color:var(--mute)"><input type="checkbox" id="cu-bi"/> built-in</label><button class="sm pri" onclick="addCustom('${r.id}')">+ Add custom</button></div>
    ${libRow(r)}
    <p class="note">Oak-outlined pieces are the usual fit for this room type. Every piece's W × D × H can be changed once selected. Catalogue is a starter set until the product database is built up.</p></div>`:''}
   <div class="card" style="margin-top:18px"><h3>Carpentry schedule: ${STATE.project?STATE.project.name:STATE.file}</h3><p class="sub">every saved SPACE Carpentry design, itemised · ${STATE.carp.length} piece${STATE.carp.length===1?'':'s'} · ${sgd(M.carpCost)} indicative</p>${carpSchedule()}
    <div class="row" style="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0"><button class="pri sm" onclick="openPlanPack()">Print plan pack</button><button class="sm" onclick="exportPlanPack()">⬇ Plan pack (.html)</button><button class="sm" onclick="exportCAD()">⬇ AutoCAD / SketchUp (.dxf / .obj)</button><span class="note" style="margin:0">dimensioned plan · existing vs proposed · room schedule · every carpentry piece with elevation and modules</span></div>
    ${STATE.carp.length?`<div class="row" style="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap">${STATE.carp.map(c=>`<div class="carp-thumb"><div style="font-size:10.5px;margin-bottom:4px"><b style="font-weight:500">${c.id}</b> ${c.name} <span style="color:var(--mute)">· ${c.room}</span></div>${c.svg||''}</div>`).join('')}</div>`:''}</div>
   <div class="grid g2" style="margin-top:18px">
    <div class="card"><h3>Room schedule</h3><p class="sub">reads straight from the model: basic options until the catalogue is built up</p>
     <div style="overflow-x:auto"><table><thead><tr><th>Room</th><th class="num">Area</th><th class="num">Free</th><th>Space</th><th>Flooring</th><th class="num">Est.</th></tr></thead><tbody>
     ${M.rooms.map(x=>`<tr class="${x.id===STATE.selRoom?'sel':''}" style="cursor:pointer" onclick="pickRoom('${x.id}')"><td>${x.program}${x.suggested?' <span class="tag t-sug">?</span>':''}<br/><span style="font-size:10px;color:var(--mute)">${x.items.map(i=>nameOf(i).split(' ')[0]).join(', ')||'empty'}</span></td><td class="num">${m2(x.area)}</td><td class="num">${Math.round(x.space.freePct*100)}%</td><td><span class="tag ${x.space.ok?'t-ok':(x.space.issues.some(i=>i.sev==='bad')?'t-dem':'t-warn')}">${x.items.length?x.space.verdict:'empty'}</span></td><td style="font-size:11px">${FLOORS[x.floor].n}${x.floorWarn?' <span class="tag t-warn">wet</span>':''}</td><td class="num">${x.floor==='keep'?'-':sgd(x.floorCost)}</td></tr>`).join('')}
     <tr><td><b>Total</b> · ${STATE.items.length} pieces</td><td class="num"><b>${m2(M.area)}</b></td><td class="num">${Math.round(M.rooms.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.space.free,0)/M.area*100)}%</td><td></td><td></td><td class="num"><b>${sgd(M.floorCost)}</b></td></tr></tbody></table></div>
     <p class="note">Flooring estimates are indicative supply-and-install rates for Singapore (S$/sq ft): for options, not a quotation.</p></div>

   </div>`;

const prEsc=x=>String(x==null?'':x).replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'})[c]);
const PR_ROOM={Foyer:'living',Living:'living',Dining:'living',Kitchen:'kitchen','Master Bedroom':'bed','Bedroom 2':'bed','Master Bath':'bath','Common Bath':'bath'};
function photoCard(views){const ok=views.filter(v=>v.room).map(v=>v.label);const P=STATE.photo||(STATE.photo={sel:ok.slice(0,3),done:null});P.sel=P.sel.filter(l=>ok.includes(l));const n=P.sel.length;const cost=Math.min(n*8,56);
 const sid=(STATE.paint&&STATE.paint.styleId)||'japandi';const bd=(typeof STYLE_BOARD!=='undefined'&&STYLE_BOARD[sid])||['japandi','Japandi'];const R=(typeof SLIB!=='undefined'&&SLIB&&SLIB.ROOM&&SLIB.ROOM[bd[0]])||null;
 const gal=P.done&&R?`<div class="grid g3" style="margin-top:12px">${P.done.views.map(l=>`<div class="view"><div style="aspect-ratio:4/3;background:#ddd center/cover url(${R[/bath/i.test(l)?'bath':/bed/i.test(l)?'bed':/kitchen/i.test(l)?'kitchen':'living']||R.living})"></div><div class="cap"><div><b>${l}</b><br/><span>photoreal · ${prEsc(P.done.style)}</span></div><span class="tag t-new">rendered</span></div></div>`).join('')}</div>`:'';
 return `<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:18px"><h3>Photoreal renders</h3><p class="sub">pick the views, see the credits before anything runs</p>
  <div class="row" style="flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;margin:8px 0 10px">${ok.map(l=>`<label class="chip ${P.sel.includes(l)?'on':''}" style="cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:14px;padding:4px 10px;font-size:12px"><input type="checkbox" ${P.sel.includes(l)?'checked':''} onchange="prTog('${l}')">${l}</label>`).join('')}</div>
  <div class="row" style="gap:12px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap"><button class="btn primary" ${n?'':'disabled'} onclick="prRun()">Render ${n} view${n===1?'':'s'} · ${cost} credits</button><span style="font-size:12px;color:var(--mute)">8 credits a view, the full set of seven or more is capped at 56. Style: ${prEsc(bd[1])}.</span></div>${gal}</div>`;}
function prTog(l){const P=STATE.photo;const i=P.sel.indexOf(l);i<0?P.sel.push(l):P.sel.splice(i,1);render();}
function prRun(){const P=STATE.photo;const sid=(STATE.paint&&STATE.paint.styleId)||'japandi';const bd=(typeof STYLE_BOARD!=='undefined'&&STYLE_BOARD[sid])||['japandi','Japandi'];
 const go=(k)=>{P.done={views:P.sel.slice(0,k),style:bd[1]};render();toast(k+' photoreal view'+(k>1?'s':'')+' ready');};
 if(!window.SPACEU||!SPACEU.job){go(P.sel.length);return;}
 const all=P.sel.concat(VIEWS.map(v=>v[1]).filter(l=>!P.sel.includes(l)&&STATE.M.rooms.some(r=>r.program===VIEWS.find(x=>x[1]===l)[0])));SPACEU.job('render',{units:P.sel.length,max:all.length,views:all,style:bd[1]}).then(r=>{if(r.ok){P.sel=all.slice(0,r.units);go(r.units);}else toast('Render cancelled. Nothing was charged.');});}
SCREEN.render=()=>{
  const M=STATE.M,T=themeT();
  const views=VIEWS.map(([p,label])=>({label,room:M.rooms.find(r=>r.program===p)}));
  return `<div class="explain"><b>Pick a house style from the SPACE library; every standard view follows it.</b> The same rooms the client just planned are drawn in that style: walls, floors, joinery and loose pieces. Fine-tune any colour by hand below. Each card is also the brief for a photoreal render.</div>
   ${libPaintCard()}
   ${photoCard(views)}
   <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:18px"><h3>Whole home: ${T.n}</h3><p class="sub">plan and 3D in the selected theme</p>
    <div class="grid g2"><div>${planSVG({mode:'proposed',furn:true,theme:T,nolabel:false})}</div><div>${isoControls('iso-render',{theme:T,maxh:400})}<div id="iso-render">${iso3D({theme:T,maxh:400})}</div></div></div></div>
   <h3 style="font-size:10.5px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.18em;color:var(--copper);font-weight:500;margin-bottom:10px">Standard views · ${views.filter(v=>v.room).length} of ${views.length}</h3>
   <div class="grid g3">${views.map(v=>v.room?`<div class="view"><div style="padding:12px 12px 4px;background:${T.wall}">${iso3D({room:v.room.id,theme:T,maxh:220})}</div>
      <div class="cap"><div><b>${v.label}</b><br/><span>${m2(v.room.area)} · ${FLOORS[v.room.floor].n} · ${v.room.items.length} piece${v.room.items.length===1?'':'s'}</span></div><span class="tag t-new">${T.n}</span></div></div>`
     :`<div class="view" style="border-style:dashed"><div style="padding:34px 12px;text-align:center;color:var(--mute);font-size:12px">No room assigned as <b>${v.label}</b><br/><span style="font-size:11px">assign it in Review to get this view</span></div></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
};

SCREEN.report=()=>complianceCard()+`<div class="card" style="margin-bottom:18px"><h3>The pack, from revision ${STATE.rev||0}</h3><p class="sub">dimensioned plan · existing vs proposed · room schedule · every carpentry piece with elevation and modules · change register · programme</p>
   <div class="row" style="margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:0;flex-wrap:wrap"><button class="pri sm" onclick="openPlanPack()">Print plan pack</button><button class="sm" onclick="exportPlanPack()">⬇ Plan pack (.html)</button><button class="sm" onclick="exportCAD()">⬇ AutoCAD / SketchUp (.dxf / .obj)</button><span class="note" style="margin:0">STL from the 3D card in Design · IFC for Revit on the bench</span></div></div>`+SCREEN.timeline();
SCREEN.timeline=()=>{
  const M=STATE.M,tl=M.timeline;
  const lo=Math.round(tl.weeks*0.9*10)/10,hi=Math.round(tl.weeks*1.2*10)/10;
  return `<div class="stats">
    <div class="stat"><b>${lo}-${hi} wk</b><span>estimated programme</span></div><div class="stat"><b>${tl.total}</b><span>working days</span></div>
    <div class="stat"><b>${STATE.kind}</b><span>property type</span></div><div class="stat"><b style="color:var(--brick)">${M.dem.length}</b><span>walls hacked</span></div>
    <div class="stat"><b style="color:var(--oak2)">${M.builtins}</b><span>built-ins · ${STATE.carp.length} designed</span></div><div class="stat"><b>${sgd(M.carpCost+M.floorCost)}</b><span>carpentry + flooring est.</span></div></div>
   <div class="card" style="margin-bottom:18px"><h3>Programme: ${STATE.file}</h3><p class="sub">Singapore, weekdays only · derived from the exact scope in this model (rev ${STATE.rev})</p>
    <div style="overflow-x:auto">${ganttSVG(tl)}</div>
    <div class="lg"><span><i style="background:#D2CDCA"></i>approvals</span><span><i style="background:#A8503B"></i>hacking</span><span><i style="background:#B99A81"></i>on-site works</span><span><i style="background:#9C7B5B"></i>off-site fabrication (parallel)</span></div></div>
   <div class="grid g2">
    <div class="card"><h3>Phases</h3><p class="sub">what drives each duration</p>
     <table><thead><tr><th>Phase</th><th class="num">Start</th><th class="num">Days</th><th>Driver</th></tr></thead><tbody>${tl.ph.map(p=>`<tr><td>${p.name}</td><td class="num">d ${p.s}</td><td class="num">${p.dur}</td><td style="font-size:11px;color:var(--mute)">${p.note}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>
    <div class="card"><h3>Assumptions</h3><p class="sub">indicative: confirmed with the contractor before contract</p>
     ${[['Working week','Mon-Fri, HDB permitted hours 9am-6pm; no Sunday / PH works'],['HDB permit',STATE.kind==='HDB'?(M.dem.length+M.neu.length?'required: ~3 weeks before hacking can start':'not required: no hacking or new walls'):'n/a: MCST approval instead'],['Carpentry','fabricated off-site after rough-in, installed after painting'],['Flooring rate','tiles ~25 m²/day · vinyl / laminate ~60 m²/day'],['Curing','3 days after new masonry before plaster'],['Excludes','structural PE endorsement, custom lead-time items, client approval delays']].map(([k,v])=>`<div class="kv" style="align-items:flex-start"><span style="flex:none">${k}</span><b style="font-family:var(--font);font-weight:400;font-size:11.5px">${v}</b></div>`).join('')}
     <div class="row" style="margin-top:14px;margin-bottom:0"><button class="pri" onclick="openPlanPack()">Print plan pack</button><button onclick="exportCAD()">AutoCAD / SketchUp pack</button><button onclick="exportLog()">Change register (.csv)</button><button onclick="exportModel()">Model (.json)</button></div></div>
   </div>`;
};

/* ===================== SHELL ===================== */
function render(){
  const locked=!STATE.M;
  document.getElementById('stages').innerHTML=STAGES.map((s,i)=>`<div class="stage ${i===STATE.cur?'on':''} ${i<STATE.maxSeen&&i!==STATE.cur?'done':''} ${(locked&&i>0)||(!STATE.locked&&i>=3)?'lock':''}" onclick="go(${i})"><div class="n">${i+1}</div><div><h4>${s.n}</h4><p>${s.d}</p></div></div>`).join('');
  const s=STAGES[STATE.cur];
  document.getElementById('eyebrow').textContent='Stage '+(STATE.cur+1)+' of '+STAGES.length;
  document.getElementById('ttl').textContent=s.t;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent=s.s+(STATE.file?' · '+STATE.file:'');
  document.getElementById('navbtns').innerHTML=(STATE.cur>0?'<button onclick="go('+(STATE.cur-1)+')">Back</button> ':'')+(STATE.cur===3&&STATE.sub!=='space'?'<button class="pri" onclick="setSub(\'space\')">Next: space →</button>':(STATE.cur<STAGES.length-1&&!locked&&!(STATE.cur===2&&!STATE.locked)?'<button class="pri" onclick="go('+(STATE.cur+1)+')">Next →</button>':''));
  document.body.dataset.stage=s.k;document.body.dataset.sub=STATE.sub||'';
  document.getElementById('body').innerHTML=SCREEN[s.k]();renderChip();
  const dz=document.getElementById('drop');
  if(dz)['dragover','dragleave','drop'].forEach(ev=>dz.addEventListener(ev,e=>{e.preventDefault();dz.classList.toggle('over',ev==='dragover');if(ev==='drop'&&e.dataTransfer.files[0])handleFile(e.dataTransfer.files[0]);}));
}
function go(i){if(i>0&&!STATE.M)return;if(i>=3&&!STATE.locked){toast('Lock the plan first: stage 3, Confirm.');i=2;}STATE.cur=i;STATE.maxSeen=Math.max(STATE.maxSeen,i);STATE.addPt=null;render();window.scrollTo({top:0});}

/* ===================== SITE BRIDGE (when opened inside the SPACE site) ===================== */
function studioTour(){const S=[
 {el:'.rail .stages',k:'Studio',title:'Six stages, one model',text:'Upload, Read, Confirm, Design, Render, Report. Every screen reads the same model, so a wall moved in Design updates the rooms, the carpentry, the views and the report.'},
 {before:()=>go(0),el:'#drop',k:'1 · Upload',title:'Start with the plan you have',text:'Drop the client\'s floor plan, or pick a Singapore sample to try things. Pick a theme now or later; it can change at Render.',tryIt:'pick a sample under the drop zone'},
 {before:()=>go(1),el:'#readcard',k:'2 · Read',title:'SPACE reads the plan',text:'Walls, openings, rooms and the scale, each with a confidence score. Below 80 percent it reads again. What you see is a proposal; nothing downstream exists yet.'},
 {before:()=>go(2),el:'#lockBtn',k:'3 · Confirm',title:'Check, fix, lock',text:'Confirm the scale and each room, fix anything flagged, then press Lock. Only your Lock creates revision 0; every round of checking is recorded.',tryIt:'press Lock as revision 0'},
 {before:()=>{lockPlan(true);setSub('walls');},el:'[onclick^="setTool"]',k:'4 · Design',title:'Demolish, add, doors',text:'Pick a tool, then click a wall on the plan. Exterior walls are refused and the refusal is logged. Door swings set themselves and every room is proven reachable.',tryIt:'click an interior wall to demolish it, then Ctrl+Z'},
 {el:'#plan-edit',k:'4 · Design',title:'The plan, with every change on the record',text:'Wall IDs light up as you hover. Every action lands in the change register with before and after, so the client can read what changed and why.'},
 {before:()=>{setSub('space');try{const r=(STATE.M.rooms.find(r=>/master/i.test(r.program||''))||STATE.M.rooms[0]);if(r)pickRoom(r.id);}catch(e){}},el:'[onclick^="pickItem"]',k:'4 · Design',title:'Plan the space with real sizes',text:'Place furniture from the picker or drop a standard set. Drag to move, rotate, resize. The space-available read tells you the moment a clearance is lost.'},
 {el:'[onclick^="openCarpentry"]',k:'4 · Design',title:'Built-ins go through SPACE Carpentry',text:'Choose a wall and open the carpentry plug-in with the clear length pre-filled. Save, and the piece is placed flush on that wall, itemised as C-01, C-02 and priced.',tryIt:'open Carpentry for this room'},
 {el:'#iso-live',k:'4 · Design',title:'The 3D follows you',text:'Rotate it, read the volumes, export STL or OBJ later. It is drawn from the same model, so it is never out of date.'},
 {before:()=>go(4),el:'[onclick^="setTheme"]',k:'5 · Render',title:'Pick a theme, get the standard views',text:'Nine themes. Foyer, living, dining, kitchen, master, bedroom two, baths. Show the bold option and the safe one in the same session.'},
 {before:()=>go(5),el:'[onclick^="exportPlanPack"]',k:'6 · Report',title:'The report and the pack',text:'A dimensioned plan pack, the carpentry pages, the change register and a Singapore programme from this exact revision, plus DXF for AutoCAD and OBJ for SketchUp.'},
 {title:'That is the loop',text:'Plan in, reading confirmed, decisions on the record, carpentry itemised, views shown, report out. Back to the dashboard from the bar above whenever you like.',last:'Finish'}];
 SpaceTour.start(S,{onFinish:()=>{try{parent.postMessage({type:'studio:tour-done'},'*');}catch(e){}}});}
window.addEventListener('message',e=>{const d=e.data||{};
 if(d.type==='space:auth'){const u=d.user||{};STATE.user={name:u.name||'Designer',email:(u.email||'guest@space.sg').toLowerCase(),role:'designer'};
  const o=store();o.users=o.users||{};o.users[STATE.user.email]={name:STATE.user.name,role:'designer',last:new Date().toISOString()};o.session=STATE.user.email;storeSet(o);
  if(d.project){const P=myProjects();const p=P.find(p=>p.name===d.project.name);
   if(p){openProject(p.id);}else{STATE.project={id:'P'+Date.now().toString(36),name:d.project.name,client:d.project.client||'',created:new Date().toISOString()};loadSample(d.project.sample||'hdb4');const st=+d.project.stage||0;if(st>=3){STATE.locked=true;logIt('Lock','plan','proposal','revision 0','scale and rooms confirmed');STATE.cur=Math.min(st,5);STATE.maxSeen=STATE.cur;}else{STATE.cur=0;STATE.maxSeen=1;}render();saveProject();hideGate();toast(st>=3?'Opened '+d.project.name:'Project created: '+d.project.name);}}
  else{showProjects();}
  if(d.tour)setTimeout(studioTour,700);}
 if(d.type==='space:tour')studioTour();});

/* SPACE guided tour: spotlight, bouncing arrow, step card. Shared by the site and the studio. */
window.SpaceTour=(function(){
 const CSS=`#sptour{position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:1000;pointer-events:none;font-family:'Jost','Helvetica Neue',Arial,sans-serif}
#sptour .sp-hi{position:fixed;border:2px solid #B99A81;border-radius:12px;box-shadow:0 0 0 9999px rgba(55,56,55,.58);transition:top .45s cubic-bezier(.2,.9,.3,1),left .45s cubic-bezier(.2,.9,.3,1),width .45s,height .45s;pointer-events:none}
#sptour .sp-hi.none{border-color:transparent}
#sptour .sp-arrow{position:fixed;width:46px;height:46px;transition:top .45s,left .45s;filter:drop-shadow(0 4px 6px rgba(0,0,0,.25))}
#sptour .sp-arrow.up{animation:spbu 1s ease-in-out infinite}#sptour .sp-arrow.down{animation:spbd 1s ease-in-out infinite}#sptour .sp-arrow.left{animation:spbl 1s ease-in-out infinite}
@keyframes spbd{0%,100%{transform:translateY(0)}50%{transform:translateY(9px)}}
@keyframes spbu{0%,100%{transform:rotate(180deg) translateY(0)}50%{transform:rotate(180deg) translateY(9px)}}
@keyframes spbl{0%,100%{transform:rotate(90deg) translateY(0)}50%{transform:rotate(90deg) translateY(9px)}}
#sptour .sp-card{position:fixed;width:340px;max-width:calc(100vw - 24px);background:#fff;border:1px solid #E2DDD8;border-radius:14px;padding:16px 18px 14px;box-shadow:0 30px 60px -30px rgba(0,0,0,.45);pointer-events:auto;transition:top .45s cubic-bezier(.2,.9,.3,1),left .45s cubic-bezier(.2,.9,.3,1);color:#373837}
#sptour .sp-k{font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',ui-monospace,monospace;font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#9C7B5B;margin-bottom:6px}
#sptour h4{font-size:17px;font-weight:500;margin:0 0 6px;line-height:1.2}
#sptour p{font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.5;color:#5A5C5A;margin:0}
#sptour .sp-nav{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-top:14px;gap:8px}
#sptour button{font:inherit;font-size:13px;padding:7px 12px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid #D2CDCA;background:#fff;color:#373837;cursor:pointer}
#sptour button.sp-next{background:#373837;color:#ECE9E6;border-color:#373837}
#sptour button.sp-skip{border:0;color:#8C8A86;padding-left:0}
#sptour .sp-dots{display:flex;gap:3px}#sptour .sp-dots i{width:9px;height:3px;border-radius:2px;background:#ECE9E6}#sptour .sp-dots i.on{background:#B99A81;width:16px}
#sptour .sp-nav>span:last-child{display:flex;gap:6px;white-space:nowrap}#sptour button{white-space:nowrap}
#sptour .sp-try{display:inline-block;margin-top:8px;font-size:12px;color:#6E7F63;font-family:'IBM Plex Mono',ui-monospace,monospace}`;
 let steps=[],i=0,root,opts={},timer=null;
 function build(){if(root)return;const st=document.createElement('style');st.textContent=CSS;document.head.appendChild(st);
  root=document.createElement('div');root.id='sptour';
  root.innerHTML=`<div class="sp-hi none"></div><svg class="sp-arrow down" viewBox="0 0 46 46"><path d="M23 4 v30 M9 22 l14 14 l14 -14" fill="none" stroke="#B99A81" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>
   <div class="sp-card"><div class="sp-k"></div><h4></h4><p></p><span class="sp-try" style="display:none"></span><div class="sp-nav"><button class="sp-skip">Skip tour</button><span class="sp-dots"></span><span><button class="sp-back">Back</button> <button class="sp-next">Next</button></span></div></div>`;
  document.body.appendChild(root);
  root.querySelector('.sp-skip').onclick=end;root.querySelector('.sp-back').onclick=()=>show(i-1);root.querySelector('.sp-next').onclick=()=>show(i+1);
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>{if(steps.length)place(steps[i],true);});
  window.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(!steps.length)return;if(e.key==='Escape')end();if(e.key==='ArrowRight')show(i+1);if(e.key==='ArrowLeft')show(i-1);});}
 function start(s,o){build();steps=s;opts=o||{};root.style.display='block';show(0);}
 function end(){if(!steps.length)return;steps=[];root.style.display='none';if(opts.onEnd)opts.onEnd();}
 function show(n){if(!steps.length||n<0)return;if(n>=steps.length){const o=opts;end();if(o.onFinish)o.onFinish();return;}
  i=n;const s=steps[i];if(s.before){try{s.before();}catch(e){}}
  const c=root.querySelector('.sp-card');c.querySelector('.sp-k').textContent=(i+1)+' of '+steps.length+(s.k?' · '+s.k:'');c.querySelector('h4').textContent=s.title;c.querySelector('p').textContent=s.text;
  const t=c.querySelector('.sp-try');if(s.tryIt){t.textContent='Try it: '+s.tryIt;t.style.display='inline-block';}else t.style.display='none';
  c.querySelector('.sp-back').style.visibility=i?'visible':'hidden';c.querySelector('.sp-next').textContent=i===steps.length-1?(s.last||'Done'):'Next';
  c.querySelector('.sp-dots').innerHTML=steps.map((_,j)=>`<i class="${j===i?'on':''}"></i>`).join('');
  clearTimeout(timer);timer=setTimeout(()=>place(s,false),s.el?60:0);}
 function place(s,quiet){const hi=root.querySelector('.sp-hi'),ar=root.querySelector('.sp-arrow'),c=root.querySelector('.sp-card');
  const el=s.el?document.querySelector(s.el):null;const vw=innerWidth,vh=innerHeight;
  if(!el){hi.className='sp-hi none';hi.style.cssText='top:50%;left:50%;width:0;height:0';ar.style.display='none';
   const cw=Math.min(340,vw-24),ch=c.offsetHeight||180;c.style.left=Math.round((vw-cw)/2)+'px';c.style.top=Math.round((vh-ch)/2)+'px';return;}
  if(!quiet){el.scrollIntoView({block:'center',inline:'nearest',behavior:'smooth'});}
  setTimeout(()=>{const r=el.getBoundingClientRect(),pad=8;hi.className='sp-hi';hi.style.cssText=`top:${r.top-pad}px;left:${r.left-pad}px;width:${r.width+2*pad}px;height:${r.height+2*pad}px`;
   const cw=Math.min(340,vw-24),ch=c.offsetHeight||200;const below=r.bottom+16+ch+60<vh,above=r.top-16-ch-60>0;
   ar.style.display='block';let cl=Math.min(Math.max(12,r.left+r.width/2-cw/2),vw-cw-12),ct;
   if(below){ar.className='sp-arrow up';ar.style.top=(r.bottom+6)+'px';ar.style.left=(r.left+r.width/2-23)+'px';ct=r.bottom+58;}
   else if(above){ar.className='sp-arrow down';ar.style.top=(r.top-54)+'px';ar.style.left=(r.left+r.width/2-23)+'px';ct=r.top-62-ch;}
   else{ar.className='sp-arrow left';ar.style.top=(r.top+r.height/2-23)+'px';ar.style.left=(r.right+6)+'px';cl=Math.min(r.right+60,vw-cw-12);ct=Math.max(12,Math.min(r.top,vh-ch-12));}
   c.style.left=Math.round(cl)+'px';c.style.top=Math.round(Math.max(12,Math.min(ct,vh-ch-12)))+'px';},quiet?0:420);}
 return {start,end,next:()=>show(i+1),active:()=>steps.length>0};
})();

</script>
