<script>
/* ===================== SPACE Studio: plan underlay (trace mode), Phase 1 =====================
   Added on top of Studio. Nothing existing is removed: the uploaded plan sits faded under the
   generated walls in Confirm and Design, in the same world millimetres. Users calibrate it,
   correct the reading over it, and see which walls deviate from it. Every correction goes into
   the existing change register as "Read correction" and can be undone with Ctrl/Cmd+Z. */
(function(){
'use strict';
const W=window;
const PDFJS='https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/';
const TS={tool:'select',sel:null,addPt:null,mouse:null,cal:null,view:{},drag:null,over:false,pdf:null,up:null,busy:false,thick:100};
const LUM={},BLOB={};const DEVC=new Map();
const escH=s=>String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'})[c]);

/* ---------- state ---------- */
function TR(){if(!STATE.trace)STATE.trace={cur:0,tol:20,snap:true,consent:false,consentImg:false,inPack:false,outbox:[],floors:[]};return STATE.trace;}
function FL(){const t=STATE.trace;if(!t||!t.floors||!t.floors.length)return null;if(t.cur>=t.floors.length)t.cur=0;return t.floors[t.cur];}
const hasUL=()=>{const f=FL();return !!(f&&f.src);};
const ulVisible=()=>{const f=FL();return !!(f&&f.src&&f.on);};
function newFloor(name,img,m,origin,file){return {id:'L'+Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,5),name,src:img?img.src:null,w:img?img.w:0,h:img?img.h:0,file:file||'',page:img&&img.page||1,origin:origin||'upload',m:m||[10,0,0,10,0,0],m0:(m||[10,0,0,10,0,0]).slice(),on:true,op:0.3,look:'grey',locked:false,cal:origin==='sample',model:null};}

/* ---------- affine maths: m=[a,b,c,d,e,f], world x=a*px+c*py+e, y=b*px+d*py+f ---------- */
const mul=(A,B)=>[A[0]*B[0]+A[2]*B[1],A[1]*B[0]+A[3]*B[1],A[0]*B[2]+A[2]*B[3],A[1]*B[2]+A[3]*B[3],A[0]*B[4]+A[2]*B[5]+A[4],A[1]*B[4]+A[3]*B[5]+A[5]];
const app=(m,x,y)=>[m[0]*x+m[2]*y+m[4],m[1]*x+m[3]*y+m[5]];
const inv=m=>{const d=m[0]*m[3]-m[1]*m[2];return [m[3]/d,-m[1]/d,-m[2]/d,m[0]/d,(m[2]*m[5]-m[3]*m[4])/d,(m[1]*m[4]-m[0]*m[5])/d];};
const mmpx=m=>Math.sqrt(Math.abs(m[0]*m[3]-m[1]*m[2]));
const rotDeg=m=>Math.atan2(m[1],m[0])*180/Math.PI;
function solve3(P,Q){const [[x1,y1],[x2,y2],[x3,y3]]=P;const D=x1*(y2-y3)-y1*(x2-x3)+(x2*y3-x3*y2);if(Math.abs(D)<1e-6)return null;
  const s=(v1,v2,v3)=>[(v1*(y2-y3)-y1*(v2-v3)+(v2*y3-v3*y2))/D,(x1*(v2-v3)-v1*(x2-x3)+(x2*v3-x3*v2))/D,(x1*(y2*v3-y3*v2)-y1*(x2*v3-x3*v2)+v1*(x2*y3-x3*y2))/D];
  const [a,c,e]=s(Q[0][0],Q[1][0],Q[2][0]),[b,d,f]=s(Q[0][1],Q[1][1],Q[2][1]);return [a,b,c,d,e,f];}
const mtx=m=>m.map(v=>+v.toFixed(6)).join(' ');

/* ---------- image helpers ---------- */
function blobUrl(f){if(!f||!f.src)return '';const b=BLOB[f.id];if(b&&b.src===f.src)return b.url;
  try{const [h,d]=f.src.split(',');const mime=(h.match(/data:([^;]+)/)||[])[1]||'image/jpeg';const bin=atob(d);const u=new Uint8Array(bin.length);for(let i=0;i<bin.length;i++)u[i]=bin.charCodeAt(i);
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([u],{type:mime}));if(b)URL.revokeObjectURL(b.url);BLOB[f.id]={src:f.src,url};return url;}catch(e){return f.src;}}
function lumFor(f){if(!f||!f.src)return null;const L=LUM[f.id];if(L&&L.src===f.src)return L.ready?L:null;
  const rec={src:f.src,ready:false};LUM[f.id]=rec;const im=new Image();
  im.onload=()=>{if(LUM[f.id]!==rec)return;const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=im.naturalWidth;c.height=im.naturalHeight;const x=c.getContext('2d');x.drawImage(im,0,0);let d;try{d=x.getImageData(0,0,c.width,c.height).data;}catch(e){return;}
    const n=c.width*c.height,lum=new Uint8Array(n);let sum=0;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const v=(d[i*4]*0.3+d[i*4+1]*0.59+d[i*4+2]*0.11)|0;lum[i]=v;sum+=v;}
    rec.w=c.width;rec.h=c.height;rec.lum=lum;rec.thr=Math.max(60,Math.min(140,(sum/n)*0.55));rec.ready=true;DEVC.clear();refresh();};
  im.src=f.src;return null;}
function downscale(im,max){const w0=im.naturalWidth||im.width,h0=im.naturalHeight||im.height;const k=Math.min(1,(max||2400)/Math.max(w0,h0));const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.max(1,Math.round(w0*k));c.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(h0*k));
  const x=c.getContext('2d');x.fillStyle='#fff';x.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);x.drawImage(im,0,0,c.width,c.height);return {src:c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.86),w:c.width,h:c.height};}
function readImage(file){return new Promise((res,rej)=>{const r=new FileReader();r.onload=()=>{const im=new Image();im.onload=()=>res(downscale(im));im.onerror=()=>rej(new Error('This image format cannot be opened here. Use JPG, PNG or PDF.'));im.src=r.result;};r.onerror=()=>rej(new Error('Could not read the file'));r.readAsDataURL(file);});}
function loadPdfJs(){if(W.pdfjsLib)return Promise.resolve(W.pdfjsLib);return new Promise((res,rej)=>{const s=document.createElement('script');s.src=PDFJS+'pdf.min.js';s.onload=()=>{try{W.pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc=PDFJS+'pdf.worker.min.js';}catch(e){}W.pdfjsLib?res(W.pdfjsLib):rej(new Error('pdf'));};s.onerror=()=>rej(new Error('pdf'));document.head.appendChild(s);});}
async function pdfPage(n){const pdf=TS.pdf;const pg=await pdf.getPage(n);const v1=pg.getViewport({scale:1});const sc=Math.min(4,2400/Math.max(v1.width,v1.height));const vp=pg.getViewport({scale:sc});
  const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.round(vp.width);c.height=Math.round(vp.height);const x=c.getContext('2d');x.fillStyle='#fff';x.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
  await pg.render({canvasContext:x,viewport:vp}).promise;return {src:c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.88),w:c.width,h:c.height,page:n,ptpx:1/sc};}

/* ---------- the Singapore samples: the brochure plan, drawn at the Read scale of 5 mm per pixel ---------- */
function makeSampleUL(){const ws=STATE.walls.filter(w=>w.state!=='new');if(!ws.length)return;
  const xs=ws.flatMap(w=>wpts(w).map(p=>p[0])),ys=ws.flatMap(w=>wpts(w).map(p=>p[1]));const x0=Math.min(...xs),x1=Math.max(...xs),y0=Math.min(...ys),y1=Math.max(...ys);
  const K=5,mg=130;const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.round((x1-x0)/K+mg*2);c.height=Math.round((y1-y0)/K+mg*2+40);const g=c.getContext('2d');
  const P=(x,y)=>[(x-x0)/K+mg,(y-y0)/K+mg];
  g.fillStyle='#FCFCFA';g.fillRect(0,0,c.width,c.height);
  g.fillStyle='#1d1d1d';
  for(const w of ws){const [[ax,ay],[bx,by]]=wpts(w);const t=w.t/K;const [px,py]=P(ax,ay),[qx,qy]=P(bx,by);
    if(w.axis==='x')g.fillRect(px-t/2,py-t/2,qx-px+t,t);else g.fillRect(px-t/2,py-t/2,t,qy-py+t);}
  for(const w of ws)for(const o of (w.opens||[])){if(o.state==='new')continue;const t=w.t/K+2;
    const [ax,ay]=w.axis==='x'?P(o.a,w.fixed):P(w.fixed,o.a),[bx,by]=w.axis==='x'?P(o.b,w.fixed):P(w.fixed,o.b);
    g.fillStyle='#FCFCFA';if(w.axis==='x')g.fillRect(ax,ay-t/2,bx-ax,t);else g.fillRect(ax-t/2,ay,t,by-ay);
    g.strokeStyle='#1d1d1d';g.lineWidth=1.2;
    if(o.kind==='window'){if(w.axis==='x'){g.strokeRect(ax,ay-t/2+2,bx-ax,t-4);g.beginPath();g.moveTo(ax,ay);g.lineTo(bx,by);g.stroke();}else{g.strokeRect(ax-t/2+2,ay,t-4,by-ay);g.beginPath();g.moveTo(ax,ay);g.lineTo(bx,by);g.stroke();}}
    else if(o.kind==='door'){const r=(o.b-o.a)/K;const s=o.into||1;g.beginPath();if(w.axis==='x'){g.moveTo(ax,ay);g.lineTo(ax,ay+s*r);g.moveTo(ax,ay+s*r);g.arc(ax,ay,r,s>0?Math.PI/2:-Math.PI/2,0,s>0);}else{g.moveTo(ax,ay);g.lineTo(ax+s*r,ay);g.moveTo(ax+s*r,ay);g.arc(ax,ay,r,s>0?0:Math.PI,Math.PI/2,s<0);}g.stroke();}}
  g.fillStyle='#333';g.font='600 17px Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif';g.textAlign='center';
  try{(STATE.M?STATE.M.rooms:[]).forEach(r=>{const [x,y]=P(r.cx,r.cy);g.fillText(String(r.program).toUpperCase(),x,y);g.font='13px Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif';g.fillText(((r.area/1e6).toFixed(1))+' sq m',x,y+18);g.font='600 17px Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif';});}catch(e){}
  const [lx]=P(x0,y0),[rx]=P(x1,y0);g.strokeStyle='#555';g.lineWidth=1;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(lx,mg-70);g.lineTo(rx,mg-70);g.moveTo(lx,mg-80);g.lineTo(lx,mg-60);g.moveTo(rx,mg-80);g.lineTo(rx,mg-60);g.stroke();
  g.fillStyle='#555';g.font='14px Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif';g.fillText(String(Math.round(x1-x0)),(lx+rx)/2,mg-78);
  g.textAlign='left';g.font='600 15px Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif';g.fillText(String(STATE.file||'Unit plan').toUpperCase(),mg,c.height-34);g.font='12px Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif';g.fillText('Floor plan for illustration only. Not to scale.',mg,c.height-16);
  const img={src:c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.86),w:c.width,h:c.height};
  const m=[K,0,0,K,x0-K*mg,y0-K*mg];
  const T=TR();T.floors=[newFloor('Level 1',img,m,'sample',STATE.file)];T.cur=0;}

/* ---------- deviation: where is the plan's wall line, relative to the model's wall? ---------- */
function bil(L,x,y){if(x<0||y<0||x>=L.w-1||y>=L.h-1)return -1;const x0=x|0,y0=y|0,fx=x-x0,fy=y-y0,i=y0*L.w+x0,l=L.lum;return (l[i]*(1-fx)+l[i+1]*fx)*(1-fy)+(l[i+L.w]*(1-fx)+l[i+L.w+1]*fx)*fy;}
function profile(f,L,axis,fixed,a,b,opens,R){const im=inv(f.m);const len=b-a;if(len<150)return null;const st=Math.max(0.5,Math.min(mmpx(f.m)/4,5));const N=Math.max(8,Math.min(40,Math.round(len/150)));
  const ts=[];for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const t=a+len*(0.08+0.84*i/(N-1));if(opens&&opens.some(o=>o.state!=='removed'&&t>o.a-60&&t<o.b+60))continue;ts.push(t);}if(ts.length<3)return null;
  const ds=[],vs=[];
  for(let d=-R;d<=R+1e-9;d+=st){let sum=0,n=0,dk=0;for(const t of ts){const wx=axis==='x'?t:fixed+d,wy=axis==='x'?fixed+d:t;const p=app(im,wx,wy);const v=bil(L,p[0]-0.5,p[1]-0.5);if(v<0)continue;n++;if(v<L.thr)dk++;sum+=v;}
    ds.push(d);vs.push(n&&dk/n>=0.5?Math.min(sum/n,L.thr-0.01):(n?Math.max(sum/n,L.thr):255));}
  const T=L.thr,runs=[];let s0=-1;
  for(let i=0;i<=vs.length;i++){const dark=i<vs.length&&vs[i]<T;if(dark&&s0<0)s0=i;if(!dark&&s0>=0){const e=i-1;
      const lo=s0>0?ds[s0-1]+(ds[s0]-ds[s0-1])*(vs[s0-1]-T)/Math.max(1e-6,vs[s0-1]-vs[s0]):ds[s0];
      const hi=e<vs.length-1?ds[e]+(ds[e+1]-ds[e])*(T-vs[e])/Math.max(1e-6,vs[e+1]-vs[e]):ds[e];runs.push({a:lo,b:hi});s0=-1;}}
  if(!runs.length)return {found:false};runs.sort((p,q)=>Math.abs((p.a+p.b)/2)-Math.abs((q.a+q.b)/2));const r=runs[0];return {found:true,off:Math.round((r.a+r.b)/2),width:Math.round(r.b-r.a)};}
const checkable=w=>w.state!=='new';
function devOf(w){const f=FL();if(!f||!f.src||!checkable(w))return null;const L=lumFor(f);if(!L)return null;
  const key=f.id+'|'+f.m.join(',')+'|'+w.axis+'|'+w.fixed+'|'+w.a+'|'+w.b+'|'+(w.opens||[]).map(o=>o.a+'-'+o.b+o.state).join(',');
  if(DEVC.has(key))return DEVC.get(key);if(DEVC.size>6000)DEVC.clear();const r=profile(f,L,w.axis,w.fixed,w.a,w.b,w.opens,300);DEVC.set(key,r);return r;}
function status(w){const r=devOf(w);if(!r)return 'na';if(!r.found)return 'miss';return Math.abs(r.off)>TR().tol?'off':'ok';}
function snapFixed(axis,fixed,a,b){const f=FL();if(!f||!f.src||!TR().snap)return null;const L=lumFor(f);if(!L)return null;const r=profile(f,L,axis,fixed,a,b,[],150);return r&&r.found?fixed+r.off:null;}
const CHK={ok:'#6E7F63',off:'#A8503B',miss:'#C9953C',na:'#373837'};

/* ---------- corrections: every one is an undo step and a change-register line ---------- */
const geo=w=>(w.axis==='x'?'horizontal ':'vertical ')+Math.round(w.a)+' to '+Math.round(w.b)+' at '+(w.axis==='x'?'y ':'x ')+Math.round(w.fixed)+' mm';
function corr(op,w,before,after,extra){const T=TR();const fl=FL();
  logIt('Read correction',w.id,before,after,op+(extra?' · '+extra:'')+(T.floors.length>1&&fl?' · '+fl.name:'')+(STATE.locked?' · amends revision 0':''));
  if(T.consent){const rec={ts:new Date().toISOString(),floor:fl?fl.id:null,op,wall:w.id,before,after,extra:extra||''};T.outbox.push(rec);if(T.outbox.length>500)T.outbox.shift();
    try{if(W.parent!==W)W.parent.postMessage({type:'space:read-feedback',payload:{project:STATE.project?STATE.project.id:null,file:STATE.file,floor:fl?fl.name:null,calibration:fl?{m:fl.m,m0:fl.m0}:null,correction:rec,image:T.consentImg?'shared on request':null}},'*');}catch(e){}}}
function moveWall(w,nf,why,batch){nf=Math.round(nf);if(nf===w.fixed)return false;if(!batch)snap();const of=w.fixed,before=geo(w);
  for(const o of STATE.walls){if(o===w||o.axis===w.axis)continue;if(o.fixed<w.a-2||o.fixed>w.b+2)continue;if(Math.abs(o.a-of)<=2)o.a=nf;else if(Math.abs(o.b-of)<=2)o.b=nf;if(o.a>o.b){const t=o.a;o.a=o.b;o.b=t;}}
  w.fixed=nf;corr(why||'move',w,before,geo(w),(nf-of>0?'+':'')+(nf-of)+' mm');if(!batch)recompute(w.id+' moved '+Math.abs(nf-of)+' mm');return true;}
function splitWall(w,at){at=Math.round(at/5)*5;if(at<=w.a+100||at>=w.b-100){toast('Too close to the end of '+w.id+': split at least 100 mm in');return;}
  snap();const before=geo(w);const nw=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(w));nw.id=nextWallId();nw.a=at;const keep=[],mv=[];for(const o of w.opens)((o.a+o.b)/2<at?keep:mv).push(o);w.opens=keep;nw.opens=mv.map(o=>({...o,id:o.id}));w.b=at;STATE.walls.push(nw);
  corr('split',w,before,geo(w)+' + '+nw.id+' '+geo(nw));TS.sel=w.id;recompute(w.id+' split into '+w.id+' and '+nw.id);}
function deleteWall(w){snap();const before=geo(w);STATE.walls=STATE.walls.filter(x=>x!==w);TS.sel=null;if(STATE.selWall===w.id)STATE.selWall=null;
  corr('delete',w,before,'removed from the reading',w.opens.length?w.opens.length+' opening(s) removed with it':'');recompute(w.id+' removed from the reading');}
function addWall(p,q){const [x1,y1]=p,[x2,y2]=q;const axis=Math.abs(x2-x1)>=Math.abs(y2-y1)?'x':'y';
  let a=axis==='x'?Math.min(x1,x2):Math.min(y1,y2),b=axis==='x'?Math.max(x1,x2):Math.max(y1,y2),fixed=axis==='x'?y1:x1;
  if(b-a<200){toast('Too short: a wall needs at least 200 mm');return;}let note='';const s=snapFixed(axis,fixed,a,b);if(s!=null){fixed=s;note='snapped to the plan line';}
  fixed=Math.round(fixed/5)*5;a=Math.round(a/5)*5;b=Math.round(b/5)*5;
  snap();const w={id:nextWallId(),axis,fixed,a,b,t:TS.thick||T_INT,ext:false,type:'full',state:'existing',opens:[]};STATE.walls.push(w);TS.sel=w.id;
  corr('add',w,'missing from the reading',geo(w),note);recompute(w.id+' added to the reading');}
function snapWall(w){const r=devOf(w);if(!r||!r.found||!r.off)return;moveWall(w,w.fixed+r.off,'snap to plan');}
function snapAll(){const L=STATE.walls.filter(w=>status(w)==='off');if(!L.length){toast('Nothing to snap: every wall is within '+TR().tol+' mm');return;}
  snap();let n=0;for(const w of L){const r=devOf(w);if(r&&r.found&&moveWall(w,w.fixed+r.off,'snap to plan',true))n++;}recompute(n+' wall'+(n===1?'':'s')+' snapped to the plan');}

/* ---------- calibration (the underlay moves, the walls never do) ---------- */
function canCal(){const f=FL();if(!f||!f.src){toast('Upload the plan first');return null;}if(f.locked){toast('Unlock the underlay position first');return null;}return f;}
function calDone(f,how,before){DEVC.clear();f.cal=true;logIt('Read calibration',f.name,before,scaleTxt(f),how);autosave();render();}
const scaleTxt=f=>mmpx(f.m).toFixed(2)+' mm/px · '+(((rotDeg(f.m)%360)+360)%360).toFixed(1)+'°';
function rotate(deg){const f=canCal();if(!f)return;const [cx,cy]=app(f.m,f.w/2,f.h/2);const r=deg*Math.PI/180,cs=Math.cos(r),sn=Math.sin(r);
  f.m=mul([cs,sn,-sn,cs,cx-cs*cx+sn*cy,cy-sn*cx-cs*cy],f.m);DEVC.clear();autosave();refresh(true);}
function nudge(dx,dy){const f=canCal();if(!f)return;f.m=f.m.slice();f.m[4]+=dx;f.m[5]+=dy;autosave();refresh(true);}

/* ---------- the trace canvas ---------- */
function wallsBox(){const ws=STATE.walls;if(!ws.length)return null;const xs=ws.flatMap(w=>wpts(w).map(p=>p[0])),ys=ws.flatMap(w=>wpts(w).map(p=>p[1]));return [Math.min(...xs),Math.min(...ys),Math.max(...xs),Math.max(...ys)];}
function fitView(){const f=FL();let b=wallsBox();if(f&&f.src){const cs=[[0,0],[f.w,0],[0,f.h],[f.w,f.h]].map(p=>app(f.m,p[0],p[1]));const ub=[Math.min(...cs.map(p=>p[0])),Math.min(...cs.map(p=>p[1])),Math.max(...cs.map(p=>p[0])),Math.max(...cs.map(p=>p[1]))];
    b=b?(STATE.walls.length>2?[Math.min(b[0],ub[0]),Math.min(b[1],ub[1]),Math.max(b[2],ub[2]),Math.max(b[3],ub[3])]:ub):ub;}
  if(!b)b=[0,0,12000,9000];const pw=(b[2]-b[0])||12000,ph=(b[3]-b[1])||9000;const v={x:b[0]-pw*0.05,y:b[1]-ph*0.05,w:pw*1.1,h:ph*1.1};TS.view[vk()]=v;return v;}
const vk=()=>{const f=FL();return f?f.id:'none';};
const view=()=>TS.view[vk()]||fitView();
function mmPerPx(){const s=document.getElementById('trsvg');try{return 1/s.getScreenCTM().a;}catch(e){return view().w/800;}}
function evMM(e){const svg=document.getElementById('trsvg');const pt=svg.createSVGPoint();pt.x=e.clientX;pt.y=e.clientY;const q=pt.matrixTransform(svg.getScreenCTM().inverse());return [q.x,q.y];}
function segDist(p,w){const [[ax,ay],[bx,by]]=wpts(w);const dx=bx-ax,dy=by-ay,L=dx*dx+dy*dy||1;let t=((p[0]-ax)*dx+(p[1]-ay)*dy)/L;t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));const x=ax+t*dx,y=ay+t*dy;return Math.hypot(p[0]-x,p[1]-y);}
function hitWall(p){const tol=10*mmPerPx();let best=null,bd=1e18;for(const w of STATE.walls){const d=segDist(p,w);if(d<=Math.max(w.t/2,tol)&&d<bd){bd=d;best=w;}}return best;}
function snapPt(p){const tol=12*mmPerPx();let best=null,bd=tol;for(const w of STATE.walls)for(const q of wpts(w)){const d=Math.hypot(p[0]-q[0],p[1]-q[1]);if(d<bd){bd=d;best=q;}}return best?best.slice():[Math.round(p[0]/5)*5,Math.round(p[1]/5)*5];}
function ortho(p,q){return Math.abs(q[0]-p[0])>=Math.abs(q[1]-p[1])?[q[0],p[1]]:[p[0],q[1]];}

function filters(){return `<defs><filter id="ulGrey" color-interpolation-filters="sRGB"><feColorMatrix type="saturate" values="0"/></filter><filter id="ulTint" color-interpolation-filters="sRGB"><feColorMatrix type="matrix" values="0.117 0.23 0.043 0 0.61  0.156 0.307 0.057 0 0.48  0.192 0.378 0.07 0 0.36  0 0 0 1 0"/></filter></defs>`;}
const lookF=f=>f.look==='grey'?'url(#ulGrey)':(f.look==='tint'?'url(#ulTint)':'none');
function trWalls(){const v=view(),px=v.w/900,fs=Math.max(px*11,40);const out=[];const dr=TS.drag&&TS.drag.kind==='wall'&&TS.drag.moved?TS.drag:null;
  for(const w of STATE.walls){const st=hasUL()?status(w):'na';const col=w.state==='new'?'#B99A81':CHK[st];const [[ax,ay],[bx,by]]=wpts(w);const sel=TS.sel===w.id;const dem=w.state==='demolished';
    if(sel)out.push(`<line x1="${ax}" y1="${ay}" x2="${bx}" y2="${by}" stroke="#B99A81" stroke-width="${w.t+px*14}" stroke-linecap="round" opacity=".45"/>`);
    out.push(`<line x1="${ax}" y1="${ay}" x2="${bx}" y2="${by}" stroke="${col}" stroke-width="${w.t}" opacity="${dem?.25:.5}" ${dem?`stroke-dasharray="${px*10} ${px*8}"`:''}/>`);
    out.push(`<line x1="${ax}" y1="${ay}" x2="${bx}" y2="${by}" stroke="${col}" stroke-width="1.4" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>`);
    for(const o of (w.opens||[])){if(o.state==='removed')continue;const h=w.axis==='x';out.push(`<line x1="${h?o.a:w.fixed}" y1="${h?w.fixed:o.a}" x2="${h?o.b:w.fixed}" y2="${h?w.fixed:o.b}" stroke="${o.kind==='window'?'#7A98A3':'#fff'}" stroke-width="${w.t*0.7}" opacity=".85"/>`);}
    const mx=(ax+bx)/2,my=(ay+by)/2;const r=st==='off'||st==='miss'?devOf(w):null;
    out.push(`<text x="${w.axis==='x'?mx:mx+fs*0.5}" y="${w.axis==='x'?my-fs*0.55:my}" font-size="${fs}" fill="${col}" font-family="IBM Plex Mono,monospace" paint-order="stroke" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="${fs*0.25}" ${w.axis==='x'?'text-anchor="middle"':''}>${w.id}${r?(r.found?' '+(r.off>0?'+':'')+r.off+' mm':' ?'):''}</text>`);}
  if(dr){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===dr.id);if(w){const g={...w,fixed:dr.nf};const [[ax,ay],[bx,by]]=wpts(g);out.push(`<line x1="${ax}" y1="${ay}" x2="${bx}" y2="${by}" stroke="#9C7B5B" stroke-width="${w.t}" opacity=".55"/><line x1="${ax}" y1="${ay}" x2="${bx}" y2="${by}" stroke="#9C7B5B" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-dasharray="6 4" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>`);}}
  return out.join('');}
function trOver(){const v=view(),px=v.w/900,r=px*7,out=[];const c=TS.cal;
  const dot=(p,n,col)=>`<circle cx="${p[0]}" cy="${p[1]}" r="${r}" fill="${col}" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="${px*2}"/><text x="${p[0]+r*1.3}" y="${p[1]-r*1.1}" font-size="${px*13}" font-weight="600" fill="${col}" paint-order="stroke" stroke="#fff" stroke-width="${px*3}">${n}</text>`;
  if(c&&c.pts){const f=FL();c.pts.forEach((q,i)=>{const p=c.kind===3&&f?app(f.m,q[0],q[1]):q;out.push(dot(p,c.kind===2?'P'+(i+1):'ABC'[i],'#9C7B5B'));});
    if(c.kind===2&&c.pts.length===2)out.push(`<line x1="${c.pts[0][0]}" y1="${c.pts[0][1]}" x2="${c.pts[1][0]}" y2="${c.pts[1][1]}" stroke="#9C7B5B" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-dasharray="6 4" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>`);}
  if(TS.tool==='add'&&TS.addPt){out.push(dot(TS.addPt,'','#373837'));if(TS.mouse){const q=ortho(TS.addPt,TS.mouse);out.push(`<line x1="${TS.addPt[0]}" y1="${TS.addPt[1]}" x2="${q[0]}" y2="${q[1]}" stroke="#373837" stroke-width="${TS.thick}" opacity=".3"/><line x1="${TS.addPt[0]}" y1="${TS.addPt[1]}" x2="${q[0]}" y2="${q[1]}" stroke="#373837" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-dasharray="6 4" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>`);}}
  if(TS.tool==='split'&&TS.mouse){const w=hitWall(TS.mouse);if(w){const at=w.axis==='x'?[TS.mouse[0],w.fixed]:[w.fixed,TS.mouse[1]];const L=px*16;out.push(`<line x1="${w.axis==='x'?at[0]:at[0]-L}" y1="${w.axis==='x'?at[1]-L:at[1]}" x2="${w.axis==='x'?at[0]:at[0]+L}" y2="${w.axis==='x'?at[1]+L:at[1]}" stroke="#A8503B" stroke-width="2" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>`);}}
  return out.join('');}
function trImage(){const f=FL();if(!f||!f.src)return '';return `<image id="trimg" href="${blobUrl(f)}" x="0" y="0" width="${f.w}" height="${f.h}" preserveAspectRatio="none" transform="matrix(${mtx(f.m)})" opacity="${f.on?f.op:0}" filter="${lookF(f)}"/>`;}
function trGrid(){const b=wallsBox()||[0,0,12000,9000];const o=[];for(let x=Math.floor(b[0]/1000)*1000;x<=b[2];x+=1000)o.push(`<line x1="${x}" y1="${b[1]}" x2="${x}" y2="${b[3]}" stroke="#F0ECE8" stroke-width="1" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>`);for(let y=Math.floor(b[1]/1000)*1000;y<=b[3];y+=1000)o.push(`<line x1="${b[0]}" y1="${y}" x2="${b[2]}" y2="${y}" stroke="#F0ECE8" stroke-width="1" vector-effect="non-scaling-stroke"/>`);return o.join('');}
function trSVG(){const v=view();return `<svg id="trsvg" viewBox="${v.x} ${v.y} ${v.w} ${v.h}" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" class="tr-${TS.tool}" onpointerdown="UL.down(event)" onpointermove="UL.move(event)" onpointerup="UL.up(event)" onpointerleave="UL.leave(event)" onpointerenter="UL.enter()" onwheel="UL.wheel(event)">${filters()}<rect x="${v.x-v.w*20}" y="${v.y-v.h*20}" width="${v.w*41}" height="${v.h*41}" fill="#fff"/>${trGrid()}${trImage()}<g id="trwalls">${trWalls()}</g><g id="trover">${trOver()}</g></svg>`;}
function refresh(img){const s=document.getElementById('trsvg');if(!s){return;}const v=view();s.setAttribute('viewBox',`${v.x} ${v.y} ${v.w} ${v.h}`);
  if(img){const i=document.getElementById('trimg'),f=FL();if(i&&f){i.setAttribute('transform',`matrix(${mtx(f.m)})`);i.setAttribute('opacity',f.on?f.op:0);i.setAttribute('filter',lookF(f));}}
  const g=document.getElementById('trwalls');if(g)g.innerHTML=trWalls();const o=document.getElementById('trover');if(o)o.innerHTML=trOver();
  const d=document.getElementById('trdev');if(d)d.innerHTML=devPanel();const sc=document.getElementById('trscale');const f=FL();if(sc&&f&&f.src)sc.textContent=scaleTxt(f);}

/* ---------- pointer ---------- */
const UL={};
UL.enter=()=>{TS.over=true;};
UL.leave=e=>{TS.over=false;TS.mouse=null;if(!TS.drag){const o=document.getElementById('trover');if(o)o.innerHTML=trOver();}};
UL.down=e=>{if(e.button>0&&e.pointerType==='mouse'&&e.button!==1)return;e.preventDefault();const svg=document.getElementById('trsvg');try{svg.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(x){}
  const p=evMM(e);TS.drag={kind:'click',sx:e.clientX,sy:e.clientY,p,v0:{...view()},moved:false};
  if(TS.tool==='select'&&!TS.cal){const w=hitWall(p);if(w){TS.drag={kind:'wall',id:w.id,sx:e.clientX,sy:e.clientY,p,orig:w.fixed,nf:w.fixed,moved:false,v0:{...view()}};}}};
UL.move=e=>{const p=evMM(e);TS.mouse=p;const d=TS.drag;
  if(d){const dist=Math.hypot(e.clientX-d.sx,e.clientY-d.sy);
    if(d.kind==='wall'){if(dist>4)d.moved=true;if(d.moved){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===d.id);const k=w.axis==='x'?1:0;d.nf=Math.round(d.orig+(p[k]-d.p[k]));const s=snapFixed(w.axis,d.nf,w.a,w.b);if(s!=null&&Math.abs(s-d.nf)<120)d.nf=Math.round(s);}
      const g=document.getElementById('trwalls');if(g)g.innerHTML=trWalls();return;}
    if(dist>5){d.moved=true;d.kind='pan';}
    if(d.kind==='pan'){const k=mmPerPx();const v=TS.view[vk()]={...d.v0};v.x=d.v0.x-(e.clientX-d.sx)*k;v.y=d.v0.y-(e.clientY-d.sy)*k;refresh();return;}}
  if(TS.tool==='add'||TS.tool==='split'){const o=document.getElementById('trover');if(o)o.innerHTML=trOver();}};
UL.up=e=>{const d=TS.drag;TS.drag=null;if(!d)return;const p=evMM(e);
  if(d.kind==='wall'){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===d.id);if(d.moved&&w&&d.nf!==w.fixed){moveWall(w,d.nf,'move');return;}TS.sel=d.id;render();return;}
  if(d.kind==='pan')return;
  // a click
  const c=TS.cal;
  if(c){const f=FL();if(!f)return;
    if(c.kind===2){c.pts.push(p);if(c.pts.length>2)c.pts=[p];render();return;}
    if(c.kind===3){if(c.pts.length<3){c.pts.push(app(inv(f.m),p[0],p[1]));if(c.pts.length===3)c.tgt=prefill3(c.pts.map(q=>app(f.m,q[0],q[1])));render();}return;}}
  if(TS.tool==='add'){const q=snapPt(p);if(!TS.addPt){TS.addPt=q;const o=document.getElementById('trover');if(o)o.innerHTML=trOver();return;}const a=TS.addPt;TS.addPt=null;addWall(a,ortho(a,q));return;}
  const w=hitWall(p);
  if(TS.tool==='split'){if(w)splitWall(w,w.axis==='x'?p[0]:p[1]);return;}
  if(TS.tool==='delete'){if(w)deleteWall(w);return;}
  TS.sel=w?w.id:null;render();};
UL.wheel=e=>{e.preventDefault();const p=evMM(e);const k=e.deltaY>0?1.15:1/1.15;zoomAt(p,k);};
function zoomAt(p,k){const v=view();const n={w:v.w*k,h:v.h*k};n.x=p[0]-(p[0]-v.x)*k;n.y=p[1]-(p[1]-v.y)*k;TS.view[vk()]=n;refresh();}
UL.zoom=k=>{const v=view();zoomAt([v.x+v.w/2,v.y+v.h/2],k);};
UL.fit=()=>{fitView();refresh();};
function prefill3(ws){const L=[];for(const w of STATE.walls)for(const q of wpts(w))L.push(q);
  if(L.length){return ws.map(p=>{let b=null,bd=600;for(const q of L){const d=Math.hypot(p[0]-q[0],p[1]-q[1]);if(d<bd){bd=d;b=q;}}return (b||[Math.round(p[0]),Math.round(p[1])]).map(v=>Math.round(v));});}
  const AB=Math.round(Math.hypot(ws[1][0]-ws[0][0],ws[1][1]-ws[0][1])/10)*10,AC=Math.round(Math.hypot(ws[2][0]-ws[0][0],ws[2][1]-ws[0][1])/10)*10;return [[0,0],[AB,0],[0,AC]];}

/* ---------- UI actions ---------- */
UL.tool=t=>{TS.tool=t;TS.addPt=null;TS.cal=null;render();};
UL.set=(k,v)=>{const f=FL();if(!f)return;f[k]=v;DEVC.clear();autosave();if(k==='op'||k==='on'||k==='look'){refresh(true);if(k!=='op')render();}else render();};
UL.opacity=v=>{const f=FL();if(!f)return;f.op=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,v/100));const i=document.getElementById('trimg');if(i)i.setAttribute('opacity',f.on?f.op:0);const l=document.getElementById('trOpL');if(l)l.textContent=Math.round(f.op*100)+'%';document.querySelectorAll('.ulplan').forEach(x=>x.setAttribute('opacity',f.on?f.op:0));};
UL.opDone=()=>autosave();
UL.tset=(k,v)=>{TR()[k]=v;DEVC.clear();autosave();render();};
UL.rot=d=>rotate(d);
UL.nudge=(dx,dy)=>nudge(dx,dy);
UL.reset=()=>{const f=canCal();if(!f)return;const b=scaleTxt(f);f.m=f.m0.slice();fitView();calDone(f,'reset to the Read alignment',b);};
UL.cal2=()=>{if(!canCal())return;TS.tool='select';TS.addPt=null;TS.cal={kind:2,pts:[]};render();};
UL.cal3=()=>{if(!canCal())return;TS.tool='select';TS.addPt=null;TS.cal={kind:3,pts:[],tgt:null};render();};
UL.calCancel=()=>{TS.cal=null;render();};
UL.apply2=()=>{const f=canCal(),c=TS.cal;if(!f||!c||c.pts.length<2)return;const real=+(document.getElementById('trReal')||{}).value;if(!(real>0)){toast('Type the real distance in mm');return;}
  const [p,q]=c.pts;const dist=Math.hypot(q[0]-p[0],q[1]-p[1]);if(dist<1){toast('Pick two different points');return;}const k=real/dist;const b=scaleTxt(f);
  f.m=mul([k,0,0,k,p[0]-k*p[0],p[1]-k*p[1]],f.m);TS.cal=null;fitView();calDone(f,'two-point scale · '+Math.round(dist)+' mm read as '+real+' mm',b);toast('Scale set: '+mmpx(f.m).toFixed(2)+' mm per pixel');};
UL.apply3=()=>{const f=canCal(),c=TS.cal;if(!f||!c||c.pts.length<3)return;const Q=[0,1,2].map(i=>[+document.getElementById('trq'+i+'x').value,+document.getElementById('trq'+i+'y').value]);
  if(Q.some(q=>!isFinite(q[0])||!isFinite(q[1]))){toast('Every point needs x and y in mm');return;}const m=solve3(c.pts,Q);if(!m||!isFinite(mmpx(m))||mmpx(m)<0.01){toast('Those three points are in a line: pick three corners that form a triangle');return;}
  const b=scaleTxt(f);f.m=m;TS.cal=null;fitView();calDone(f,'three-point align',b);toast('Aligned on three points: skew removed');};
UL.thick=v=>{TS.thick=+v||100;};
UL.snapW=id=>{const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===id);if(w)snapWall(w);};
UL.snapAll=()=>snapAll();
UL.del=id=>{const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===id);if(w)deleteWall(w);};
UL.moveBy=id=>{const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===id);const v=+(document.getElementById('trMv')||{}).value;if(!w||!v){toast('Type the offset in mm, for example 20 or -35');return;}moveWall(w,w.fixed+v,'move');};
UL.pick=id=>{TS.sel=id;const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===id);if(w){const v=view(),[[ax,ay],[bx,by]]=wpts(w);const cx=(ax+bx)/2,cy=(ay+by)/2;TS.view[vk()]={...v,x:cx-v.w/2,y:cy-v.h/2};}render();};
UL.floor=i=>{const T=TR();if(i===T.cur||!T.floors[i])return;saveLive(FL());T.cur=i;loadLive(FL());STATE.selWall=null;TS.sel=null;TS.cal=null;TS.addPt=null;STATE.hist=[];STATE.redo=[];
  if(STATE.walls.length)recompute('Now on '+FL().name);else{STATE.M=null;render();}};
UL.addFloor=()=>{const T=TR();if(!T.floors.length){toast('Upload the first floor plan first');return;}saveLive(FL());T.floors.push(newFloor('Level '+(T.floors.length+1),null,null,'upload'));T.cur=T.floors.length-1;loadLive(FL());
  STATE.selWall=null;TS.sel=null;STATE.hist=[];STATE.redo=[];STATE.M=null;autosave();render();toast(FL().name+' added: upload its plan');};
UL.rename=v=>{const f=FL();if(!f)return;f.name=String(v||'').trim().slice(0,30)||f.name;autosave();render();};
function saveLive(f){if(!f)return;f.model=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify({walls:STATE.walls,items:STATE.items,progAt:STATE.progAt,floorAt:STATE.floorAt,carp:STATE.carp}));}
function loadLive(f){const m=(f&&f.model)||{walls:[],items:[],progAt:[],floorAt:[],carp:[]};STATE.walls=m.walls||[];STATE.items=m.items||[];STATE.progAt=m.progAt||[];STATE.floorAt=m.floorAt||[];STATE.carp=m.carp||[];if(f)f.model=null;}

/* ---------- upload ---------- */
async function takeFile(file){if(!file)return null;const isPdf=/pdf$/i.test(file.type)||/\.pdf$/i.test(file.name);TS.busy=true;render();
  try{if(isPdf){let lib;try{lib=await loadPdfJs();}catch(e){throw new Error('The PDF reader could not load here. Save page 1 as a PNG or JPG and upload that.');}
      const buf=await file.arrayBuffer();TS.pdf=await lib.getDocument({data:new Uint8Array(buf)}).promise;TS.pdfName=file.name;const img=await pdfPage(1);return {...img,name:file.name,pages:TS.pdf.numPages,pdf:true};}
    const img=await readImage(file);TS.pdf=null;return {...img,name:file.name,pages:1,pdf:false};}
  catch(e){toast(e.message||'Could not open that file');return null;}finally{TS.busy=false;}}
W.handleFile=async function(f){const up=await takeFile(f);if(up){TS.up=up;TS.up.ratio=0;}render();};
UL.page=async n=>{if(!TS.pdf||!TS.up)return;TS.busy=true;render();try{const img=await pdfPage(+n);Object.assign(TS.up,img);}catch(e){toast('Could not render that page');}TS.busy=false;render();};
UL.ratio=v=>{if(TS.up)TS.up.ratio=+v||0;};
function startM(up,fitTo){if(fitTo){const [x0,y0,x1,y1]=fitTo;const k=Math.max((x1-x0)/up.w,(y1-y0)/up.h)*1.12;return [k,0,0,k,(x0+x1)/2-k*up.w/2,(y0+y1)/2-k*up.h/2];}
  if(up.pdf&&up.ratio&&up.ptpx){const k=0.352778*up.ptpx*up.ratio;return [k,0,0,k,0,0];}const k=12000/up.w;return [k,0,0,k,0,0];}
UL.startBlank=()=>{const up=TS.up;if(!up)return;if(STATE.locked){toast('This project is locked at revision 0: start a new project for a new plan');return;}
  const m=startM(up);const T=TR();T.floors=[newFloor('Level 1',up,m,'upload',up.name)];T.cur=0;const f=FL();f.cal=!!(up.pdf&&up.ratio);
  STATE.file=up.name;STATE.walls=[];STATE.items=[];STATE.carp=[];STATE.progAt=[];STATE.floorAt=[];STATE.log=[];STATE.rev=0;STATE.M=null;STATE.locked=false;STATE.hist=[];STATE.redo=[];STATE.selWall=null;
  TS.up=null;TS.sel=null;TS.tool='select';TS.cal=f.cal?null:{kind:2,pts:[]};fitView();STATE.cur=2;STATE.maxSeen=Math.max(STATE.maxSeen,2);autosave();render();try{window.scrollTo({top:0});}catch(e){}
  toast(f.cal?'Drawing scale applied: trace the walls':'Step 1: click both ends of a known dimension to set the scale');};
UL.useAsUL=()=>{const up=TS.up;if(!up)return;const T=TR();const b=wallsBox();const m=up.pdf&&up.ratio?startM(up):startM(up,b);
  const cur=FL();const nf=newFloor(cur?cur.name:'Level 1',up,m,'upload',up.name);if(cur){nf.id=cur.id;nf.model=cur.model;nf.name=cur.name;T.floors[T.cur]=nf;}else{T.floors=[nf];T.cur=0;}
  TS.up=null;fitView();STATE.cur=STATE.locked?3:2;STATE.sub='walls';STATE.maxSeen=Math.max(STATE.maxSeen,STATE.cur);autosave();render();try{window.scrollTo({top:0});}catch(e){}toast('Plan placed under the walls: calibrate it in the check card');};
UL.floorFile=async inp=>{const file=inp.files&&inp.files[0];if(!file)return;const up=await takeFile(file);if(!up){render();return;}const f=FL();if(!f){TS.up=up;UL.useAsUL();return;}
  Object.assign(f,{src:up.src,w:up.w,h:up.h,file:up.name,page:up.page||1,origin:'upload'});const m=startM(up,wallsBox());f.m=m;f.m0=m.slice();f.cal=false;DEVC.clear();fitView();autosave();render();toast(f.name+': plan placed, now calibrate it');};
UL.floorPage=async n=>{const f=FL();if(!TS.pdf||!f)return;TS.busy=true;render();try{const img=await pdfPage(+n);Object.assign(f,{src:img.src,w:img.w,h:img.h,page:+n});const m=startM(img,wallsBox());f.m=m;f.m0=m.slice();f.cal=false;DEVC.clear();fitView();}catch(e){toast('Could not render that page');}TS.busy=false;autosave();render();};
UL.sendRead=()=>{if(!TS.up)return;TS.up.queued=true;try{if(W.parent!==W)W.parent.postMessage({type:'space:read-request',file:TS.up.name,page:TS.up.page||1},'*');}catch(e){}render();};
UL.clearUp=()=>{TS.up=null;render();};
UL.sendNow=()=>{const T=TR();toast(T.outbox.length?T.outbox.length+' correction'+(T.outbox.length===1?'':'s')+' queued for SPACE':'Nothing queued yet');};

/* ---------- panels ---------- */
function devPanel(){const f=FL();if(!f||!f.src)return '<p class="note" style="margin:0">Upload the plan to check the walls against it.</p>';if(!lumFor(f))return '<p class="note" style="margin:0">Reading the plan image…</p>';
  const T=TR();const ws=STATE.walls.filter(checkable);const st=ws.map(w=>[w,status(w)]);const ok=st.filter(x=>x[1]==='ok').length,off=st.filter(x=>x[1]==='off'),miss=st.filter(x=>x[1]==='miss');
  const rows=[...off,...miss].slice(0,14).map(([w,s])=>{const r=devOf(w);return `<tr><td><a href="#" onclick="UL.pick('${w.id}');return false" style="color:var(--ink);font-family:var(--mono)">${w.id}</a></td><td style="color:${CHK[s]}">${s==='off'?(r.off>0?'+':'')+r.off+' mm':'not on the plan'}</td><td style="text-align:right">${s==='off'?`<button class="sm" onclick="UL.snapW('${w.id}')">Snap to plan</button>`:`<button class="sm danger" onclick="UL.del('${w.id}')">Remove</button>`}</td></tr>`;}).join('');
  return `<div class="trstats"><div><b style="color:${CHK.ok}">${ok}</b><span>within ${T.tol} mm</span></div><div><b style="color:${CHK.off}">${off.length}</b><span>off the plan</span></div><div><b style="color:${CHK.miss}">${miss.length}</b><span>not found</span></div></div>
   ${off.length>Math.max(3,ws.length*0.4)?`<p class="note" style="margin:0 0 6px;color:#9A6A2E"><b>Most walls are off.</b> The plan is probably not calibrated yet: set the scale or align it first, then snap what is left.</p>`:''}${rows?`<table class="trtbl"><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table>${off.length>1&&off.length<=Math.max(3,ws.length*0.4)?`<div class="row" style="margin:8px 0 0"><button class="sm pri" onclick="UL.snapAll()">Snap all ${off.length} to the plan</button></div>`:''}`:`<p class="note" style="margin:6px 0 0;color:var(--moss)">${ws.length?'Every wall sits on the plan line.':'No walls yet: trace them with Add wall.'}</p>`}`;}
function selPanel(){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===TS.sel);if(!w)return '';const r=devOf(w);const s=status(w);
  return `<div class="trsel"><div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between;margin:0 0 6px"><b style="font-family:var(--mono)">${w.id}</b><button class="sm" onclick="UL.pick(null)">close</button></div>
   <div class="kv"><span>Position</span><b>${escH(geo(w))}</b></div><div class="kv"><span>Thickness</span><b>${w.t} mm</b></div>
   <div class="kv"><span>Against the plan</span><b style="color:${CHK[s]}">${s==='na'?(w.state==='new'?'new design wall':'no plan to check'):(s==='miss'?'no line found within 300 mm':(r.off>0?'+':'')+r.off+' mm'+(s==='ok'?' · within tolerance':''))}</b></div>
   <div class="row" style="margin:10px 0 0;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap">${r&&r.found&&r.off?`<button class="sm pri" onclick="UL.snapW('${w.id}')">Snap to plan</button>`:''}<span style="display:inline-flex;gap:4px;align-items:center"><input id="trMv" type="number" step="5" placeholder="mm" style="width:70px;padding:4px 6px"/><button class="sm" onclick="UL.moveBy('${w.id}')">Move</button></span><button class="sm" onclick="UL.tool('split')">Split…</button><button class="sm danger" onclick="UL.del('${w.id}')">Remove from reading</button></div>
   <p class="note" style="margin:8px 0 0">Drag the wall on the plan to move it. Remove means SPACE read a wall that is not there: it is not a demolition.</p></div>`;}
function calPanel(f){const c=TS.cal;
  if(c&&c.kind===2)return `<div class="trcal"><b>Two-point scale</b><p class="note" style="margin:4px 0 8px">${c.pts.length<2?`Click point ${c.pts.length+1} of 2 on the plan: both ends of a dimension, or the two jambs of a door.`:'Now type the real distance between P1 and P2.'}</p>${c.pts.length===2?`<div class="row" style="gap:6px;margin:0"><input id="trReal" type="number" min="10" step="1" placeholder="e.g. 900" style="width:110px"/><span class="note" style="margin:0">mm</span><button class="sm pri" onclick="UL.apply2()">Apply</button><button class="sm" onclick="UL.calCancel()">Cancel</button></div>`:`<button class="sm" onclick="UL.calCancel()">Cancel</button>`}</div>`;
  if(c&&c.kind===3){if(c.pts.length<3)return `<div class="trcal"><b>Three-point align</b><p class="note" style="margin:4px 0 8px">Click corner ${'ABC'[c.pts.length]} on the plan (${c.pts.length} of 3). Pick three corners far apart that form a triangle.</p><button class="sm" onclick="UL.calCancel()">Cancel</button></div>`;
    return `<div class="trcal"><b>Three-point align</b><p class="note" style="margin:4px 0 8px">Where do A, B and C really sit? ${STATE.walls.length?'Prefilled with the nearest wall corners':'Prefilled: A at 0,0, B along one wall, C along the wall at right angles'}. Edit any value, in mm.</p>
     ${[0,1,2].map(i=>`<div class="row" style="gap:6px;margin:0 0 4px;align-items:center"><b style="width:14px">${'ABC'[i]}</b>x <input id="trq${i}x" type="number" value="${c.tgt[i][0]}" style="width:92px"/> y <input id="trq${i}y" type="number" value="${c.tgt[i][1]}" style="width:92px"/></div>`).join('')}
     <div class="row" style="gap:6px;margin:6px 0 0"><button class="sm pri" onclick="UL.apply3()">Align</button><button class="sm" onclick="UL.calCancel()">Cancel</button></div></div>`;}
  const dis=f.locked?'disabled':'';

function recentCorr(){const L=STATE.log.filter(l=>/^Read (correction|calibration)/.test(l.action)).slice(-5).reverse();if(!L.length)return '';

function floorTabs(){const T=TR();if(!T.floors.length)return '';return `<div class="trtabs">${T.floors.map((f,i)=>`<button class="${i===T.cur?'on':''}" onclick="UL.floor(${i})">${escH(f.name)}${f.src?'':' · no plan'}</button>`).join('')}<button onclick="UL.addFloor()">+ Add floor</button></div>`;}
function uploadBlock(){const up=TS.up;if(TS.busy&&!up)return '<p class="note" style="color:var(--moss)">Opening the plan…</p>';if(!up)return '';
  const walls=STATE.walls.length>0;
  return `<div class="ulup"><div class="ulthumb"><img src="${up.src}" alt="Uploaded floor plan"/></div><div class="ulmeta"><b>${escH(up.name)}</b><span>${up.w} × ${up.h} px${up.pdf?' · PDF page '+(up.page||1)+' of '+up.pages:''}</span>
    ${up.pdf&&up.pages>1?`<label class="note" style="margin:6px 0 0;display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center">Page <select onchange="UL.page(this.value)" style="width:auto">${Array.from({length:up.pages},(_,i)=>`<option value="${i+1}" ${up.page==i+1?'selected':''}>${i+1}</option>`).join('')}</select> one floor per page</label>`:''}
    ${up.pdf?`<label class="note" style="margin:6px 0 0;display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center">Drawing scale <select onchange="UL.ratio(this.value)" style="width:auto"><option value="0">not sure: calibrate</option>${[50,75,100,150,200].map(r=>`<option value="${r}" ${up.ratio==r?'selected':''}>1:${r}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>`:''}
    ${up.queued?`<p class="note" style="color:var(--moss);margin:8px 0 0"><b>Queued for Read.</b> Corvic returns the walls and the scale, and SPACE lines this plan up under them automatically. The 40 credits are charged when the walls come back, not now.</p>`:''}
    <div class="row" style="gap:8px;margin:10px 0 0;flex-wrap:wrap"><button class="pri" onclick="UL.startBlank()" ${STATE.locked?'disabled':''}>Trace it myself · free</button>${walls?`<button onclick="UL.useAsUL()">Use as the underlay for the current walls</button>`:''}${up.queued?'':`<button onclick="UL.sendRead()">Send to Read · 40 credits</button>`}<button class="sm" onclick="UL.clearUp()">Remove</button></div>
    <p class="note" style="margin:8px 0 0">${STATE.locked?'This project is locked at revision 0, so it cannot be re-traced: use the plan as its underlay, or start a new project.':(walls?'Trace it myself starts a blank reading of this plan and replaces the current walls.':'Trace it myself puts the plan in Confirm: set the scale, draw the walls over it, lock.')}</p></div></div>`;}

const _rd=SCREEN.read;SCREEN.read=function(){if(!STATE.M)return _rd();const f=FL();let conf,low,how,matched=null;
  const ws=STATE.walls.filter(checkable);
  if(f&&f.src&&lumFor(f)&&f.origin!=='sample'){const st=ws.map(w=>[w,status(w)]).filter(x=>x[1]!=='na');const ok=st.filter(x=>x[1]==='ok').length;conf=st.length?Math.round(100*ok/st.length):0;low=st.filter(x=>x[1]!=='ok').map(x=>x[0].id);matched=[ok,st.length];how='measured wall by wall against your uploaded plan';}
  else{const r=readStats();conf=r.conf;low=r.low;how=f&&f.origin==='sample'?'sample plan, pre-read by SPACE':'reported by the reader';}

    <div class="kv"><span>Walls ${matched?'on the plan line':'read'}</span><b>${matched?matched[0]+' of '+matched[1]:ws.length}</b></div>

    <div class="kv"><span>Scale</span><b>${f&&f.src?escH(scaleTxt(f)):(STATE.kind==='HDB'?'5.0 mm / px · from an 800 mm door':'from the dimension text')}</b></div>

    <div class="row" style="margin-top:14px"><button class="pri" onclick="go(2)">Check and correct →</button></div>
    <p class="note">SPACE reads fast, never perfectly. Nothing becomes the model until you check it in Confirm and lock it.</p></div>
   <div class="card"><h3>What SPACE read</h3><p class="sub">${low.length?'flagged walls tagged on the plan':'every wall shown as read'}</p>
    <div class="plan-wrap">${planSVG({mode:'changes',clickable:false,walltags:low.length?low:true})}</div></div></div>`;};
W.UL=UL;

/* ===================== SPACE Home: the phone version for Homeowner pass and Explore =====================
   Same model underneath as SPACE Studio. On a phone, Homeowner and Explore get five simple steps:
   My home, Try walls, Furnish, Style, My ideas. Designer, Studio and Enterprise get a view-only screen
   with an "Edit anyway" way out. Tablets and laptops keep the full Studio. */
(function(){
'use strict';
const W=window;
const H={tab:'home',sel:null,force:false,cmp:[],up:null,name:''};
const PRO=['designer','studio','enterprise','commercial'];
const LIMIT={explore:1,homeowner:10};
const escH=s=>String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'})[c]);
const plan=()=>STATE.plan||'';
const isPhone=()=>W.innerWidth<=700;
function mode(){if(!isPhone()||H.force)return 'full';return PRO.includes(plan())?'view':'home';}
const RATE={ward:[200,320],kit:[200,320]}; /* S$ per foot run, Singapore mid-market ranges, Sep 2026 */
const money=v=>'S$'+(Math.round(v/10)*10).toLocaleString();
const IC={home:'<path d="M4 11l8-7 8 7v9h-5v-6H9v6H4z"/>',walls:'<path d="M4 5h16M4 5v14h16V5M12 5v6M12 15v4"/>',furn:'<path d="M4 12h16v5H4zM6 12V8h12v4M6 17v2M18 17v2"/>',style:'<path d="M12 3a9 9 0 100 18c1.5 0 2-1 2-2s-1-2 0-3 3 0 4-1 3-2 3-5a9 9 0 00-9-7z"/><circle cx="8" cy="10" r="1.2"/><circle cx="12" cy="7" r="1.2"/><circle cx="16" cy="10" r="1.2"/>',ideas:'<path d="M9 18h6M10 21h4M12 3a6 6 0 00-4 10c1 1 1 2 1 3h6c0-1 0-2 1-3a6 6 0 00-4-10z"/>'};
const svgI=k=>`<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="22" height="22" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round" aria-hidden="true">${IC[k]}</svg>`;

/* ---------- root and switching ---------- */

  if(m==='full')return _render.apply(this,arguments);

  try{renderChip();}catch(e){}};

function homeScreen(){lastM='home';const has=!!STATE.M;const t=has?H.tab:'home';
  const tabs=[['home','My home'],['walls','Try walls'],['furn','Furnish'],['style','Style'],['ideas','My ideas']];
  const body={home:tabHome,walls:tabWalls,furn:tabFurn,style:tabStyle,ideas:tabIdeas}[t]();
  return `<header class="shh"><div><b>SPACE Home</b><span>${escH(STATE.project?STATE.project.name:(STATE.file||'Your home'))}</span></div>${has?`<button class="shundo" onclick="undo()" ${STATE.hist&&STATE.hist.length?'':'disabled'} aria-label="Undo">↶ Undo</button>`:''}</header>
   <main class="shb">${body}</main>
   <nav class="shnav">${tabs.map(([k,l])=>`<button class="${t===k?'on':''}" ${!has&&k!=='home'?'disabled':''} onclick="SH.tab('${k}')">${svgI(k)}<span>${l}</span></button>`).join('')}</nav>
   ${H.sel&&t==='walls'?wallSheet():''}`;}

function roomRange(r){const ft=v=>v/304.8;const B=r.bbox;const L=Math.max(B.x1-B.x0,B.y1-B.y0);
  if(/Bedroom/.test(r.program)){const run=Math.min(ft(L)*0.6,9);return ['Wardrobe, about '+Math.round(run)+' ft',run*RATE.ward[0],run*RATE.ward[1]];}
  if(r.program==='Kitchen'){const run=Math.min(ft(L),14);return ['Kitchen cabinets, about '+Math.round(run)+' ft',run*RATE.kit[0],run*RATE.kit[1]];}
  return null;}

function costRanges(){const M=STATE.M;let lo=0,hi=0;const rows=[];

const SH={};
SH.tab=k=>{H.tab=k;H.sel=null;STATE.hlWall=null;render();try{root().querySelector('.shb').scrollTop=0;window.scrollTo(0,0);}catch(e){}};
SH.desk=()=>{H.force=true;render();toast('Full editor: best on a tablet or laptop');};
SH.sample=k=>{H.tab='home';loadSample(k);};
SH.photo=inp=>{const f=inp.files&&inp.files[0];if(!f)return;H.up=f.name;try{if(W.parent!==W)W.parent.postMessage({type:'space:read-request',file:f.name,page:1},'*');}catch(e){}H.tab='home';render();};
SH.tap=e=>{const svg=e.currentTarget.querySelector('svg');if(!svg||!STATE.M)return;const pt=svg.createSVGPoint();pt.x=e.clientX;pt.y=e.clientY;const q=pt.matrixTransform(svg.getScreenCTM().inverse());
  const B=allBounds(),pad=44,vb=svg.viewBox.baseVal;const s=Math.min((vb.width-pad*2)/(B.x1-B.x0),(vb.height-pad*2)/(B.y1-B.y0));const x=B.x0+(q.x-pad)/s,y=B.y0+(q.y-pad)/s;
  let best=null,bd=18/s;for(const w of STATE.walls){const [[ax,ay],[bx,by]]=wpts(w);const dx=bx-ax,dy=by-ay,L=dx*dx+dy*dy||1;let t=((x-ax)*dx+(y-ay)*dy)/L;t=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t));const d=Math.hypot(x-(ax+t*dx),y-(ay+t*dy));if(d<bd){bd=d;best=w;}}
  H.sel=best?best.id:null;STATE.hlWall=H.sel;render();};
SH.close=()=>{H.sel=null;STATE.hlWall=null;render();};
SH.wall=a=>{const id=H.sel;H.sel=null;STATE.hlWall=null;if(a==='restore')restore(id);else demolish(id);};
SH.reset=()=>{snap();let n=0;STATE.walls=STATE.walls.filter(w=>{if(w.state==='new'){n++;return false;}return true;});STATE.walls.forEach(w=>{if(w.state==='demolished'){w.state='existing';n++;}});logIt('Restore','all walls','changed','as built',n+' wall'+(n===1?'':'s')+' put back');recompute('Every wall is back');};
SH.room=id=>{H.room=id;render();};
SH.set=()=>{if(H.room)applySet(H.room);};
SH.clear=()=>{if(H.room)clearRoom(H.room);};
SH.style=(id,lib)=>{if(lib)applyStyle(id);else setTheme(id);};
SH.renders=()=>{const vs=views();if(!vs.length)return;const P=STATE.photo||(STATE.photo={sel:[],done:null});P.sel=vs.slice(0,3);try{prRun();}catch(e){toast('Renders are not available here');}};
SH.nm=v=>{H.name=v;};
SH.save=()=>{const I=STATE.ideas||(STATE.ideas=[]);const lim=LIMIT[plan()]||(plan()?99:10);if(I.length>=lim){toast(plan()==='explore'?'Explore keeps 1 idea. The Homeowner pass keeps up to 10.':'You have saved '+lim+' ideas: delete one to save another');return;}
  const M=STATE.M,c=costRanges();const name=(H.name||'').trim()||'Idea '+(I.length+1);
  I.push({id:'I'+Date.now().toString(36),name,ts:Date.now(),data:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify({walls:STATE.walls,items:STATE.items,progAt:STATE.progAt,floorAt:STATE.floorAt,paint:STATE.paint||null,theme:STATE.theme})),
    svg:planSVG({mode:'proposed',furn:true,nolabel:true,w:320,h:220,id:'idea-'+I.length}),stats:{rooms:M.rooms.length,area:(M.area/1e6).toFixed(0),dem:M.dem.length,lo:c.lo,hi:c.hi}});
  H.name='';logIt('Idea saved',name,'-',M.rooms.length+' rooms','SPACE Home');autosave();render();toast('Saved: '+name);};
SH.open=id=>{const x=(STATE.ideas||[]).find(i=>i.id===id);if(!x)return;snap();const d=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x.data));STATE.walls=d.walls;STATE.items=d.items;STATE.progAt=d.progAt;STATE.floorAt=d.floorAt;STATE.paint=d.paint;STATE.theme=d.theme||STATE.theme;logIt('Idea opened',x.name,'-','-','SPACE Home');recompute('Opened: '+x.name);};
SH.del=id=>{STATE.ideas=(STATE.ideas||[]).filter(i=>i.id!==id);H.cmp=H.cmp.filter(i=>i!==id);autosave();render();};
SH.cmp=id=>{if(H.cmp.includes(id))H.cmp=H.cmp.filter(i=>i!==id);else{H.cmp.push(id);if(H.cmp.length>2)H.cmp.shift();}render();if(H.cmp.length===2)try{root().querySelector('.shcmp').scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'});}catch(e){}};
SH.cmpClear=()=>{H.cmp=[];render();};
SH.share=()=>{const M=STATE.M;const c=costRanges();const txt=`My SPACE idea for ${STATE.file||'my home'}: ${M.rooms.length} rooms, ${M.dem.length} wall${M.dem.length===1?'':'s'} knocked down, ${STATE.paint&&STATE.paint.style?STATE.paint.style+' style, ':''}rough cost ${money(c.lo)} to ${money(c.hi)}. Planned in SPACE.`;
  if(navigator.share){navigator.share({title:'My SPACE idea',text:txt}).catch(()=>{});return;}
  try{navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt).then(()=>toast('Summary copied: paste it into a message'),()=>toast(txt));}catch(e){toast(txt);}};
W.SH=SH;

/* ---------- keep ideas and the plan with the project ---------- */
const _pd=W.projectData;W.projectData=projectData=function(){const d=_pd.apply(this,arguments);if(STATE.ideas&&STATE.ideas.length)d.ideas=STATE.ideas;return d;};
const _op=W.openProject;W.openProject=openProject=function(id){const p=(myProjects().find(x=>x.id===id))||null;STATE.ideas=(p&&p.data&&p.data.ideas)||[];H.cmp=[];H.sel=null;return _op.apply(this,arguments);};
const _np=W.newProject;W.newProject=newProject=function(){STATE.ideas=[];H.cmp=[];H.tab='home';return _np.apply(this,arguments);};

})();

/* ---------- v10: structural detection (walls and pillars) + site view ---------- */
(function(){
const W=window;
const SC_LABEL={S:'Structural',N:'Not structural',C:'Please check'};
const esc=s=>String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'})[c]);

/* ---- classify: thick solid lines are structural, thin outlined walls are not, anything in between asks a person ---- */
function classify(w){
  if(w.state==='new'){w.sc='N';w.scWhy='new wall, drawn by you';return;}
  if(w.scBy&&w.sc){return;}
  if(w.sc&&w.scT===w.t&&w.scE===!!w.ext)return;
  w.scT=w.t;w.scE=!!w.ext;
  if(w.shelter){w.sc='S';w.scWhy='household shelter wall';return;}
  if(w.ext){w.sc='S';w.scWhy='exterior wall';return;}
  if(w.t>=150){w.sc='S';w.scWhy='thick solid line on the plan ('+w.t+' mm)';return;}
  if(w.t<=110){w.sc='N';w.scWhy='thin outlined wall ('+w.t+' mm)';return;}
  w.sc='C';w.scWhy='line thickness unclear ('+w.t+' mm)';
}
function classifyAll(){(STATE.walls||[]).forEach(classify);}
W.stClassify=classifyAll;
const pend=()=>(STATE.walls||[]).filter(w=>w.state!=='demolished'&&w.sc==='C');

/* ---- sample plans: shelter walls are thick RC, one wall is unclear, pillars along the facade ---- */
const PIL=[[0,0],[1,0],[0,1],[1,1],[6400/11000,0],[3800/11000,1],[8200/11000,1],[1,4400/8400]];
function applySample(s){const progOf=(x,y)=>{const r=s.rects.find(r=>x>r.r[0]&&x<r.r[2]&&y>r.r[1]&&y<r.r[3]);return r?r.p:null;};
  const xs=s.rects.flatMap(r=>[r.r[0],r.r[2]]),ys=s.rects.flatMap(r=>[r.r[1],r.r[3]]);const x0=Math.min(...xs),x1=Math.max(...xs),y0=Math.min(...ys),y1=Math.max(...ys);
  STATE.walls.forEach(w=>{if(w.state==='new'||w.ext)return;const c=(w.a+w.b)/2,o=140;const P=w.axis==='x'?[[c,w.fixed-o],[c,w.fixed+o]]:[[w.fixed-o,c],[w.fixed+o,c]];const ps=P.map(p=>progOf(p[0],p[1]));
    if(ps.some(p=>p&&/Shelter/.test(p))){w.t=200;w.shelter=true;}else if(ps.includes('Bedroom 2')&&ps.includes('Bedroom 3'))w.t=125;});
  STATE.pillars=PIL.map(([fx,fy],i)=>{const S=400;let x=x0+fx*(x1-x0),y=y0+fy*(y1-y0);x=Math.min(Math.max(x,x0+S/2),x1-S/2);y=Math.min(Math.max(y,y0+S/2),y1-S/2);return {id:'P'+(i+1),x,y,s:S};});
  STATE.structOK=null;STATE.site=null;}
const sampleOf=()=>Object.values(SAMPLES).find(x=>x.label===STATE.file);
const _ls=W.loadSample;W.loadSample=loadSample=function(key){const r=_ls.apply(this,arguments);const s=SAMPLES[key];if(s){applySample(s);classifyAll();_rc0();}return r;};
/* ---- keep classification current ---- */
const _rc=W.recompute;W.recompute=recompute=function(){try{classifyAll();}catch(e){}return _rc.apply(this,arguments);};
function _rc0(){W.recompute();}

/* ---- save and reopen ---- */
const _pd=W.projectData;W.projectData=projectData=function(){const d=_pd.apply(this,arguments);d.pillars=STATE.pillars||[];d.structOK=STATE.structOK||null;d.site=STATE.site||null;return d;};
const _op=W.openProject;W.openProject=openProject=function(id){const p=(myProjects().find(x=>x.id===id))||null;const d=p&&p.data||{};STATE.pillars=d.pillars||[];STATE.structOK=d.structOK||null;STATE.site=d.site||null;return _op.apply(this,arguments);};

/* ---- guards: structural walls are never hacked; unclear ones wait for a person ---- */
function refuse(w,what){
  const msg=w.sc==='S'?`${w.id} is structural (${w.scWhy}). Do not hack. Check with HDB before any change.`:`SPACE is not sure ${w.id} is non-structural. Decide it in Confirm first, or ask your designer or HDB.`;
  logIt('Refused',w.id,SC_LABEL[w.sc].toLowerCase(),'unchanged',what+': '+(w.sc==='S'?'structural, needs HDB or PE approval':'structure not confirmed'));STATE.rev++;toast(msg);render();}
const _dm=W.demolish;W.demolish=demolish=function(id){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===id);if(w&&w.state!=='new'&&(w.sc==='S'||w.sc==='C')&&!w.ext){refuse(w,'knock down');return;}return _dm.apply(this,arguments);};
const _st=W.setType;W.setType=setType=function(id,type){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===id);if(w&&type!=='full'&&w.state!=='new'&&(w.sc==='S'||w.sc==='C')&&!w.ext){refuse(w,'change to '+type);return;}return _st.apply(this,arguments);};
if(typeof W.addOpen==='function'){const _ao=W.addOpen;W.addOpen=addOpen=function(id,kind){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===id);if(w&&w.state!=='new'&&(w.sc==='S'||w.sc==='C')){refuse(w,'new '+kind);return;}return _ao.apply(this,arguments);};}

/* ---- human confirmation ---- */
W.stSet=function(id,v){const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!w)return;const b=SC_LABEL[w.sc]||'unread';w.sc=v;w.scBy='you';w.scAt=new Date().toISOString();w.scWhy=v==='S'?'confirmed structural by you':'confirmed not structural by you';
  logIt('Structure',id,b.toLowerCase(),SC_LABEL[v].toLowerCase(),'confirmed by a person in Confirm');STATE.rev++;try{autosave();}catch(e){}render();};
W.stOK=function(){if(pend().length){toast('Decide the walls marked Please check first');return;}STATE.structOK={at:new Date().toISOString(),S:STATE.walls.filter(w=>w.sc==='S'&&w.state!=='demolished').length,P:(STATE.pillars||[]).length};
  logIt('Structure','plan','flagged by SPACE','confirmed by you',STATE.structOK.S+' structural walls, '+STATE.structOK.P+' pillars');STATE.rev++;try{autosave();}catch(e){}render();toast('Structure confirmed. Structural walls stay locked in Design.');};
W.stShow=function(v){STATE.stShow=v;render();};
const _lp=W.lockPlan;W.lockPlan=lockPlan=function(){if(!STATE.locked&&pend().length){toast('Decide the walls marked Please check before locking');go(2);return;}if(!STATE.locked&&!STATE.structOK&&STATE.walls.length){W.stOK();}return _lp.apply(this,arguments);};

function card(){const live=(STATE.walls||[]).filter(w=>w.state!=='demolished');if(!live.length)return '';
  const S=live.filter(w=>w.sc==='S'),N=live.filter(w=>w.sc==='N'),C=live.filter(w=>w.sc==='C'),P=STATE.pillars||[];const ok=STATE.structOK&&!C.length;
  return `<div class="card stcard" style="margin-top:18px"><div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap"><div><h3>Structure check</h3><p class="sub">SPACE flags every structural wall and pillar it finds. Nothing is locked until a person confirms it.</p></div>
   <span class="tag ${ok?'t-new':'t-warn'}">${ok?'Confirmed by you':C.length?C.length+' to check':'Waiting for you'}</span></div>
   <div class="stleg"><span><i class="s"></i>Structural: thick solid line, do not hack <b>${S.length}</b></span><span><i class="p"></i>Pillar <b>${P.length}</b></span><span><i class="c"></i>Please check <b>${C.length}</b></span><span><i class="n"></i>Not structural: thin outlined wall <b>${N.length}</b></span></div>
   ${C.length?`<p class="note" style="margin:10px 0 6px"><b>Please check.</b> The line is neither clearly thick nor thin. Look at the original plan and decide.</p>${C.map(w=>`<div class="kv stq"><span>${w.id} · ${mm(wlen(w))} · ${esc(w.scWhy)}</span><b><button class="sm" onclick="stSet('${w.id}','S')">Structural</button> <button class="sm" onclick="stSet('${w.id}','N')">Not structural</button></b></div>`).join('')}`:''}
   ${S.length?`<details style="margin-top:8px"><summary class="note" style="cursor:pointer">Structural walls (${S.length})</summary>${S.map(w=>`<div class="kv"><span>${w.id} · ${mm(wlen(w))}</span><b>${esc(w.scWhy)}${w.scBy?'':` <button class="sm" onclick="stSet('${w.id}','C')" title="Send back to Please check">Not sure</button>`}</b></div>`).join('')}</details>`:''}
   <p class="note" style="margin-top:10px">Structural walls and pillars cannot be hacked in Design. Any change to them needs approval from HDB, or a professional engineer for condos and landed homes.</p>
   <div class="row" style="gap:8px;margin-top:10px;flex-wrap:wrap"><button class="${ok?'sm':'pri sm'}" onclick="stOK()" ${C.length?'disabled':''}>${ok?'Confirmed':'I have checked the structure'}</button><button class="sm" onclick="stShow(${STATE.stShow===false?'true':'false'})">${STATE.stShow===false?'Show':'Hide'} structure colours</button></div></div>`;}
const _cf=SCREEN.confirm;SCREEN.confirm=function(){const h=_cf.apply(this,arguments);if(!STATE.M)return h;const i=h.indexOf('<div class="card trcard"');return i>0?h.slice(0,i)+card()+h.slice(i):h+card();};

/* wall panel in Design: say why a wall is locked */
const _ed=SCREEN.edit;SCREEN.edit=function(){let h=_ed.apply(this,arguments);const w=STATE.walls.find(x=>x.id===STATE.selWall);
  if(w&&w.sc&&w.state!=='new'){const tag=`<div class="stban ${w.sc}"><b>${SC_LABEL[w.sc]}</b> · ${esc(w.scWhy)}${w.sc==='S'?'. Do not hack. Check with HDB before any change.':w.sc==='C'?'. Decide it in Confirm before changing it.':'. You can knock it down or change it.'}</div>`;
    h=h.replace(/(<button class="sm" onclick="STATE\.selWall=null;render\(\)">close<\/button><\/div>)/,'$1'+tag);}
  return h;};

/* ---- plan layer: pillars always; wall colours in Confirm, structural walls in Design ---- */
W.stPlanLayer=function(X,Y,s,o){const out=[];if(!STATE.M)return '';
  const show=STATE.stShow!==false&&(STATE.cur===2||(STATE.cur===3&&STATE.sub!=='space'));
  if(show){for(const w of STATE.walls){if(w.state==='demolished'||!w.sc)continue;if(STATE.cur===3&&w.sc==='N')continue;
    const [[x1,y1],[x2,y2]]=wpts(w);const lw=Math.max(2.5,w.t*s);
    const col=w.sc==='S'?'#B4533D':w.sc==='C'?'#D69A1E':'#4F8C80';
    out.push(`<line x1="${X(x1)}" y1="${Y(y1)}" x2="${X(x2)}" y2="${Y(y2)}" stroke="${col}" stroke-width="${w.sc==='N'?Math.max(2,lw*.6):lw+3}" opacity="${w.sc==='N'?.75:STATE.cur===3?.45:.7}" ${w.sc==='C'?'stroke-dasharray="7 5"':''} stroke-linecap="butt" pointer-events="none"/>`);}}
  for(const p of STATE.pillars||[]){const h=p.s/2;out.push(`<g pointer-events="none"><rect x="${X(p.x-h)}" y="${Y(p.y-h)}" width="${p.s*s}" height="${p.s*s}" fill="#2F302F" stroke="#B4533D" stroke-width="${show?1.6:0}"/><line x1="${X(p.x-h)}" y1="${Y(p.y+h)}" x2="${X(p.x+h)}" y2="${Y(p.y-h)}" stroke="#8C8A86" stroke-width="1"/></g>`);}
  return out.join('');};

/* =====================================================================
   SITE VIEW: stand in the room at eye level (1.6 m) and look at one wall
   ===================================================================== */
const FAM={couple:[['Adult',1720],['Adult',1600]],three:[['Adult',1720],['Adult',1600],['Child',1150]],four:[['Adult',1720],['Adult',1600],['Teen',1550],['Child',1150]],five:[['Adult',1720],['Adult',1600],['Teen',1550],['Child',1150],['Child',950]],elder:[['Adult',1720],['Adult',1600],['Parent',1600],['Parent',1520]]};
const FAML={couple:'Couple',three:'Family of 3',four:'Family of 4',five:'Family of 5',elder:'With elderly parents'};
const EYE=1600,CEIL_MIN=2400;
function site(){if(!STATE.site)STATE.site={wall:'N',fam:'couple',people:FAM.couple.map(p=>({n:p[0],h:p[1]})),ceil:H_FULL,drop:0,fan:false};return STATE.site;}
W.svSet=function(k,v){const S=site();if(k==='fam'){S.fam=v;S.people=FAM[v].map(p=>({n:p[0],h:p[1]}));}else if(k==='h'){const [i,val]=v;S.people[i].h=Math.max(800,Math.min(2100,Math.round(+val||0)));}else S[k]=v;try{autosave();}catch(e){}render();};

function backWall(r,side){const b=r.bbox;// facing `side`: returns width, depth, left-to-right mapping
  if(side==='N')return {w:b.x1-b.x0,d:b.y1-b.y0,u:(x,y)=>x-b.x0,on:w=>w.axis==='x'&&Math.abs(w.fixed-b.y0)<2,rng:w=>[w.a-b.x0,w.b-b.x0],dist:(x,y)=>y-b.y0,flip:false};
  if(side==='S')return {w:b.x1-b.x0,d:b.y1-b.y0,u:(x,y)=>b.x1-x,on:w=>w.axis==='x'&&Math.abs(w.fixed-b.y1)<2,rng:w=>[b.x1-w.b,b.x1-w.a],dist:(x,y)=>b.y1-y};
  if(side==='E')return {w:b.y1-b.y0,d:b.x1-b.x0,u:(x,y)=>y-b.y0,on:w=>w.axis==='y'&&Math.abs(w.fixed-b.x1)<2,rng:w=>[w.a-b.y0,w.b-b.y0],dist:(x,y)=>b.x1-x};
  return {w:b.y1-b.y0,d:b.x1-b.x0,u:(x,y)=>b.y1-y,on:w=>w.axis==='y'&&Math.abs(w.fixed-b.x0)<2,rng:w=>[b.y1-w.b,b.y1-w.a],dist:(x,y)=>x-b.x0};}

function figure(x,base,h,sc,col,label){// simple standing figure, feet at (x,base), height h px
  const hd=h*.13,sh=base-h*.82,hip=base-h*.48;const w=h*.13;
  return `<g><circle cx="${x}" cy="${base-h+hd/2}" r="${hd/2}" fill="${col}"/><path d="M${x-w} ${sh} Q${x} ${sh-h*.03} ${x+w} ${sh} L${x+w*.8} ${hip} L${x+w*.55} ${base} L${x+w*.12} ${base} L${x} ${hip+h*.05} L${x-w*.12} ${base} L${x-w*.55} ${base} L${x-w*.8} ${hip} Z" fill="${col}"/><text x="${x}" y="${base+13}" font-size="10" text-anchor="middle" fill="#5B5A57" font-family="IBM Plex Mono,monospace">${esc(label)}</text></g>`;}

function siteSVG(r){const S=site();const B=backWall(r,S.wall);const ceil=S.ceil-(+S.drop||0);
  const VW=640,VH=400,f=300,cx=VW/2,cy=VH*0.52;// camera 400 mm in from the opposite wall, eye at 1.6 m
  const cam=B.w/2,zb=B.d+400;const P=(u,y,z)=>[cx+f*(u-cam)/z,cy-f*(y-EYE)/z];
  const k=f/zb;const bx=u=>cx+(u-cam)*k,by=y=>cy-(y-EYE)*k;
  const out=[];const L=bx(0),R=bx(B.w),T=by(ceil),F=by(0);
  // room box: floor, ceiling, side walls to the frame edge
  const far=(u,y)=>{const z=zb*0.18;return P(u,y,z);};
  const [fl,ft]=far(0,ceil),[fr,frt]=far(B.w,ceil),[fl0,fb]=far(0,0),[fr0,fb2]=far(B.w,0);
  out.push(`<polygon points="${fl},${ft} ${L},${T} ${L},${F} ${fl0},${fb}" fill="#E9E3DA"/>`,`<polygon points="${fr},${frt} ${R},${T} ${R},${F} ${fr0},${fb2}" fill="#E4DDD3"/>`,
    `<polygon points="${fl0},${fb} ${L},${F} ${R},${F} ${fr0},${fb2}" fill="#D9C7B1"/>`,`<polygon points="${fl},${ft} ${L},${T} ${R},${T} ${fr},${frt}" fill="#F7F4EF"/>`,
    `<rect x="${L}" y="${T}" width="${R-L}" height="${F-T}" fill="#F1ECE4" stroke="#8C8A86" stroke-width="1"/>`);
  for(let i=1;i<8;i++){const z=zb*(0.18+i*0.1);if(z>=zb)break;const a=P(0,0,z),b=P(B.w,0,z);out.push(`<line x1="${a[0]}" y1="${a[1]}" x2="${b[0]}" y2="${b[1]}" stroke="#CDB9A2" stroke-width=".6"/>`);}
  if(+S.drop){const T0=by(S.ceil);out.push(`<rect x="${L}" y="${T0}" width="${R-L}" height="${T-T0}" fill="#E6E0D6" stroke="#B9B2A8" stroke-dasharray="3 3"/>`);}
  // openings on the back wall
  for(const w of STATE.walls.filter(active).filter(B.on)){const [ra]=B.rng(w);
    for(const o of w.opens.filter(o=>o.state!=='removed')){let [a,b]=B.rng({a:o.a,b:o.b});const u0=Math.max(0,Math.min(a,b)),u1=Math.min(B.w,Math.max(a,b));if(u1<=u0)continue;
      const lo=o.kind==='window'?(o.sill||900):0,hi=Math.min(o.head||2100,ceil);
      out.push(`<rect x="${bx(u0)}" y="${by(hi)}" width="${(u1-u0)*k}" height="${(hi-lo)*k}" fill="${o.kind==='window'?'#CFE0E6':o.kind==='door'?'#C9A77F':'#E8E1D6'}" stroke="#6E6C68" stroke-width="1"/>`);}}
  // things against the back wall
  const near=STATE.items.filter(it=>inRoom(r,it.x,it.y)).map(it=>({it,b:itemBox(it)})).filter(({b})=>{const d=Math.min(B.dist(b.x0,b.y0),B.dist(b.x1,b.y1));return d<250;});
  const shelves=[];
  for(const {it,b} of near){const us=[B.u(b.x0,b.y0),B.u(b.x1,b.y1)];const u0=Math.min(...us),u1=Math.max(...us);const h=Math.min(b.h||800,ceil);
    const wall=h>=1800;out.push(`<rect x="${bx(u0)}" y="${by(h)}" width="${(u1-u0)*k}" height="${h*k}" fill="${wall?'#B99A81':'#CDB59D'}" stroke="#6E5A48" stroke-width="1" opacity=".92"/><text x="${bx((u0+u1)/2)}" y="${by(h)+12}" font-size="9.5" text-anchor="middle" fill="#2F302F">${esc(nameOf(it))}</text>`);
    if(wall)shelves.push({name:nameOf(it),top:h-100});}
  // ceiling fan in the room centre
  let fanLow=null;if(S.fan){const z=zb-B.d/2,fy=ceil-450;fanLow=fy;const a=P(cam-600,fy,z),b=P(cam+600,fy,z),t=P(cam,ceil,z);out.push(`<line x1="${t[0]}" y1="${t[1]}" x2="${t[0]}" y2="${a[1]}" stroke="#5B5A57" stroke-width="2"/><ellipse cx="${t[0]}" cy="${a[1]}" rx="${(b[0]-a[0])/2}" ry="${(b[0]-a[0])/14}" fill="none" stroke="#5B5A57" stroke-width="2.4"/>`);}
  // eye level
  out.push(`<line x1="0" y1="${cy}" x2="${VW}" y2="${cy}" stroke="#3E8E86" stroke-width="1" stroke-dasharray="6 5"/><text x="8" y="${cy-5}" font-size="10" fill="#3E8E86" font-family="IBM Plex Mono,monospace">eye level 1.60 m</text>`);
  // people, standing 1.2 m in front of the back wall
  const zp=Math.min(zb-250,Math.max(zb-1200,f*EYE/(VH-26-cy)));const n=S.people.length;const cols=['#373837','#6E5A48','#4F6B66','#8A6A55','#5B5A57'];
  S.people.forEach((p,i)=>{const u=B.w*(i+1)/(n+1);const [x,base]=P(u,0,zp);const top=P(u,p.h,zp)[1];out.push(figure(x,base,base-top,1,cols[i%cols.length],(p.h/1000).toFixed(2)+' m'));});
  // ceiling height tag
  out.push(`<text x="${R+6<VW-60?R+6:R-70}" y="${(T+F)/2}" font-size="11" fill="${ceil<CEIL_MIN?'#B4533D':'#2F302F'}" font-family="IBM Plex Mono,monospace">${(ceil/1000).toFixed(2)} m</text>`);
  return {svg:`<svg viewBox="0 0 ${VW} ${VH}" class="svsvg" role="img" aria-label="Site view of ${esc(r.program)} at eye level"><defs><clipPath id="svc"><rect width="${VW}" height="${VH}"/></clipPath></defs><rect width="${VW}" height="${VH}" fill="#FBF9F6"/><g clip-path="url(#svc)">${out.join('')}</g></svg>`,ceil,fanLow,shelves,B};}

function checks(r,R){const S=site();const out=[];const tall=Math.max(...S.people.map(p=>p.h));const short=Math.min(...S.people.filter(p=>p.n!=='Child').map(p=>p.h).concat([tall]));
  out.push([R.ceil>=CEIL_MIN?'ok':'bad',`Floor to ceiling ${(R.ceil/1000).toFixed(2)} m`,R.ceil>=CEIL_MIN?`At or above the 2.40 m minimum.${+S.drop?' The false ceiling is fine.':''}`:`Below the 2.40 m minimum for HDB flats. Reduce the false ceiling drop.`]);
  if(R.fanLow!=null){const gap=R.fanLow-tall;out.push(['info',`Fan blades at about ${(R.fanLow/1000).toFixed(2)} m`,`${gap>0?(gap/1000).toFixed(2)+' m above the tallest person\'s head':'At or below head height of the tallest person'}. Information only: check the fan maker\'s mounting height.`]);}
  const ups=S.people.filter(p=>p.n!=='Child');
  for(const sh of R.shelves){const who=ups.filter(p=>p.h*1.24<sh.top).map(p=>(p.h/1000).toFixed(2)+' m');out.push([who.length?'warn':'ok',`${sh.name}: top shelf about ${(sh.top/1000).toFixed(2)} m`,who.length?`Out of reach for ${who.join(', ')} without a step stool. Consider a pull-down rail or a lower top shelf.`:'Everyone can reach it.']);}
  if(/Kitchen/.test(r.program)){const ideal=Math.round((short*0.63-120)/10)*10;out.push(['info','Worktop height',`Standard 900 mm. For the ${(short/1000).toFixed(2)} m cook, about ${ideal} mm is the comfortable rule of thumb.`]);}
  if(!R.shelves.length&&!/Kitchen/.test(r.program))out.push(['info','Nothing tall on this wall yet','Place a wardrobe or tall unit against this wall to check reach.']);
  return out;}

function siteCard(){if(!STATE.M||!STATE.locked)return '';const r=STATE.M.rooms.find(x=>x.id===STATE.selRoom)||STATE.M.rooms[0];if(!r)return '';const S=site();const R=siteSVG(r);
  return `<div class="card svcard" style="margin-top:18px"><div class="row" style="justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap"><div><h3>Site view · ${esc(r.program)}</h3><p class="sub">stand in the room at eye level (1.60 m) and look at one wall, with your household in it</p></div>
   <div class="tool svwall">${[['N','North'],['E','East'],['S','South'],['W','West']].map(([k,l])=>`<button class="${S.wall===k?'on':''}" onclick="svSet('wall','${k}')">${l} wall</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
   <div class="svgrid"><div>${R.svg}</div><div class="svside">
    <p class="note" style="margin:0 0 6px"><b>Household</b></p><select onchange="svSet('fam',this.value)">${Object.entries(FAML).map(([k,l])=>`<option value="${k}" ${S.fam===k?'selected':''}>${l}</option>`).join('')}</select>
    <div class="svppl">${S.people.map((p,i)=>`<label>${esc(p.n)} <input type="number" min="800" max="2100" step="10" value="${p.h}" onchange="svSet('h',[${i},this.value])"/> mm</label>`).join('')}</div>
    <p class="note" style="margin:10px 0 6px"><b>Ceiling</b></p>
    <label class="svf">Floor to slab <input type="number" min="2200" max="4000" step="10" value="${S.ceil}" onchange="svSet('ceil',+this.value)"/> mm</label>
    <label class="svf">False ceiling drop <input type="number" min="0" max="800" step="10" value="${S.drop}" onchange="svSet('drop',+this.value)"/> mm</label>
    <label class="svf"><input type="checkbox" ${S.fan?'checked':''} onchange="svSet('fan',this.checked)"/> Ceiling fan</label>
   </div></div>
   <div class="svchk">${checks(r,R).map(([k,t,d])=>`<div class="${k}"><b>${t}</b><span>${d}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>
   <p class="note" style="margin-top:8px">Heights are editable. Reach and worktop figures are rules of thumb for planning, not measurements.</p></div>`;}
